# Release checklist

One living record of what changes, how to test it, which settings it needs, and what still needs a human to confirm. Every pull request adds its entry to the top of **Latest changes**, with its PR link ([AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md), [ADR 0033](adr/0033-pull-requests-merge-to-main.md)). Merging a pull request into `main` publishes it, so an entry there is live once its PR is merged.

## Latest changes

- **Pull requests merge to `main`** ([#3](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site/pull/3), [ADR 0033](adr/0033-pull-requests-merge-to-main.md)): the standing `integration` branch and Release PR are retired. Branch from `main`, open the PR against `main`; merging publishes. Several dependent PRs use a temporary `integration/<feature>` branch. Test: open a PR against `main` and confirm the required checks run. No site or board change.

## 2026-10-18: first public release (SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site#1)

Everything in this section is live. Items under its **Needs your confirmation** headings stay open until answered; answered items move to the matching **Answered** list.

### What changed

- **No more "Archive" photo label** ([contract](contracts/remove-archive-label.md)): the "Archive" label on photos (Resources, Home story kickers) and the "Old photo" tick in the dashboard photo editor are removed, along with the stored `historical`/`year` photo fields. Stored flags are ignored when read and dropped when a page is next saved. Test: open `/resources` and `/` and confirm no item shows "Archive"; open a page's photo in the dashboard and confirm there is no "Old photo" tick; run `npm run check`. No setting changes. Needs the maintainer's confirmation on the PR that removing the dashboard tick is wanted (D-2).
- **Clean public release preparation** ([#118](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site-private-history/issues/118), [contract](contracts/public-launch-preparation.md)): replaces the root Unlicense with MIT for project-authored code while preserving the original grant for prior releases; excludes brand/media and third-party terms; removes obsolete source briefs and concept images from the current tree; keeps the historical tracker private and uses a clean public repository for code. Test: verify package and lockfile license metadata, confirm the six obsolete source files are absent, inspect all current links and onboarding instructions, compare hashes of `public/` assets with the baseline, and run `npm run check`. Staging invite/password-reset rehearsal and clean repository publication remain separate launch gates. No account or production setting is changed by this documentation preparation.
- **Focused QA and coverage** ([#124](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site-private-history/issues/124), [contract](contracts/admin-browser-qa.md)): adds a fresh-local admin browser smoke gate with desktop/light and phone/dark checks, permission refusal, edit/public result, validation, session loss and accessibility. CI retains failure evidence briefly. Unit coverage reports loaded application modules and enforces measured 88/89/84 line/branch/function floors. Test: install Chromium once, run `npm run test:smoke` and `npm run check`; inspect failures and `coverage/`. Sample evidence does not establish hosted Auth/RLS or all permission combinations. The linter excludes ignored draft scratch/private tooling directories, so the normal workspace gate remains usable. No board workflow or production setting changes.
- **Session-loss smoke assertion** ([launch issue #118](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site-private-history/issues/118)): checks that the original event description remains visible and rejected text stays absent with retrying semantic Playwright assertions, instead of comparing layout-dependent whole-article `innerText`. Test: run the focused session-loss spec in both `desktop-light` and `phone-dark`, then `npm run test:smoke`.
- **Delegated engineering** ([ADR 0032](adr/0032-delegated-implementation-and-human-merge.md), [contract](contracts/delegated-engineering.md)): capable planner → Luna/Sonnet implementation → independent capable review → maintainer review/merge. Shared role instructions back native Claude/Codex adapters. The earlier automatic integration-merge permission is superseded. Test: run agent configuration checks; verify native role support/model routing in each installed client before dispatch. No permission bypass, daemon or GitHub-writing hook.

- **Private access and tracking**: both agents use one ignored browser profile for GitHub/Supabase/Vercel sessions and a project-local GitHub CLI wrapper. Password saving is disabled; other sites use isolated contexts. A shared task-boundary skill maintains existing issues, milestones and the organization Project. No automatic GitHub-writing hooks or website changes. Test: open Chrome MCP twice from fresh clients and confirm profile reuse; check `.agents/local/` is ignored and untracked. Initial browser sign-in may be required once in this new profile.

- **Agent parity follow-up** ([ADR 0031](adr/0031-agent-neutral-setup-and-staging-writes.md)): staging MCP now supports authorized reads and writes in both clients. Shared skills live in `.agents/skills`; client paths link to that same directory. Maestro resolves the Git root without a Claude variable. Test: (1) run `npm run agents:sync -- --claude-local`; (2) confirm both clients discover the shared skills and project-local production server; (3) verify staging write capability with a temporary, rolled-back SQL probe. OAuth consent is separate per client after changing the endpoint. No website feature or production data changes.

- **Developer MCP setup** ([#122](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site-private-history/issues/122), [ADR 0030](adr/0030-project-local-mcp-additions.md)): shared servers stay in `.mcp.json`; optional project-local additions and the active generated Codex config stay out of Git. `npm ci` generates the config; OAuth sign-ins persist separately. Test: (1) run `npm run agents:sync` and `npm run agents:check`; (2) confirm `git check-ignore .mcp.local.json .codex/config.toml`; (3) make a harmless MCP request from a fresh Codex client. Settings: optional local additions only; no application environment variables or board changes. Live connection results and unresolved provider status are tracked in #122. No deployment or paid feature is enabled by this tooling change.
- **Tracking and spending policy (2026-10-12):** [ADR 0029](adr/0029-tracked-work-and-zero-spend.md) requires tickets before tasks, scope explanations and progress updates, and keeps infrastructure authorization at $0. The developer guide separates board, owner, developer and AI instructions. [Epic #109](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site-private-history/issues/109) tracks the ecosystem; [#110](https://github.com/SGU-MSA/sgu-msa-site-private-history/issues/110) tracks organization settings. This docs-only change does not alter the application or any account.

- Production now deploys from `main` only; pull requests no longer create Vercel previews ([ADR 0010](adr/0010-production-deploys-from-main-only.md)).
- Visitor statistics (Vercel Analytics and Speed Insights) run only when the owner sets them on ([ADR 0011](adr/0011-production-analytics-explicit-setting.md)); the Privacy page wording says so.
- In-app Help (`/admin/help`) gained tips: overnight events, calendar files, SVG images and no autosave.
- **Settings are checked before every deploy.** If a required Vercel setting is missing or has a wrong value, the deploy stops with a message naming it, and the live site stays on the previous version. Nothing falls back to a guessed value any more (for example the site address or the look). Sample (demo) data can never run on the live site ([ADR 0012](adr/0012-ports-adapters-and-fail-fast-configuration.md)).
- Visitors see no change once the settings below are in place.
- **Board sign-in always shows the sign-in form.** Because the database settings are now required for every deploy, the old "The board portal is awaiting setup" message on `/admin` is gone, and the board guide no longer mentions it.
- The hidden demo switch in the Ramadan page settings (`content/ramadan.yaml`, which was off) is removed: sample data exists only in the local demo ([data-providers contract](contracts/data-providers.md), D-3).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change): pages no longer ask "is this the demo?". They follow site-wide rules (whether search engines may list the site, the demo banner, the board portal) and where the data came from (live or sample), so later dashboard features plug in without page rework (WP-2).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change on the live site): every server-side date rule (which news and notices are visible, which events are upcoming, whether the congregation schedule is current, prayer start times, the prayer-settings save check) now asks one shared server clock instead of each reading the time on its own. On the live site that clock is the real time. In the local demo it is the demo's start time, so the sample events, notices and congregation times always match each other, even across midnight (WP-3).
- **The local demo is now an editable sandbox** (on a developer's computer only, never the live site). In `npm run demo`, **/admin** shows a sign-in form (username **admin**, password **admin**) instead of the "read-only design demo" note. Founders can try every dashboard edit (events, notices, news, newsletters, photos and PDFs, prayer times) on sample data: changes show on the demo's public pages and stay while you click around or refresh, and restarting the demo erases them all. The demo banner now says "Sample edits reset on restart · Payments disabled" instead of "Editing and payments disabled" ([data-providers contract](contracts/data-providers.md), D-6, WP-8).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change on the live site): board saves, deletes, uploads, sign-in and sign-out now go through one small "content editor" and "board sign-in" interface, so the live site keeps using Supabase exactly as before (same messages, same safety rules) and later dashboard features (archive, history, People) plug in without touching database code in the pages (WP-8).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change): the page text that still lives in the `content/*.yaml` files (menus, footer, page headings, About, FAQ, Get Involved, Merch, Resources, Support, Ramadan, form buttons) is now read through one small "editorial source" interface instead of each page opening the files itself. The text and the files are unchanged. This is the step that lets a later dashboard feature (roadmap item PR5) move these pages into the dashboard one page at a time without reworking the pages (WP-4).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change): the **Donate** page and the Ramadan page's **Sponsorship** button now get the giving link through one small "payments" interface instead of reading the `DONATION_URL` setting themselves. The pages, their words and the setting are unchanged. This is the slot where the later Go2Pay feature (roadmap item PR6) plugs in without reworking the pages ([ADR 0005](adr/0005-payments-channel-policy.md), WP-5).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change): the **Find us on campus** box (home page, **/prayer** and **/contact**) now gets the musallah location through one small "places" interface instead of reading the `MUSALLAH_*` settings itself. The box, its words, its **Open Google Maps** and **Open Apple Maps** links and the settings are unchanged. This is the slot where the later single-pin map (roadmap item PR7) plugs in: the board will set the one musallah pin in the dashboard, and that pin will feed both the map and the directions (WP-6). (Replaced by the PR7 entry below: the `MUSALLAH_*` settings are no longer read, and the box now uses the pin stored in the database.)
- Behind the scenes (no visible change): unused code was removed: an old Google Drive shortcut list, a Google Calendar link helper and "integration status" helpers that no page has used since the dashboard was rebuilt, plus an unused `public_url` line in the payments part of `content/integrations.yaml` (the giving link comes only from the `DONATION_URL` setting). The developer and agent docs, and a new "Data sources" picture in the [architecture diagrams](architecture.md), now describe how the site picks its data sources, and how a later feature (Go2Pay, page text in the dashboard, the map pin) adds one. This finishes the data-providers work (WP-7).
- **Outdated files cleaned up** ([cleanup contract](contracts/cleanup-outdated.md)). On the live site only four sentences change, because no route video has played since it was retired: the "Find the Musallah" intro on **/prayer**, the FAQ answer "Where is the Musallah located?", the "Finding the Musallah" card on **/resources** and the "Find the Musallah" card on **/contact** now point to the written directions instead of a video (D-9). Everything else looks the same. Behind the scenes, settings in the `content/*.yaml` files that did nothing were removed, so nobody edits them expecting a change: the board-portal role list (`content/admin.yaml`, which also contradicted the equal-admins decision in [ADR 0007](adr/0007-people-and-permissions.md)), the Drive and Calendar sections of `content/integrations.yaml`, a hidden announcement bar and the "POC mode" switch in `content/site.yaml`, and the old copies of events, prayer times, Jumu'ah times and newsletters that the dashboard already replaces. Also removed: unused code, the 12.6 MB web copy of the route video (D-2), old test reports and screenshots, retired code samples, the Facebook photo archive (D-5), the original route video recording (D-6) and duplicate copies of reference files. Kept: the founder's two design briefs, the three mock-up screenshots and the logo source (D-7), the payments proposal and merch notes that ADR 0005 links (D-8), and the inherited photos with their photo manifest (until the new design). Git history keeps every deleted file.
- **The local sandbox signs in like the live site** (on a developer's computer only). In `npm run demo`, **/admin** shows a **Username** and **Password** form instead of the **Sign in as demo admin** button: type username **admin** and password **admin** (D-3, D-10). The live site's sign-in form is unchanged (email and password; a never-shown "Continue with Google" placeholder is removed from the code) and refuses admin / admin. The **/demo/prayer-settings** preview page is gone (D-1): try the real prayer form in the sandbox instead.
- **New design direction chosen: Quiet Paper** ([ADR 0018](adr/0018-design-direction-quiet-paper.md)). Nothing looks different yet: the decision is recorded now, and the new look arrives in the next design pull requests (roadmap PR2, then PR8).
- Planned, not built yet: a live preview beside each dashboard editor showing how an item will look on the public site before saving (roadmap PR5). A full-page "preview as a visitor" for unsaved drafts is noted for later, only if the board asks for it.
- Home: the opening section now slides in gently without fading, so its text is fully readable from the first moment (fixes a colour-contrast check that could fail during the fade).
- Behind the scenes (no visible change): shared AI-agent setup, decision records (ADRs 0001–0018), updated guides and roadmap.
- **PR3 step 1 of 8: the database rules for People, permissions, the activity log and archiving** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-A). Nothing new appears in the dashboard yet; the screens come in the next PR3 steps. Once the three new database updates are applied:
  - The database now holds a **People** list with three starter sections (Board, Musallah team, Advisors). Each current board login gets a hidden People entry in Board, named after its login email, and the "manage people" permission (D-9). Nobody appears on the public site from this list yet.
  - The database itself enforces the safety rules: nobody can remove their own access or their own "manage people" permission, the last active "manage people" holder keeps it even when two people act at the same moment, and turning someone's access off signs them out on every device (D-15).
  - **Every change is recorded** in an add-only activity log: who, what, when, and before → after for events, notices, news, newsletters and prayer settings (prayer changes are highlighted). People and access entries record which fields changed but not names, emails or consent details (D-5). Nobody, the site owner included, can edit or delete an entry.
  - **Archive instead of delete:** archived items are hidden from the public site together with their photos and files. Permanent delete works only on archived items and only for a "manage people" holder (D-6). Until the **Archive** button arrives (PR3 step 6), the dashboard's **Delete permanently** button is refused for current items with the usual error message.
  - The invite and password-reset email texts are written (D-1), but no email is sent yet: the owner connects the MSA Gmail in a later PR3 step.
  - For developers: `npm run test:db` checks all of these rules on a throwaway database, locally and on every pull request.
- **PR3 step 2 of 8: the plumbing behind the People, access and Recent changes screens** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-B). Nothing new appears on the live site or in the dashboard yet; the screens come in the next PR3 steps. Behind the scenes:
  - The site can now read and change People, team sections and dashboard access, read the activity log ("Recent changes"), archive, restore and permanently delete items, and read the public team list, always through the same small interfaces as before, so the database rules from step 1 decide everything.
  - **One new required setting, `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`** (see Settings below). It is used for one thing only: sending the invite email to a new board member. The live site refuses to deploy without it, and the key never reaches visitors' browsers. Passwords are never stored by the site: Supabase keeps only a one-way scrambled copy.
  - The local sandbox (`npm run demo`, a developer's computer only) now also holds sample People: the sandbox admin's own entry "Omar Haddad" (hidden from the public list), "Maryam Siddiqui" (listed, with consent) and "Dr. Kareem Mansour" (not listed), the three starter sections, and a sandbox activity log. The sandbox never sends an email. Restarting the demo resets them. (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
- **PR2: the Quiet Paper look arrives, in light and dark** ([design-foundation contract](contracts/design-foundation.md), [ADR 0018](adr/0018-design-direction-quiet-paper.md)). Per D-5 this Release PR waits for PR8, so the whole new look goes live in one step.
  - Every public page uses the new Quiet Paper colours (light: Green Ink, whiter paper with deep green ink, PR8 D-5) and the new fonts (Instrument Serif headings, Instrument Sans text, Amiri Quran for Arabic). Page layouts stay as they are until PR8; only colours, fonts, buttons, the header and the footer change.
  - **Dark mode (Dusk).** A round sun/moon button in the header switches light and dark. Until a visitor taps it, the site follows their phone or computer setting; after a tap the choice is remembered on that device. The page never flashes the wrong colours on load. Nothing is stored about the visitor except that one choice in their own browser.
  - The **header and footer** are new: the official seal (in the theme's ink colours) with "SGU MSA", the page links, the theme switch and **Donate**. The phone menu works as before.
  - **Home** now shows, beside the prayer times, the **dhikr of the hour** (a short Arabic remembrance that follows the time of day: morning, just after a prayer, Friday, evening or night) and **today's Hijri date** (English first, Arabic beneath, with a small moon; it changes at midnight with the normal date, PR8 D-11). A thin **tasbih thread** of 99 beads runs down the left margin and fills as you scroll. Each Arabic line has a small **?** that shows its English meaning and reference (surah and verse; PR8 D-6).
  - The Home headline now comes from the Home text file: "A little closer. To faith. To each other." with its two-sentence welcome (D-6).
  - The Arabic reminders live in one file, `content/reminders.yaml` (generated from the named sources, never retyped); the board can edit them from the dashboard after PR5.
  - The six old review looks, the `SITE_DESIGN` setting and `npm run review` are gone. Developers get **Storybook** (`npm run storybook`) showing every shared piece in light and dark; CI now builds it on every pull request (D-1, D-2).
- **PR3 step 3 of 8: the People screens** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-C). The dashboard has a new **People** card. Any board member can now:
  - see everyone on the team, grouped by section (Board, Musallah team, Advisors, or any section the board adds), with small labels showing whether each person is on the public page, whether they have a dashboard login ("No login", "Invited", "Login active", "Login deactivated") and their permissions ("Manage people", "Manage money");
  - add or edit a person: name, role title, section, order number (lower numbers come first), the required question **Show on public page?** (Yes or No, nothing chosen in advance), and an optional photo;
  - answering **Yes** asks for the person's listing consent: the date and how it was given (by email, by message, in person or on paper). A photo asks for its own, separate photo consent. Without them the save is refused with a message naming the missing consent, and everything typed stays in the form (a chosen photo must be chosen again, because browsers cannot keep it); (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - on **Edit sections**: rename a section, change its order number, add a new section, and archive a section that has nobody in it. (Since review 2 PR-B, sections are **groups**: **Edit groups**, **Archived groups**.)
  - The public team list on **/about** (which uses the "Show on public page?" answer), inviting logins, permissions, archiving people and Recent changes arrive in the next PR3 steps. Until then **/about** looks the same as before.
- **PR8 step 1: Home and Prayer get their new Quiet Paper layouts** (the layouts the board approved in the D1 pictures; PR8 contract draft, D-0). Same information as before, laid out on paper sheets with large serif headings, thin lines and more space. Nothing changes in the dashboard or in where the times, events or notices come from.
  - **Home:** the headline "A little closer. To faith. To each other." with the welcome text and two links (**What’s happening**, **Find the Musallah**, from the Home text file). Beside it: the dhikr of the hour, the Hijri date, the next prayer in large type circled with a hand-drawn ink ring, today's six start times with each iqamah time under it, today's date with the Jumu’ah times, and **View Prayer & Jumu’ah**. Below, in this order: the featured notice (a thin strip), the verse band (13:28, with its **?**), **Upcoming at MSA** with 5:2 beside it, **Our community, built by students** (a photo with the six ways to help and 49:10), Community updates (unchanged until the Community page's own step), **Your first visit** with the **Find us on campus** box, **More than a place to pray**, the common questions, and an inked **Support our community** band with 3:92 and a **Donate** button that opens **/donate**.
  - **Prayer:** "Prayer & Musallah" heading; **Today · (date)** with the Hijri date under it and one table of when each prayer **begins** and its **iqamah**, the next prayer circled in ink and marked "Next"; 4:103 under the table; the Jumu’ah card in ink beside it; **Find the Musallah** with the numbered written directions, the photo, the **Find us on campus** box and the three route photos; **Musallah information**; and the Musallah helpers band with its form button.
  - Removed from Home on purpose (approved prototype): the old hero photo strip and "New here? You’re welcome here." line, and the separate iqamah and Jumu’ah boxes (their times now sit in the prayer block). The "in N min" countdown is gone: the ringed next prayer updates when the page refreshes itself (every minute while open).
  - The **Find us on campus** box has the new look on **/contact** too; it works exactly as before.
- **PR8 board feedback, shared parts** (PR8 contract draft, D-5 and D-6). Every page gets these:
  - **The light theme is Green Ink** (whiter paper, deep green ink), the board's pick. The warm Paper palette is removed, and so is the hidden switch that compared the two. Dark mode (Dusk) is unchanged. Every light text colour still passes the WCAG AA contrast check. The browser bar colour and the browser-tab icon use Green Ink too.
  - **Short Arabic stays on one line.** A short verse or dhikr (the margin verses, the dhikr of the hour, the footer verse) no longer breaks onto a second line: on a small phone it gets a little smaller instead. (Review fixes PR-3 extended this to every verse and dhikr: see its entry.)
  - **The footer verse (20:114) is Arabic with a "?"**, like every other reminder, instead of the English sentence and reference. Its text now comes from the one reminders file (`content/reminders.yaml`); the separate copy in `content/site.yaml` is removed.
  - **The Instagram and Facebook icons are the real logos** (one colour, matching the other footer icons), instead of a camera and an @ sign.
  - **The "?" bubble shows only the meaning and the reference** (surah and verse). The source line is no longer shown to visitors; the sources stay in `content/reminders.yaml` for review.
- **PR8 Events: the "Almanac" layout** (the option you picked, PR8 contract draft D-1) on **/calendar** and each event's page. Events still come from the dashboard; the filters and **Add to Calendar** work as before.
  - **/calendar:** a small month is always open on its own paper sheet on the left (on a phone: at the top). Days with an event carry a small dot in the event's category colour and jump to that event; the next upcoming event's day is circled in ink. Under it: **Upcoming events** / **Past events**, the category buttons (each with its colour dot), the **Term** list, the number of events and 5:2 with its **?**. On the right, the events are small paper cards grouped by month: the day and weekday, the title (opens the event page), time and place, category · organization · term (and "Pending verification" when not confirmed) and **Add to Calendar**. The next upcoming event's card has an ink edge.
  - **Removed on purpose (your choice with D-1):** the separate **Month** view and the **Agenda / Month** switch. The small month replaces them. The photo under the Events heading is also gone (the picked picture has none).
  - **Each event's page:** **All events**, then the tags, the large title, the date and time, **Add to Calendar** (and **Register** when there is a link), the description, the photo and the SGU or "not yet confirmed" note. Beside it (on a phone: below it) the small month with the event's day circled, then Location, Source and Status, then 5:2.
- **PR8: the Donate page becomes a letter** (option C "Letter", the layout the board picked; PR8 contract draft, D-2). **/donate** is now one centred paper sheet, like a letter from the MSA: the seal at the top, "Giving at SGU MSA", the heading "Support _our community_", the thank-you line, a thin line with a small ink mark, then the giving status with its one button, and the Arabic verse 3:92 (with its **?**) closing the letter like a signature. The words, the two states and where the giving link comes from are unchanged:
  - While giving is not set up (today): "Giving details are being finalized", the note that no payments are collected on this website, and **Contact the MSA**.
  - Once the giving link is set: "Make a donation", the note about continuing to the approved payment provider, and **Continue to donate**, circled with the hand-drawn ink ring.
- **PR8: Resources gets its new layout, "The Index"** (the option the user picked from pictures; PR8 contract draft, D-4). Same resources, words and links as before, laid out like a library catalogue:
  - The heading "Resources" with "Publication library" in italics under it, the short intro, and the 20:114 verse with its **?** beside it (on a phone: under it).
  - On a computer, the search box and the categories run down a quiet column on the left (it stays in view while you scroll); the chosen category is circled in ink and each one shows how many resources it has. On a phone they sit above the list and the categories scroll sideways.
  - Each resource is a numbered, ruled line: its title and description, its category and "updated" note (or "SGU MSA resource"), the **Archive** tag when its picture is historical, and an **Open** link (or its "coming soon" label when it has no link yet). The resource pictures are no longer shown on this page; they stay in `content/resources.yaml`.
  - **Back issues**: the newsletter archive is a ledger by period (the period in large type, then the label and edition, the summary and **Open PDF** or "Link coming soon"). "Newsletter editions will be listed here." and "Newsletters are temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly." still show when they apply.
  - The home page's newsletter link (**/resources?category=newsletter**) still opens the page with **Newsletter** chosen.
- **PR8: Community gets its new layout, "Front page"** (the option the user picked from pictures; PR8 contract draft, D-4). Same updates, words and order as before, laid out like a newspaper front page:
  - The heading "Community" with "updates" in italics under it, the short intro, and the 49:10 verse with its **?** beside it (on a phone: under it).
  - The top update (a featured one first, otherwise the newest) is the lead story on a lifted paper sheet: its photo, its kind ("Announcement" or "Community news") circled in ink, the date, the title and the summary. Every other update follows as a ruled line: date and kind, title, summary and a small photo. The whole story or line opens the update.
  - Dates now read "3 October 2026" instead of "2026-10-03".
  - Each update's own page reads like an article: an **All community updates** link back, the kind and date, the title, the summary in italics, the photo (only when one was uploaded, as before) on a paper sheet, then the text with 49:10 in the margin and **Learn more** when the update has a link.
  - "Some updates are temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly." (with whatever did load), "There are no current updates. New community news and notices will appear here." and the "page not found" page for a missing update still show when they apply. The Community updates block on the home page is unchanged.
- **PR3 fixes before the next steps** (the planner's amendments D-19 to D-24 in the [PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), FIX). The three PR3 database updates were changed in place, because nobody has applied them yet (see Settings below).
  - **Archived sections can be brought back.** **People → Edit sections** now lists **Archived sections** at the bottom, each with a **Restore** button. A restored section comes back with its name and order number. If a current section already has that name, Restore is refused with "A current section already has this name. Rename that section first, then restore this one." (D-22) (Since review 2 PR-B, sections are **groups**: **Edit groups**, **Archived groups**.)
  - **Archived entries cannot be changed until they are restored.** The database refuses any change to an archived person or section except Restore, for everyone, the site owner included (D-20).
  - **An archived person's login stays off.** Turning on the login of someone in Archived is refused with "Restore this person first." (D-19). The login controls themselves arrive in PR3 step 4.
  - **Deleting a person also deletes their login.** When permanent delete arrives (PR3 step 6), permanently deleting a person also deletes their login account (their email) in Supabase, so nothing is left behind (D-21).
  - **Unused photos are cleaned up for everyone.** When any board member replaces a person's photo, or a save with a new photo is refused, the unused file is deleted. Before, only "manage people" holders could delete it, so files were left behind (D-23).
  - Behind the scenes (no visible change): the rule "is this item visible to visitors now?" is written once in the database instead of eight times (D-24).
- **PR3 step 4 of 8: dashboard logins and passwords** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-D). Board members can now join, leave and reset their password without the site owner (once the email settings below are done):
  - **Each person's page in People has a "Dashboard login" box.** A board member with the "manage people" permission can type an email and click **Send invite**, choosing the two permissions ("Manage people", "Manage money"; nothing is ticked in advance). The person gets the invite email (D-1) with a link to choose their own password; the link works once and expires in 24 hours. Until they do, the person shows as **Invited**, with **Send the invite again**. Anyone else sees the login state and "Only a board member who can manage people can invite, change permissions or deactivate logins."
  - **Permissions** can be changed with **Save permissions**. **Deactivate login** signs the person out on every device at once and closes the dashboard to them; **Reactivate login** opens it again with their existing password (no new invite). Nobody can remove their own access or their own "manage people" permission (those controls are greyed out with the reason, and the database refuses them too), the last active "manage people" holder keeps it, and an archived person's login cannot be turned on ("Restore this person first.", D-19).
  - **Sign-in page**: "Need access? Ask a board member who can manage people." and a **Forgot your password?** link. The forgot-password page always answers "If this email belongs to a board account, a reset link is on its way. Check your inbox and spam folder.", so it never reveals who has an account.
  - **Choosing a password** (after an invite or reset link): at least 12 characters, including a lower-case letter, a capital letter, a number and a symbol (D-16), typed twice. Saving it signs the person out on their other devices (D-15) and opens the dashboard with "Your password is saved." Passwords are never stored by the website: Supabase keeps only a one-way (bcrypt) scrambled form that nobody at the MSA can read.
  - A used, expired or broken email link goes back to sign-in with "This link has expired or was already used. Ask for a new invite or use Forgot password."
  - There is no "sign out on all devices" button and no two-step sign-in (D-15): deactivating someone, or a password reset, ends their other sessions instead.
  - In the local sandbox no email is ever sent; since the review fixes (PR-1) invites and resets answer exactly like the live site.
- **PR3 step 5 of 8: Recent changes** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-E). Every board member can now see who changed what and when:
  - The dashboard has a new **Recent changes** card. It opens a list of every change to events, announcements, community news, newsletters, prayer settings, People, team sections and logins, newest first, 25 at a time, with **Older changes** at the bottom (and **Newest changes** to go back).
  - Each change shows what happened and to which item (for example "Edited event: Iftar night"), when (Grenada time), who did it (their name from People; "Site owner" for a change made directly in Supabase; "Former member" when that person's People entry was permanently deleted), and for each field the old value → the new value.
  - **Everyone sees everything** (D-4). For People and logins the list says only _which_ details changed (name, consent dates and methods, photo); the old and new values are never stored or shown, and no email address appears (D-4, D-5). (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - **Prayer changes stand out**: they have a gold edge and a "Prayer change" label in the list, and for 7 days after a prayer-settings change the dashboard shows "Prayer settings changed recently" with who changed it and when, and a link to Recent changes.
  - Nobody can edit or delete entries (the database refuses it, WP-A). Its help card is in **Help** since PR3 step 8.
- **PR3 fixes, part 2** (the planner's amendments D-25 to D-28 in the [PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), FIX2). Two of the PR3 database updates were changed in place again, because nobody has applied them yet (see Settings below).
  - **Invite and reset links are no longer used up by email scanners.** Some email systems (SGU uses Outlook) open every link in an email to check it before the person does, which used up the one-time link. Now the link opens a page "Continue to choose your password" with a **Continue** button; the link is used only when the person clicks **Continue**, then they choose their password as before (D-25).
  - **A photo can be removed from an archived person.** When someone withdraws their photo consent, their photo and photo consent can be cleared even while they are in Archived, without restoring them first. Nothing else about an archived person can be changed (D-26). The **Remove photo (consent withdrawn)** button is on the person's page since PR3 step 6. (Since review 2 PR-B the button is **Remove photo** and there is no photo consent record.)
  - **Recent changes is more accurate when a person is added.** A new person's empty details (for example no photo or no consent) are no longer listed as "changed"; only the details actually filled in are (D-27). Values are still never stored.
  - **Re-saving a text with line breaks logs nothing.** Saving an event, notice, news post or newsletter whose multi-line text you did not change no longer shows a "changed (spaces or line breaks only)" entry in Recent changes (D-28).
- **PR3 step 6 of 8: Archive instead of delete, Restore, Duplicate and prayer history** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-F). Removing something can now be undone:
  - **Archive replaces "Delete permanently"** on events, announcements, community news and newsletters. **Archive this item → Archive** takes the item and its image or PDF off the website at once. Each list (and **People**) now has two tabs, **Current** and **Archived**.
  - **Archived items are read-only.** Opening one shows what it held, a **Restore** button and **Delete permanently**. **Restore** (any board member) brings an item back as an **unpublished draft**, so it is checked before it goes public again (D-13).
  - **Delete permanently** is only in Archived, only for board members who can manage people, and only after typing **DELETE** (D-6). Its image or PDF is deleted too, unless another item still uses it. Recent changes keeps a record that it happened.
  - **Duplicate** on events and announcements: **Duplicate as a new draft** makes an unpublished copy with "(copy)" after the title and the same date, and opens it for editing (D-13).
  - **People: Archive this person** takes them off the public team list and turns their login off in the same step: they are signed out on every device (D-12). Nobody can archive themselves, or the last person who can manage people. An archived person's login cannot be turned back on: **Reactivate login** is greyed out with "Restore this person first." (D-19). **Restore** brings the entry back; their login stays off until a manager reactivates it.
  - **Deleting a person permanently also deletes their login account** (their email in Supabase), so nothing is left behind (D-21). If that part fails, the person is still deleted and the page asks the site owner to delete the account in Supabase → **Authentication** → **Users**.
  - **Remove photo (consent withdrawn)** on a person's page (current or archived) deletes their photo and the photo consent record, and nothing else (D-26, ADR 0014). (Since review 2 PR-B the button is **Remove photo** and there is no photo consent record.)
  - **Prayer history**: the **Prayer & iqamah** page now ends with **History**, the last 20 changes to the prayer settings (when, who, what changed). **Restore the version before this change** puts the settings back as they were before that change. It is checked exactly like a normal save (a version that no longer gives correct prayer times is refused) and shows in Recent changes like any change.
  - If a file cannot be deleted after a permanent delete or a photo removal, the page says "A file is left over" with a **Remove** button to try again; the file is never public in the meantime (D-23).
  - Help cards for these screens are in **Help** since PR3 step 8.
- **PR3 step 7 of 8: the public team list on /about comes from People** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-G). The board now decides who appears on the About page from the dashboard, without a developer:
  - The **Leadership** block on **/about** now lists the people from **People** whose answer to **Show on public page?** is **Yes** and who have a recorded listing consent: one small heading per team section (Board, Musallah team, Advisors, or any section the board adds), in the sections' order, and under each the role title and name of each person, in their order number (lower first) (D-10, D-11). (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - A person's photo shows only when they also have a recorded photo consent. No photo means no picture at all (no grey box). The photo's description for screen readers is the person's name. (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - Nobody else ever appears: people answered **No**, people without consent, archived people and people in archived sections are left out. Changing someone to **No**, archiving them or clicking **Remove photo (consent withdrawn)** takes them (or their photo) off the page at once. (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - While nobody is listed yet, or if the list cannot be loaded, the block shows "Board roster to be confirmed." instead.
  - The old list of board roles with "To be confirmed" (President, Vice President, Brothers’ VP, Sisters’ VP, Secretary, Treasurer) and the separate **Faculty Advisor** box are gone, together with the roster in the page text file `content/about.yaml`. Only the block's heading ("Board", now "Our team", see PR3 fixes, part 3), its note ("The MSA board is elected by students each year.") and the "to be confirmed" text stay in that file until page text moves to the dashboard (roadmap PR5). An advisor is now added in People, for example in the **Advisors** section.
  - **/privacy** has a new paragraph, "Team list and board records": what the People list holds (name, role, section, consent records, an optional photo and the login email of anyone with dashboard access), that it is kept in Supabase, that a person is listed only with consent and their photo only with a separate photo consent, and that either consent can be withdrawn. The "Board access" paragraph now says that board members who can manage people, and the site owner, manage logins, and that Supabase stores passwords only as a one-way bcrypt hash that nobody at the MSA can see. (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - This needs the three PR3 database updates (see Settings below); until they are applied, **/about** shows "Board roster to be confirmed.".
- **PR3 fixes, part 3** (your answers of 2026-10-03 and the planner's amendments D-29 to D-31 in the [PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), FIX3). One of the PR3 database updates was changed in place again, because nobody has applied it yet (see Settings below).
  - **The team block on /about is now headed "Our team"** instead of "Board", so the heading no longer repeats the name of the first section under it. The small "Leadership" label above it and the note under it stay as they were.
  - **Prayer history Restore stays as built**: **Restore the version before this change** puts the prayer settings back as they were _before_ the change you pick (your answer; nothing changed on screen).
  - **The Faculty Advisor sentence stays removed** until the founder decides (see "You or the founder must decide" below).
  - **Recent changes is more accurate when a person is permanently deleted.** The entry now lists only the personal details that person actually had (for example their name), not every empty one such as a photo or consent they never had (D-29). Values are still never stored.
  - **Re-saving an unchanged Schedule notice logs nothing.** A multi-line **Schedule notice** on the **Prayer & iqamah** page now saves plain line breaks, like events and notices already do, so saving the page without changing the notice adds no "changed (spaces or line breaks only)" entry to Recent changes (D-30). A notice last saved before this change logs one such entry on its next save, once.
- **PR8: Get Involved gets its new Quiet Paper layout, "Chooser"** (the option the board picked, PR8 D-4). Same pathways, words and form buttons as before, laid out differently:
  - At the top: "Contribute", the big **Get Involved** heading, "Every part of the MSA is run by students. These are the ways in." with the Arabic verse 5:2 and its **?** beside it, then the intro sentence.
  - Below, a list of the eight pathways (number, title and one line each). The chosen pathway opens on a paper sheet beside the list (on a phone: right under its own line), and its title in the list is circled in ink. The first pathway is open when you arrive. The sheet shows everything the old page showed for that pathway: its icon, title, summary, photo (Volunteer only), text, the four Student Khutbah steps, "The form asks about" list, the note, and the same form button or "Coming Soon" text as before.
  - The Home page's links to a pathway (for example **Give a Student Khutbah**) open the page with that pathway already open and in view. Without JavaScript every line is a link that shows its pathway, so every pathway can still be reached.

- **PR3 step 8 of 8: help and setup guides** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), WP-H). This finishes PR3: every new dashboard screen now has a help card, and the site owner can do every one-time setting from the owner guide alone.
  - **In-app Help (`/admin/help`)** starts with a **Topics** list that jumps to each card. New cards, each with short numbered steps and, where useful, a link that opens the screen: **People** (add, edit, order, sections), **Team list and consent** (who appears on the About page, listing and photo consent, withdrawn consent), **Inviting and access** (invite, send again, the two permissions, deactivate and reactivate), **Your password and Forgot password** (click **Continue** on the email link; the password rule "At least 12 characters, including a lower-case letter, a capital letter, a number and a symbol."; Forgot your password?), **Recent changes**, **Archive, restore and duplicate**, **Delete permanently** (privacy cases only, type DELETE) and **Prayer history**. (Since review 2 PR-B the card is **Team list**, with no consent.)
  - Rewritten cards: **Correct or remove an item** (Archive instead of delete), **When something goes wrong** (use **Forgot your password?**; ask a board member who can manage people instead of the site owner) and **End of term** (new members are invited from People, leaving members are deactivated or archived, at least two people keep "manage people", the team list and consent are updated). The other cards are unchanged.
  - Help was text with numbered steps; PR4 steps 6 and 7 added the "Show me how" tours and a picture for each step ([ADR 0008](adr/0008-layered-admin-onboarding.md)).
  - **Owner guide** ([owner-setup.md](guides/owner-setup.md)): §10 is now "Board logins: email, password rules and the first admin", with the exact clicks to connect the MSA Gmail (2-Step Verification, the app password "SGU MSA website", Supabase SMTP Settings, a test invite; Gmail sends about 500 emails a day), the email texts, link address, 24-hour links and password rule, and the `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` key "website-invites". Until Gmail works, the owner adds an admin with one line, `select public.owner_add_admin('email', 'Full name', true);` (also how the first admin of a new database is made). The old SQL that added and removed admins by hand is gone: board members who can manage people do that in People. §8 lists the three PR3 database updates (staging first). §9 no longer says "never use `sb_secret_`" while the settings table asks for one: a secret key goes only in `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`. Turnover (§13), the setup checklist (§14), backups (§15), troubleshooting (§16, with rows for invite emails and email links) and final acceptance (§17) now match People.
  - **Board guide**: signing in by invite and **Forgot your password?**; the "What you can do" list adds People, logins, Recent changes and Archive; the "cannot do yet" table no longer lists access and password resets, and "restore something deleted" now means only _permanently_ deleted items (archived ones you restore yourself).
  - Developer guide, architecture diagrams (new section 5: invite, email link and password; the ports picture shows PeopleEditor, ActivityLog and AccountInviter; a punctuation error that stopped diagram 4 from being drawn is fixed) and the roadmap (PR3 **Done**; PR5 no longer lists an "Our team" page or Duplicate, which PR3 delivered) are updated.

- **PR8 board answers** ([PR8 contract](contracts/restyle-public.md) D-10):
  - **The footer lists Community and FAQ** too, under **Quick links**.
  - **Resources has its own verse:** 96:1, "Recite in the name of your Lord who created." (the first words revealed). 20:114 stays in the footer, so a page no longer shows the same verse twice.
  - **The Support page is switched off.** It described a proposed peer-support service that SGU has not approved, and nothing linked to it. **/support** now shows "page not found". To bring it back, set `enabled: true` in `content/support.yaml`.
- **PR8: every time of day on the public site reads like "1:30 PM"** (12-hour clock with AM or PM, never "13:30"; PR8 contract D-8). This covers the prayer table on **/prayer** (Begins and Iqamah), the Jumu'ah card (Khutbah and Iqamah), the Home prayer block (the next prayer, each prayer's time and "Iqamah …", and the Jumu'ah line), the **Tonight** box on **/ramadan** (Maghrib and Isha), and event times on **/calendar**, each event's page and the Home "Upcoming" list. Nothing changes in the dashboard: it still takes times as 24-hour (for example 13:30), and the saved times stay as they are. Text typed into a content file as words (for example the Ramadan iftar "6:40 PM") shows exactly as written.
- **PR8: About gets its new layout, "Chapters"** (the option the user picked from pictures; [PR8 contract](contracts/restyle-public.md) D-7). Same words, team list and order as before, laid out like a book with numbered chapters:
  - At the top: "Who we are", the big **About SGU MSA** heading and "Your Muslim home away from home." in grey italics.
  - Beside the page (on a phone: above it, in two columns) a numbered index: **01 Our Mission**, **02 What We Do**, **03 Our team**, **04 MSA at SGU**, **05 Contact**. Each line jumps to its chapter. Each chapter starts with a line, its number and its heading.
  - **Our Mission**: the first paragraph is set large, the second as normal text. **What We Do**: the six activities in two or three columns, each with its icon, title and line.
  - **Our team** (still from **People** in the dashboard, with the same consent rules): the small **Leadership** label and the note stay; each section (for example Board, Musallah team) has its name and then one paper card ("seat") per listed person, with the consented photo, the role title in small capitals and the name. With nobody listed (or if the list cannot be loaded), "Board roster to be confirmed." shows on one card. Under the team: the Arabic verse 49:10 with its **?**. (Replaced by review 2 PR-B: no consent records any more; see that entry below.)
  - **MSA at SGU**: the two paragraphs and the campus photo, wide, on two paper sheets.
  - **Contact** is a dark ink band across the page with its text, note and the email as a light button.
- **PR8: Contact gets its new layout, "Calling card"** (the option the user picked, PR8 contract D-10). Same words as before, laid out on paper:
  - At the top: "Contact & visit", the large heading "You’re welcome here" and the welcome line in grey italics under it. Beside it (on a phone: below it) a paper card, "Get _in touch_", with the MSA email in large type circled by the hand-drawn ink ring, then **MSA on Instagram** and **MSA on Facebook** as lines with the real logos (the same names as the footer icons). The "Email the MSA" line is not repeated, because the email is already shown above it. Until an email is published, the card says "Contact details will be published here."
  - Below, after a thin line: "Find _the Musallah_" with its note and a **View directions** link (an underlined link now, before an outlined button) to the written directions on **/prayer**, and beside it the **Find us on campus** box, which works exactly as before. No verse on this page.

- **PR4 step 1 of 8: the database can now store the Hijri date adjustment and one Friday's Jumu'ah change** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-1). Once the database update below is applied:
  - The prayer settings hold a new **Hijri date adjustment**: one day earlier, as calculated, or one day later. It starts at "as calculated", which is what the site shows today.
  - A change for one date can now also say that **that Friday's Jumu'ah is cancelled**, or **moved to a different khutbah time and iqamah time**. The database refuses such a change on a day that is not a Friday or a time that is not a real clock time. (Review fixes PR-3 removed the room: the musallah is one room.)
  - Nothing is removed: the old calculation settings stay stored (unused since PR4, removed in PR7), and the website that is live today keeps working with the updated database, so the update can be applied before the Release PR.
  - Every change to these settings is recorded in **Recent changes**, like the other prayer settings.
- **PR8: the FAQ page becomes "Folded notes"** (the option the user picked from pictures; [PR8 contract](contracts/restyle-public.md) D-7). Same questions, answers and words as before, laid out differently:
  - On a computer the heading ("You asked, we answered", **Common** _Questions_ and the short intro) stays on the left as you scroll, and the questions sit on a paper sheet on the right (on a phone: the heading first, the sheet below it).
  - Each question is numbered (01, 02, …) with a round **+** button. Click a question: its answer unfolds under it, its number is circled in ink and the **+** becomes **−**. Click again: it folds away, and the tasbih thread in the margin shrinks back with the page (D-9).
  - The tasbih thread now runs down this page too. There is no Arabic verse on this page (as agreed). The Home page's common questions look exactly as before.
- **PR8: Merch gets its new layout, "Now Wearing"** (the option the user picked from pictures; [PR8 contract](contracts/restyle-public.md) D-7). Same collections, photos, words and labels as before, laid out differently, with no verse on this page:
  - At the top: "Three collections", the big **Merch** heading, the line "An archive of what the MSA has made, and what students are wearing now." in grey italics, and the shirt note "All MSA shirts are long-sleeve."
  - The current collection (**Shukr**) comes first: its lead photo on two layered paper sheets, with the code, name, Arabic name (level, on one line), quote, description, and Garment / Colourway / Released as a dotted list. Its status "Ordering details to be confirmed" is circled in ink; when the board adds an order link it becomes the **Order** button inside the ring. Its other photos sit in a row underneath (on a phone the name and status come before the photo).
  - Below, under a new heading **The archive** (or **More collections** if a collection that is not archived is in the list), the Green and Maroon collections side by side (on a phone one under the other), oldest first, each with its photo, small photos, Arabic name, quote, description, details and "Archive collection — no longer available". Archive photos keep their **Archive** tag.
  - The tasbih thread runs down the margin as on the other restyled pages. The order of collections still comes only from `release_order` in `content/merch.yaml`.
- **PR8: Ramadan gets its new layout, "Tonight first"** (the option the user picked from pictures; PR8 contract D-7). Same words, buttons and states as before, laid out so the page opens on tonight:
  - At the top: "Ramadan 1448 AH", the big heading **Ramadan** with _at SGU_ in grey italics under it, the welcome line, the **Ramadan Calendar** button and the **Donate / Sponsor** button (or "Donor Form Coming Soon" while that form is not set up), and the Arabic verse 2:183 with its **?**. Out of season the **Ramadan Calendar** button is circled in ink.
  - Beside it (on a phone: under it), **Tonight** on a paper sheet, with today's Hijri date at the top. Out of season it says "Ramadan schedule will be published here."; in Ramadan it lists Maghrib, Iftar, Isha and Tarawih in large type (12-hour clock), with Iftar circled in ink.
  - **During the month** is a numbered list (01, 02, …) of the programmes, each with its detail or "Schedule coming soon.", then the note.
  - **Support Ramadan at SGU** is now one ink band (deep green in light mode, light in dark mode, like the Donate band on the home page): the text, the list of areas, the payments note and the same **Donate / Sponsor** button (or "Donor Form Coming Soon"), then **Your support helps provide** with its five icons and the allocation note. Payments still go only through the approved giving link (Go2Pay) when the owner sets it.
  - **Volunteer during Ramadan** sits beside the lantern photo (the photo moved here from the top of the page), with the same volunteer form button (or "Volunteer Form Coming Soon").
  - **From previous Ramadans**: the four archive pictures, each with its **Archive** tag and description, staggered on a computer.
  - The small grey labels "Contribute", "Transparency" and "Archive" above those blocks are gone (the picked picture has none).

- **PR8: Privacy and the system pages get the "Ledger" layout** (the option you picked, PR8 contract D-7). Same words as before; no verse on these pages.
  - **/privacy:** the big heading **Privacy** with the page's one-line summary in grey italics under it ("How this website handles public visits, the team list and board sign-in."). Beside it (on a phone: under it), three short promises with a tick, taken word for word from the notice: "You do not need an account to browse.", "This site does not collect payment information." and "No advertising or custom profiling is added." Then each part of the notice as a ruled row, title in italics on the left and the text on the right (on a phone: title above text): Public visits, Optional map, Board access, Team list and board records, Basic measurement. **Questions** closes the page in a shaded band, with **Contact & Visit** circled in ink and the "should review this notice … before launch" sentence in italics under it.
  - **Page not found:** "404", the large heading **Page not found** and the sentence about the links on the left; on the right (on a phone: under it) the links **Home** (circled in ink), **Prayer & Musallah** and **Calendar** as large ruled rows with arrows.
  - **When something cannot load** (the error page): "Something could not be loaded", the same sentence as before and a **Try again** button with a small circular arrow, with the seal under it on a computer.
  - **While a page loads:** "SGU MSA · Loading the latest information…" above a pale outline of a page (heading and ruled rows) that gently pulses; it stays still when the device asks for less motion.
- **PR4 step 2 of 8: prayer start times use one fixed calculation, and the site shows the Hijri adjustment and a one-Friday Jumu'ah change** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-2). The board sets the Hijri adjustment and a Jumu'ah change on the new Prayer & iqamah screen (step 4).
  - **Prayer start times always use Muslim World League with Standard Asr** ([ADR 0017](adr/0017-fixed-prayer-calculation-mwl-standard-asr.md)) and the stored Musallah location. The old choices (method, Asr convention, "Leadership has confirmed calculation settings" and minute corrections to start times) are gone from the Prayer & iqamah screen and no longer change anything. The latitude and longitude boxes stay (as "Musallah location for prayer times") until the map pin in PR7.
  - **/prayer** under the table now says "Begins: calculated for the Musallah (Muslim World League · Standard Asr · America/Grenada)." The home page's small line under the Jumu'ah times says "Muslim World League · Standard Asr". Without a stored location, both pages say "Prayer start times will appear once the Musallah location is set." (before: "awaiting confirmation").
  - **Hijri date**: Home, **/prayer** and the **Tonight** sheet on **/ramadan** move the Hijri date by the stored adjustment (one day earlier, as calculated, or one day later; it starts at "as calculated").
  - **One Friday's Jumu'ah change** shows from the Saturday before until that Friday ends. Cancelled: the **/prayer** Jumu'ah card says "No Jumu’ah this Friday, 10 October." with the reason under it, and the home page's Jumu'ah line says the same. Different time: the card shows "Changed this Friday, 17 October" above the new khutbah and iqamah, then the reason and "Usually: Khutbah 12:45 PM · Iqamah 1:00 PM"; the home line says "Changed this Friday, 17 October, khutbah …, iqamah …". The next week shows the regular Jumu'ah again.
  - **A change for one date that sets an iqamah time** now shows its reason to visitors on **/prayer**, under the table: "Iqamah: Changed today: Mid-term break. …" (D-5).
  - The Prayer & iqamah screen refuses a prayer that has both a fixed iqamah time and "minutes after the start": it says, for example, "Fajr iqamah: pick Fixed time or After adhan, not both." (wording since 2026-10-05). Before, the minutes silently won.
  - Restoring an older prayer version from **History** still works; a version saved before this step keeps today's Hijri adjustment, and its old minute corrections are dropped. **Recent changes** names the new setting "Hijri date adjustment".

- **PR4 step 3 of 8: a refused save in the four content editors lists every problem at once and keeps what you typed** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-3). Applies to events, announcements, community news and newsletters.
  - **Fixes a real bug:** before, when a save was refused (for example a published event without a place), the form emptied and you had to type everything again, although the message said "Your entered text is still here". Now every box, choice and tick stays as you left it. A picture or PDF you had chosen must be chosen again (browsers do not allow keeping it); the message says so.
  - **All problems at once:** instead of only the first problem, the box above the Save button says "Please fix 3 things before saving:" and lists each one, top to bottom in the order of the form, for example "Starts: add a start time, or save as a draft.", "Place: add where it happens, or save as a draft." and "Describe the picture: write one sentence about the picture for people who cannot see it." (new wording, see Needs your confirmation).
  - Every message now starts with the name of the box it is about, as that form shows it (for example "Event name:", "Ends:", "Sign-up link:", "Hide after:", "The newsletter:"; a developer-side consistency fix, 2026-10-05, so each message names a box that is on the form; see Needs your confirmation). The People forms are unchanged (they already kept what was typed); the Prayer & iqamah screen has the same list (step 4).
  - **Fixes a second bug:** a link that is not a web address at all (for example with a space in it) showed an error page and lost what you typed. It is now refused like any other problem, with "Link: use a secure web address (starting with https://)."

- **PR4 fix: old prayer settings that have both a fixed iqamah time and "minutes after the start" keep working, and the dashboard asks the board to pick one** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), D-14 and its 2026-10-05 amendment, your answer to Q58). Before PR4 the dashboard allowed both for one prayer (the minutes won). Step 2 made the site refuse to even read such settings, so the public prayer times would have shown as unavailable and the dashboard's Prayer & iqamah page as "Settings could not be loaded".
  - Now such settings are read as stored: visitors see the "minutes after the start" time, exactly as before, and the Prayer & iqamah page opens.
  - On Prayer & iqamah that prayer shows both boxes (the time and the minutes), neither **Fixed time** nor **After adhan** picked, and a red line under them: "Fajr iqamah: pick Fixed time or After adhan, not both." Picking one hides the other box; the next **Save prayer settings** stores only the one picked.
  - **Save prayer settings** refuses a prayer with both, with the same words, until one is picked.
  - Restoring an older version from **History** that had both for one prayer is refused with "This earlier version cannot be restored: Fajr iqamah: pick Fixed time or After adhan, not both." (fix the current settings by hand instead).
  - The second read-only check under Settings below stays as an extra safety net, but it no longer blocks the release.

- **PR4 step 4 of 8: the new Prayer & iqamah screen** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-4; the approved picture "option A", D-1). Dashboard → **Prayer & iqamah** is now one page with four numbered sections and one **Save prayer settings** button, so nothing is hidden before you save:
  - A note at the top says prayer **start** times (adhan) are worked out automatically for the Musallah (Muslim World League · Standard Asr) and you only set the **iqamah**.
  - **1. Daily iqamah**: a table with one line per prayer. Each prayer has a **Fixed time / After adhan** switch and one box (D-2): a time such as 12:30, or the minutes after the adhan, such as 20 (filling in both by mistake is no longer possible). The **Today** column shows the iqamah visitors see today (from the saved settings). Under the table: **Times start on** and **Times end on** ("After this date the iqamah times are hidden until you update them.").
  - **2. Jumu’ah (every Friday)**: **Khutbah** and **Iqamah** (the **Room** box went in review fixes PR-3).
  - **3. Changes for one date**: "Only that date changes. The next day goes back to normal by itself." **+ Add a date** adds a card with **Date** and **Reason (shown to visitors)**. When the date is a Friday, **Jumu’ah on this Friday** offers **As usual**, **No Jumu’ah this week** or **Different time** (then Khutbah and Iqamah). A closed line **Different iqamah for a daily prayer on this date** opens the five daily times for that date. **Remove this date** takes the card away (saved with the next Save). Up to 20 dates, as before.
  - **4. Hijri date**: **One day earlier**, **As calculated** or **One day later**, each showing the date it gives today, for example "One day later — 24 Rabiʻ II 1448 AH" (D-6). The choice stays until someone changes it.
  - **Note for visitors (optional)** (before: "Schedule notice"), shown under the prayer table on **/prayer**.
  - A closed section **Musallah location for prayer times (until the map pin)** keeps the latitude and longitude boxes until PR7 (D-3).
  - A refused save lists every problem at once and keeps everything you typed, like the content editors since step 3. The messages name the box, for example "Changes for one date: add a reason (shown to visitors)." and "Times start on and Times end on: fixed times need both dates, and the end cannot be before the start."
  - The dashboard card now reads "Prayer & iqamah — Iqamah, Jumu’ah, one-day changes and Hijri date →".

- **PR4 step 5 of 8: simpler event, announcement, news and newsletter forms** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-5; the approved pictures, D-7). Each form now shows only the boxes that change something on the website, with plain names and short hints. Nothing stored is lost: a box a form no longer shows keeps what was saved in it.
  - **Event:** **Event name**; **Date**, **Starts** and **Ends (optional)** side by side ("Grenada time. Events that go past midnight: add one entry per day."); **Place** ("For example “Charter Hall, room 2”."); **What is it about?**; **Picture (optional)** and **Describe the picture** ("One sentence, for people using screen readers."); **Sign-up link (optional)**. Gone: "Short summary", "Expiration date" and "Feature on the homepage" (they did nothing for events). An older event that only had a short summary shows it in **What is it about?**, and after the next save visitors see exactly the same description.
  - **Announcement:** **Headline**; **One-line summary** ("Shown on the homepage strip and in lists."); **Full message**; **Show from** and **Hide after (optional)**; **Show in the homepage notice strip** ("Only the newest one with this ticked shows."); picture, description and **Link (optional)**.
  - **Community news:** **Headline**; **One-line summary**; **Full story**; **Date** ("A future date publishes it on that day."); picture, description and **Link (optional)**; **Pin to the top of Community updates**; a closed **More options** line holds **Hide after (optional)** (it opens by itself when a date is set).
  - **Newsletter:** **Issue name**; **Issue date**; **Short description (optional)**; **The newsletter**: **Upload a PDF (max 3 MB)** or **Link to it (https://…)**, then the file or the link. Gone: "Full description", "Feature on the homepage" and "Expiration date" (the last one silently took the issue out of the archive).
  - **"Who can see it?"** replaces the "Published" tick on every form: **Only the board (draft)** or **Everyone on the website**. It works exactly like the tick did.
  - The list of problems of a refused save is now at the **top** of the form, and the page scrolls up to it. The button is now just **Save**.
  - An archived event's page no longer shows a "Short summary" line (events have no summary box any more); its name and date still show.

- **PR4 step 6 of 8: "Show me how" tours** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-6; D-8, D-9, D-10). **You approved the 20 step texts and the tour behaviour on 2026-10-03**, before this step was merged. A short guided tour points at the parts of a page one at a time, each with one or two sentences, "Step 1 of 5" and the buttons **Skip tour**, **Back** and **Next** (**Done** on the last step). The rest of the page is dimmed while it runs.
  - **The first time someone opens the dashboard, its tour starts by itself** (5 steps: the content cards, Prayer & iqamah, People, Recent changes, Help). **Skip tour** or **Done** ends it, and it does not start by itself again in that browser (D-9: remembered in the browser only, so it can show again on a new phone or after clearing the browser; nothing is stored in the database).
  - A **Show me how** button beside **Sign out** replays the page's tour on four pages: the dashboard, **Prayer & iqamah** (6 steps: automatic start times, Daily iqamah, Jumu’ah, Changes for one date, Hijri date, Save and History), **editing an item** (events, announcements, news, newsletters; 5 steps: name, date and time, Who can see it?, Save, Duplicate or archive) and **People** (4 steps: Add a person, Show on public page?, Dashboard login, Edit sections). No other tour starts by itself. Adding `?tour=1` to one of these pages' addresses starts its tour (for links from the Help page in step 7).
  - Works with the keyboard alone (the step's **Next** button gets the focus; Tab moves between the buttons; Enter presses; **Escape** ends the tour like **Skip tour**) and with screen readers (each step is announced as a dialog with its title). With "reduce motion" switched on in the device settings, the page jumps to each step instead of gliding.
  - **The 20 step texts you approved** (title, then text):
    - **Dashboard:** 1 "Your content": "Events, announcements, community news and newsletters each have a card. Open one to add, edit or publish items." · 2 "Prayer & iqamah": "Set iqamah times, the weekly Jumu’ah, changes for one date and the Hijri date. Prayer start times are calculated automatically." · 3 "People": "Add team members, choose who appears on the public team list, and give someone a dashboard login." · 4 "Recent changes": "See who changed what and when, to check an edit or find something to put back." · 5 "Help": "Step-by-step guides for every task. Press “Show me how” at the top of a page to replay its tour."
    - **Prayer & iqamah:** 1 "Start times are automatic": "Prayer start (adhan) times are calculated for the Musallah. You only set when the congregation prays." · 2 "Daily iqamah": "For each prayer choose Fixed time, such as 12:30, or After adhan, such as 20 minutes after the start." · 3 "Jumu’ah": "The khutbah time and iqamah time used every Friday." · 4 "Changes for one date": "Add a date to change that day’s iqamah or one Friday’s Jumu’ah. The next day goes back to normal by itself." · 5 "Hijri date": "Move the Hijri date one day earlier or later when the local moon sighting differs from the calculation." · 6 "Save and History": "One Save button keeps every section. History, below it, lists earlier versions you can restore."
    - **Editing an item:** 1 "Name": "The name visitors see first. Keep it short and clear." · 2 "Date and time": "When it happens or when it shows on the website. All times are Grenada time." · 3 "Who can see it?": "“Only the board” keeps a private draft. “Everyone on the website” publishes it." · 4 "Save": "Nothing is saved until you press Save. If something is missing, a list at the top says what to fix." · 5 "Duplicate or archive": "Duplicate copies this item as a new draft. Archive takes it off the website; you can restore it later."
    - **People:** 1 "Add a person": "Add a team member with their role and section." · 2 "Show on public page?": "Shows whether someone is on the public team list. Change it when editing them; it needs their recorded consent." · 3 "Dashboard login": "Shows whether someone can sign in to this dashboard. Invite them, or turn their login off, when editing them." · 4 "Edit sections": "Add, rename or reorder the sections the team is grouped in, such as Board."
  - The tour is only part of the dashboard; visitors' pages do not load it. It uses the free react-joyride library (MIT licence), already approved in [ADR 0008](adr/0008-layered-admin-onboarding.md).
  - **Fix (D-15, planner amendment):** an event or newsletter that still had an old "Expiration date" from before step 5 (when those forms lost that box) now loses it the next time it is saved. Before, that hidden date could take the item off the website, or refuse the save with "Hide after: choose the item's date or a later one." although the form shows no Hide after box. Announcements and community news keep their **Hide after** box and date.

- **PR4 step 7 of 8: pictures on the Help page, and a reminder before the iqamah times end** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-7; D-11, D-12).
  - **Help pictures (D-11).** Dashboard → **Read the board editing guide** now shows a picture of the real screen with the button or box to use circled in red. The eight most common tasks have a picture under every numbered step: **Create and publish**, **Events**, **Announcements**, **Prayer & iqamah**, the new **Change one Friday’s Jumu’ah**, the new **Hijri date**, **Archive, restore and duplicate** and **Inviting and access**. Every other help topic has one picture. Clicking a picture opens it full size in a new tab. 46 pictures in all.
  - **Help cards rewritten for the new screens** (steps 4 and 5): numbered steps with the new names (**Who can see it?**, **Save**, **Show from**, **Hide after**, **Show in the homepage notice strip**, **Fixed time / After adhan**, **Times end on**), two new cards (**Change one Friday’s Jumu’ah**, which also says to publish an announcement for now, D-4; and **Hijri date**), and **End of term** now says to check the Hijri date setting. Cards for pages with a tour end with **Show me on the real page →**, which opens that page with its "Show me how" tour running.
  - **Dashboard reminder (D-12).** From 7 days before the date in **Times end on** until that day, the dashboard shows "Iqamah times end on 8 Oct — update them" (with that date) and a link **Open Prayer & iqamah →**. Since step 8 it stays after that date too (see step 8). It goes away once **Times end on** is moved later.
  - Behind the scenes: the pictures are taken automatically from the sandbox (sample data, never the real site) by one developer command, `npm run help:pictures`, so they can be re-taken whenever a screen changes. The automatic checks fail when a circled button is renamed or moved, or a picture is missing or too large.

- **PR4 step 8 of 8: the iqamah reminder stays after the times have ended, and the guides are up to date** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md), WP-8; D-12 amendment). This finishes PR4.
  - **The reminder keeps showing after the end date** (your answer of 2026-10-04, "show the warning"). Once the date in **Times end on** has passed, the iqamah times are hidden on the public pages, and the dashboard now says "Iqamah times ended on 8 Oct — update them" (with that date, written the same way as before) with the same **Open Prayer & iqamah →** link, until a board member sets a later **Times end on**. Before the end date nothing changes: "Iqamah times end on 8 Oct — update them" from 7 days before. One rule in the code decides both, so a later reminder (for example by email) can reuse it.
  - **Guides:** "Prayer times explained" describes the new screen (fixed calculation, iqamah as a fixed time or minutes after the adhan, changes for one date including one Friday's Jumu'ah, the Hijri date); the board guide says the Help page has pictures and tours; the **Prayer & iqamah** help card and the board guide now also say the reminder stays after the end date until a later **Times end on** is set (text only, the pictures are unchanged); the developer and owner guides no longer mention the removed calculation settings; the architecture picture shows the Hijri date taking the board's adjustment.
