  - **Roadmap:** PR4 is **Done**. The Jumu'ah changes moved from PR5 to PR4 (PR5 keeps the "Also show an urgent notice?" question, D-4); PR7 will remove the old, unused prayer settings from the database; the three inventory items PR4 left out (#68, #72, #85) are listed as not scheduled yet.
  - **Your PR4 answers** (all of 2026-10-03, each the recommended option, plus the 2026-10-04 reminder answer) are recorded in the contract's Decisions, D-1 to D-13.

- **PR8 clean-up: no visible change** ([PR8 contract](contracts/restyle-public.md), WP-8). Behind the scenes only: the page styles and the two-line headline every public page shares moved to one shared place, three small pieces that pages had copied (the ink ring around one button, the social icons and the three-dot ink mark) became one shared piece each, and old colour names and page styles that nothing uses any more were removed. Six old colour names stay until the dashboard restyle, because the dashboard's sign-in form still uses them. Every page, light and dark, and the dashboard should look exactly as before.

- **Dashboard breadcrumb trail** (user's choice, 2026-10-03: option A of a trail, a back link, or a browser Back button). Every dashboard page below the dashboard now shows where you are above its title, for example **Dashboard › Events › Welcome to the community**. Each part is a link to that page (never the browser history), so you can go up one level or straight home. It replaces the lone **Board dashboard** link and the separate **Back to People** / **Back to archived …** links.

- **A shared photo viewer, ready for later use (no visible change yet).** Developers now have one photo viewer (the Filmstrip look the user picked) for pages that want visitors to look at photos closely. Nothing on the public site or the dashboard uses it yet, so nothing looks different; the first page to use it is Merch (roadmap PR5). Nothing gets the viewer by default: each page chooses it for its own photos, and decorative or heading pictures never get it. With several photos it shows the photo large, a strip of small photos with the ink ring around the one you are on, **Previous** / **Next** and "2 of 5"; with one photo, just the photo and **Close**. A line of text under the photo appears only when that photo has a caption (merch photos will not); the photo description that screen readers read is never shown as text.
- **Hijri date always beside the normal date** (user's choice, 2026-10-03: option A, "prayer times only, always paired with the normal date"; [PR8 contract](contracts/restyle-public.md) D-11). The Hijri date shows only with the prayer times (Home, Prayer) and on Ramadan, always next to the normal date; events, posts, resources and anything the board types never show it, and visitors get no calendar setting.
  - **Home:** the prayer block now starts with today's date (for example "Saturday, October 3") with the Hijri date ("22 Rabiʻ II 1448 AH", Arabic beneath, small moon) as the smaller line under it. The date moved up from the Jumu’ah line at the bottom, so it is not shown twice; that line now starts with the Jumu’ah details.
  - **Prayer:** unchanged: "Today · Saturday, October 3" with the Hijri date under it.
  - **Ramadan:** the **Tonight** sheet starts with today's date, the Hijri date under it. The small line above the page heading adds the Gregorian months of that Ramadan: "Ramadan 1448 AH · Feb–Mar 2027". The months are worked out from the Hijri year in the board's text (same Umm al-Qura calendar and the board's Hijri ±1 day setting), so changing the text to "Ramadan 1449 AH" changes the months too; text without a Hijri year shows as typed.
  - The " · from Maghrib" note after the Hijri date is gone, and the Hijri date now changes at midnight together with the normal date beside it (like phone calendars and printed timetables), so the two always match. Prayer times and Ramadan's **Tonight** still follow the real sunset.

- **The "page not found" design can carry a page's own wording** (internal; nothing visible changes yet). PR6 uses it for an old Give link to a closed fund: "This has closed" with links to Donate and Home, as the user approved.

- **PR5a step 3: phone photos are made smaller before upload** (roadmap PR5, your decision D0 #13 "the browser shrinks photos before upload"; PR5 plan draft, PR5a-WP3). On every photo box in the dashboard (events, announcements, news, People):
  - When you choose a photo, the phone or computer makes it smaller first (at most 1600 pixels on its longest side, which is what the site keeps anyway) and turns it upright. A 5 MB camera photo becomes a few hundred KB, so it uploads quickly and no longer hits the 3 MB limit. This happens quietly: no line appears, and **Save** is greyed out for the second or so it takes (your answers to Q92, Q93 and Q45, 2026-10-05).
  - If the browser cannot open the photo (for example an iPhone HEIC photo on some Android browsers), the photo stays as it is and a line says so, with what to do (take a screenshot of it or share it as JPEG). A photo still over 3 MB is then not sent: **Save** stops before anything uploads and points at the photo box.
  - Nothing else changes: the server still accepts only JPEG, PNG or WebP up to 3 MB and still makes its own smaller WebP copy, so a photo that skipped the browser step is checked exactly as before. PNG and WebP pictures keep their see-through parts.
  - Behind the scenes: the photo limits (3 MB, 1600 pixels, at most 3 new photos and 3.5 MB of photos per save) live in one place that both the browser and the server read; the "3 MB" in the photo and PDF hints, the Help page and the server's "Each upload must be 3 MB or smaller." come from there too (same words as before). The browser's check before **Save** looks only at photo boxes; a PDF is checked by the server alone. No new library.
  - Lines appear under the photo box only for real problems: the could-not-shrink line above, and "Add up to 3 new photos per save, then save again." No hint was added under the box.
- **Small fixes from your answers (2026-10-05)**:
  - Content forms (events, announcements, news, newsletters): a refused save names each box as the form shows it, for example "Event name: fill in this box." or "Headline: fill in this box." instead of "Title: add a title." (developer-side consistency fix; see Needs your confirmation). The form's labels are the only source of these names, so a renamed box renames its messages too. On Prayer & iqamah, a **Note for visitors** over 1,000 characters is now refused with "Note for visitors: use 1,000 characters or fewer." (before, a technical English message).
  - Prayer & iqamah: a prayer stored with both a fixed time and minutes shows both and asks the board to pick one (Q58; see the PR4 fix above).
  - Phone photos are made smaller without any line or hint (Q92, Q93, Q45; see PR5a step 3 above).
  - **Prayer calculation confirmed:** you checked the calculated times (Muslim World League, Standard Asr, [ADR 0017](adr/0017-fixed-prayer-calculation-mwl-standard-asr.md)) against mobile prayer apps on 2026-10-05 and they match (Q84). Nothing more to confirm.
  - The MSA is a student club run by its board (your answer to Q91): the docs now name the board wherever they asked a religious leader to confirm or review something, for example the Arabic reminders.
  - Docs only (no visible change): **Needs your confirmation** below moves what your answers settled into **Answered (2026-10-05)**, and the archived photo notes (`docs/archive/notes/ASSET_MANIFEST.md`) no longer say the Facebook photo archive and the route video are kept (both remain only in the private-history repository; Q61 A).
- **PR6 step 1: one new required setting, `GO2PAY_API_KEY`** (nothing visible changes; [roadmap](roadmap.md) PR6, WP-1). This is the Go2Pay key the website will later use to make, change and close the MSA's payment pages by itself. Nothing uses it yet.
  - Allowed values: `off`, a Go2Pay **test** key (`g2p_test_…`, takes no real money) or a **live** key (`g2p_live_…`, real money). The live site accepts only a live key or `off`, and stays `off`: which key it gets is undecided (P-70 under "You or the founder must decide"). A developer's computer accepts only a test key or `off`.
  - If it is missing or wrong, the deploy stops with a message naming it (the live site stays on the previous version). The message never shows the key.
  - The key never reaches visitors' browsers, and only one file in the code may hand it on.
  - The [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md#the-payments-key-go2pay_api_key) has the steps: `off` on the live site (which key comes later is undecided, P-70), the developer's test key (also waits for P-70), revoke if leaked, rotate yearly.
- **PR6 step 2: what Go2Pay can and cannot do, checked with the test key** (no code; [Go2Pay notes](proposals/go2pay.md)). No payment was made and no real product was changed.
  - Go2Pay **can**: show a donation box on a payment page (preset buttons plus an "Other" amount, with its own caption), list payments per product, show the giver's optional note, and tell us the 5% fee on each payment.
  - Go2Pay **cannot** (today): filter payments by date, or let the website create a product with the test key (every try failed with a Go2Pay server error, so the product checks are waiting on Go2Pay).
  - **Safety finding:** the full key can read the MSA's bank details, and a withdrawal feature exists that the public docs do not describe. The plan required Go2Pay's written promise that no key can withdraw money or change bank accounts; that promise is missing, so this is a question for you (P-70 under "You or the founder must decide").
  - The email to Go2Pay support is drafted in the [Go2Pay notes](proposals/go2pay.md), ready to send from the MSA Gmail.
- **PR5a part 1 of 3 (PR-A): the database and rules for urgent notices, event status and the New board term** ([PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md), WP1 and WP2). Nothing new appears in the dashboard or on the public site yet: the screens come in PR-B (editors and public pages) and PR-C (New board term, Recent changes filters, help). Once the database update below is applied:
  - **Urgent notices:** an announcement can now carry an exact end time ("Hide at", Grenada time). While it is urgent it is never also "featured" and has no "Hide after" date, and it ends on or after its own date. Several can be current at once; each one disappears from the public site, photo included, at its own minute, by itself. The order they will rotate in is fixed: newest date first (D-30).
  - **Event status:** an event can be Scheduled, Cancelled (a reason is required) or Postponed (with the earlier date kept, or "new date to be announced"). It stays one event with one address and one calendar entry. Calendar files will say CONFIRMED, CANCELLED (titled "Cancelled: …") or TENTATIVE (postponed, no new date yet), and a re-added file replaces the old one.
  - **Overnight events:** an event's end time may now be earlier than its start (for example 10 PM to 3 AM): it ends the next morning, and it stays in "upcoming" until then. An end equal to the start is refused with "Ends: choose a time different from the start time." News, notices and newsletters keep "end after start". Until PR-B, the event form's hint still says "Events that go past midnight: add one entry per day." and the public pages show such an event as "10:00 PM – 3:00 AM" without "(next day)".
  - **New board term:** two new lists in the database, the hand-over steps (six starter steps: the five from the "End of term" help card plus "Change shared passwords") and who holds each shared login (the account, a holder and a 2-step phone holder chosen from People, the last change date and a short note). No box can hold a password or code. Any board member can read and change them, nobody can delete them (archive instead), visitors see nothing, and every change is in Recent changes.
  - **Recent changes:** urgent notices are now highlighted like prayer changes; the dashboard's "prayer times changed" note still points only at prayer changes. The log can now be filtered by person ("Site owner" included), kind (Prayer & iqamah, Events, Announcements, News, Newsletters, People and logins, New board term) and Grenada days; the filter screen comes in PR-C.
  - **Manage people:** the rule "fewer than two active people can manage people" is ready for a warning (PR-C). The database still only blocks zero.
  - The local sandbox (`npm run demo`) has samples for all of it: two urgent notices (one ends two hours after the demo starts), a cancelled, two postponed and an overnight event, the six steps with two ticked, and two shared logins. Until PR-B they show like ordinary notices and events.
  - For developers: `npm run test:db` and the new `tests/notices-migration.test.ts` check every database rule above.
  - The Help page's picture of a refused event save still shows the old line "Ends: must be after the start time, on the same day."; all help pictures are re-taken in PR-C, after the new screens exist.
- **PR5a part 2 of 3 (PR-B): urgent notices under the menu, cancelled and postponed events, the Jumu'ah notice question, and Edit | Preview tabs** ([PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md), WP4, WP5, WP6). Needs PR-A's database update (below) on the database the site uses.
  - **Announcements → How important is it?** replaces the "Show in the homepage notice strip" tick box with three choices: **Normal** (Community updates only), **Featured** (also the homepage notice strip) and **Urgent** (on every page, under the menu). Urgent swaps **Hide after** for **Hide at (exact time)**: a date and a time, Grenada time. When Urgent is chosen and other urgent notices are current, the form lists them ("Urgent now: 2 …"); the Announcements list shows "Urgent now: N" above the list. Featured with no **Hide after** date shows the warning "Featured notices with no Hide after date stay on Home until you change them." (it still saves).
  - **The urgent band:** a dark ink band under the menu on every public page (not in the dashboard). One notice stays still. Two or more show newest first with ‹ › arrows, a dot each, "1 of N" and swipe on phones, and **no pause button**: it moves on every 7 seconds until the visitor taps an arrow or a dot or swipes, then stays where they left it on that page; it waits while the mouse or a finger is on it or keyboard focus is inside; with "Reduce motion" on the phone it never moves by itself. Each notice leaves the band at its own **Hide at** minute: every public page is now rendered fresh on each visit for this.
  - **Events → Is it still happening?** (at the end of the event form): **Scheduled**, **Postponed** ("New date is known → change the Date above" or "New date not known yet") or **Cancelled** with a required **Reason**. Visitors see the event's line under its title on **/calendar**, the event page and Home's week: "Cancelled: (reason)" with the title struck through, "Postponed from Sat 10 Oct", or "Postponed: new date to be announced". A cancelled event stays listed until its date passes. **Add to Calendar** gives a file marked CANCELLED and titled "Cancelled: …".
  - **Overnight events:** an end earlier than the start shows as "10:00 PM – 3:00 AM (next day)". The event form's hint under the date and times now says "Grenada time. An end time earlier than the start means the next day." (it said "Events that go past midnight: add one entry per day.").
  - The event page's sign-up button reads **Sign up →** (it read **Register**). A link to an archived or unknown event shows "This event is no longer listed" with links to **Events** and **Home**.
  - **Edit | Preview tabs** on the event, announcement, news and newsletter forms, on every screen size. **Preview** shows the item as visitors will see it, drawn by the public site's own parts (the event card and event page top; the notice strip or urgent band and the announcement page; the news page; the newsletter archive row), labelled "Preview (not saved)". Going back to **Edit** keeps everything typed and a chosen photo. A future **Show from** date shows "Scheduled: appears on 10 Oct". Every text box shows a grey example (for example "Iftar at the Musallah", "Musallah, Charter Hall").
  - **Prayer & iqamah → Changes for one date:** a Friday whose Jumu'ah is cancelled or moved, new or changed in this save, asks **Also show an urgent notice on every page?** (Yes or No, required). **Yes** shows the notice text, prefilled ("No Jumu’ah at the Musallah this Friday (16 Oct)" or "Jumu’ah this Friday (16 Oct) moves to 1:15 PM in Lower Modica Hall") and **Hide at**, prefilled 30 minutes after that Friday's iqamah (the new one if moved, the usual one if cancelled); both can be changed. Saving posts the urgent notice right after the prayer change. Saving the page again unchanged asks nothing and posts nothing. If the notice cannot be saved the prayer change is kept and the page says "The Jumu’ah change is saved, but the urgent notice could not be posted. Post it from Announcements."
  - **/ramadan:** the small line above the heading reads "Ramadan 2027" (it read "Ramadan 1448 AH · Feb–Mar 2027"). The Hijri date stays only next to tonight's prayer times (D-31).
  - Help: the Announcements card now says to choose **Featured** or **Urgent** under **How important is it?**, and the Jumu'ah card's note describes the new question. The full help update (new cards, tours) comes in PR-C. The help pictures were re-taken from the sandbox, since the content forms changed (they now show the Edit | Preview tabs, the grey examples and the new boxes).

- **PR5a part 3 of 3 (PR-C): New board term, Recent changes filters, Remove login, HEIC photos, and the help for all of PR5a** ([PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md), WP7, WP8, WP3c; the contract is now marked done). Needs PR-A's database update (below).
  - **New board term** (a new dashboard card, `/admin/handover`): the hand-over checklist with a tick box per step (the day it was ticked shows beside it) and "2 of 6 done"; **Start a new term…** clears every tick and keeps the steps; **Edit the steps** adds, rewords, moves up or down, archives and restores steps (each step can link to a dashboard page, chosen from a list). Beside it, **Who holds each shared login**: the account, the holder and the 2-step phone holder chosen from People, the day the password was last changed and a short note; **+ Add an account**, click an account to edit or archive it, **Archived accounts** to restore. No box takes a password or a code. Any board member can do all of it; every change is in Recent changes.
  - **Fewer than two people can manage people:** the dashboard and the New board term page show "Only 1 active person can manage people. Give a second person Manage people." with **Open People →**. A warning only; nothing is locked.
  - **Recent changes filters:** **Who** (each person who has or had a login, or Site owner), **What** (Prayer & iqamah, Events, Announcements, News, Newsletters, People and logins, New board term), **From** and **Until** (Grenada days), then **Show**. The filters stay in the page address, so **Older changes** and a shared link keep them; **Clear filters** shows everything. Nothing is ever removed. An urgent notice's change now carries the label "Urgent notice" (prayer changes keep "Prayer change").
  - **Remove login** (people who can manage people, deactivated logins only): on a person's page, under **Dashboard login**, type the person's name to delete their login account for good. Their People entry and history stay: their past changes in Recent changes keep their name, and **Who** still lists them. A new invite gives access again.
  - **HEIC photos:** when a browser cannot open a HEIC photo (Android, Chrome on a computer), the photo box says "This photo is in HEIC format, which this browser can't open. Share it from the phone as JPEG (or take a screenshot), then choose it again." and Save stops; nothing is uploaded and the typed text stays. iPhone and Mac Safari keep turning HEIC into a smaller JPEG with no message. The photo box still offers only JPEG, PNG and WebP.
  - **Help** (`/admin/help`): new cards **Cancel or postpone an event**, **Urgent notices** (several take turns, newest first, each hides at its own time), **Phone photos** (what to do with the HEIC line), **Find a change**, and **New board term** (replaces the "End of term" list, links to the new screen); the Events card now explains overnight events and points to the new card instead of "unpublish and announce"; Inviting and access mentions Remove login; Recent changes mentions urgent notices. **Show me how** tours: the item tour adds Edit or Preview, How important is it? and Is it still happening?; the Prayer tour adds the Jumu'ah notice question; the dashboard tour adds New board term; new tours on New board term and Recent changes. All help pictures were re-taken.
  - **Privacy page:** one added sentence: "The dashboard records which board member holds each shared account, by name only."
  - The [board guide](guides/board.md) lists the new tasks; "See how a post will look before saving" is no longer under "cannot do yet".
- **PR7 part 1 of 2 (PR-A): one musallah pin, stored in the database, feeds the directions and the prayer start times** ([PR7 contract](contracts/pr7-single-pin-map.md), WP-1, WP-2, WP-3). Needs the PR7 database update below on the database the site uses.
  - **The musallah location is now one pin in the database**, not a Vercel setting. It starts at the Google Maps place "MSA Mosque" that you gave (12.0025521, −61.7723834, D-3); the board confirms it on site (Needs your confirmation). Only a place in Grenada, Carriacou or Petite Martinique can be stored (D-9). Every move will be listed in **Recent changes** as "Edited musallah location" (with the old and new latitude and longitude) under **What → Prayer & iqamah**; it is not highlighted (E-4).
  - **Find us on campus** (home page, **/prayer**, **/contact**): the **Open Apple Maps** and **Open Google Maps** links now show on every site, and both go to the pin with walking directions. The Google link is `https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=12.0025521%2C-61.7723834&travelmode=walking`; the Apple link uses Apple's current address `https://maps.apple.com/directions?destination=…&mode=walking` (E-5). The **Load location map** button and its OpenStreetMap preview are gone; until PR-B the box shows the drawn placeholder above the two links. PR-B brings the real map, the "Apple devices only" rule for the Apple button, the button names from D-7 and the privacy page wording.
  - **Prayer start times are calculated at the pin.** The latitude and longitude boxes on **Prayer & iqamah** ("Musallah location for prayer times (until the map pin)") are gone; PR-B puts the "Musallah location" section with the draggable dot in their place. Saving prayer times, and restoring an earlier version from **History**, never writes coordinates any more, and an old History version that has them still restores. If the pin cannot be read (for example the database is down, or the update below is not applied yet), the prayer block says "Prayer start times are temporarily unavailable." (D-10) and the iqamah times set as "minutes after the adhan" hide; fixed iqamah times still show.
  - If production had no coordinates stored, **prayer start times start showing** with this release. If it had some, starts may move by up to a minute on some days (see the read-only check in Settings).
  - **Settings:** `MUSALLAH_LOCATION_CONFIRMED`, `MUSALLAH_LATITUDE` and `MUSALLAH_LONGITUDE` are no longer read or required. A deploy without them works; leftover values are ignored. The [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md) §11 now says so.
  - Sandbox (`npm run demo`): the sample pin is the same Google place, so sample prayer starts move by up to a minute from before (they used a test point 5.8 km away).
  - **Do not merge a Release PR that has PR-A without PR-B.** Until PR-B, the box shows the drawn placeholder ("Your entrance pin goes here … This diagram is not a real map.") instead of a map. PR-B (next entry) replaces it.
  - For developers: `npm run test:db` and the new `tests/musallah-location-migration.test.ts` check the table's rules; `tests/places.test.ts` checks the exact links, the Apple-device rule (used by PR-B) and the Grenada box.
- **PR7 part 2 of 2 (PR-B): the musallah map, Get directions by device, and moving the pin from the dashboard** ([PR7 contract](contracts/pr7-single-pin-map.md), WP-4, WP-5, WP-7). Needs PR-A's database update (below). No new setting.
  - **Find us on campus** (home page, **/prayer**, **/contact**): a map in the site's paper colours with street names, shop names and campus building names (Charter Hall, Superdorm 3 and others, from OpenStreetMap), and the musallah as a small dot with a small "SGU Musallah" tag, in light and dark (the theme switch recolours it). It loads by itself when the box comes near the screen: no button, and a page you never scroll down loads nothing extra. A phone set to save data shows "Map hidden to save data." instead. The only credit is a small ⓘ in the corner: tapping it shows "© OpenStreetMap contributors · OpenFreeMap" (D-15); there is no MapLibre logo. On a phone one finger scrolls the page and two fingers move the map.
  - Under the map: **Get directions** with **Apple Maps** and **Google Maps** on an iPhone, iPad or Mac, and **Google Maps** only on every other device; both give walking directions to the pin (D-7). The drawn placeholder and the **Open … Maps** text links are gone. Map service down: "The map can’t be shown right now." with the buttons still there. Pin unreadable (database down): "The map can’t be loaded right now. Please use the written directions." and no buttons.
  - **Dashboard → Prayer & iqamah → Musallah location** (the last section, under **History**): drag the dot to the entrance (or click it and use the arrow keys: about 2 m a press, 20 m with Shift), then **Save location** → "Musallah location saved." It has its own Save, so saving prayer times and moving the pin never block each other. A dot dropped outside Grenada jumps back with "The pin must be in Grenada. Drag it back onto the SGU campus." **Try Google Maps directions ↗** and **Try Apple Maps directions ↗** open directions to the saved pin. If two board members save at once, the second sees "Someone else saved the location first. Reload to see it, then try again." If the pin cannot be read: "The musallah location could not be loaded. Check your connection and reload." and no map. Every move is in **Recent changes**.
  - **Privacy page:** "Optional map" is now **Map**: "When you scroll to the “Find us on campus” box, your browser loads map pictures from OpenFreeMap (tiles.openfreemap.org). Like any website, OpenFreeMap sees your internet address. We send it nothing else, and it sets no cookies. The written directions work without the map." (D-10)
  - **Help** (`/admin/help`): new cards **Move the musallah pin** (with a picture) and **Google Maps listing** (text only, no pictures of Google's screens: ask SGU first, claim, verify, website, hours with a Jumu'ah line, real photos, report wrong photos, check the pin; D-11). The Prayer & iqamah **Show me how** tour has two more steps. The board guide no longer lists "Change the map pin" as a site-owner task; the owner guide §11 points to the Google Maps listing card.
  - Security headers: the site now allows the map service only (`connect-src https://tiles.openfreemap.org`), a same-site map worker (`worker-src 'self'`) and no frames at all (`frame-src 'none'`; the OpenStreetMap preview is gone).
  - New free package `maplibre-gl` 6.12.0 (BSD-3-Clause, approved as D-12). Campus building names come through our own route `/api/campus-names`, which asks OpenStreetMap at most once a day per pin; visitors' browsers never contact OpenStreetMap.
  - For developers: `tests/map.test.ts` (paper style, credit, colour tokens, campus names never failing, CSP), the e2e tests in `tests/e2e/demo.spec.ts` (iPhone and Pixel buttons, the map loads only when scrolled to, blocked map service, Save-Data, the pin section: keyboard move, two tabs, outside Grenada), new help picture `public/admin-help/musallah.webp`; the Prayer & iqamah help pictures were re-taken because the screen gained the section.
- **Daily check, stage 1: one warning email to the MSA Gmail when something needs fixing** ([daily-check contract](contracts/daily-check-notifications.md), WP-1 to WP-7; [ADR 0022](adr/0022-daily-check-and-warning-emails.md)). Needs the database update and the three new settings below.
  - **Once a night** (between 01:00 and 02:00 Grenada time) Vercel starts the website's check. Today it checks one thing: the iqamah times. From 7 days before **Times end on**, the MSA Gmail gets **one** short email from itself, "SGU MSA website: 1 thing needs attention", listing the problem with a link to the dashboard page that fixes it. Nothing wrong, no email.
  - **At most two emails per problem:** the first when it starts ("Iqamah times end on Thu 15 Oct (in 7 days)"), a last call the day before ("(tomorrow)"), then nothing more: the dashboard keeps showing it until someone fixes it. A night Vercel skips is caught up the next night. A new **Times end on** date is a new problem with its own two emails.
  - **One shared inbox:** the email goes only to the MSA Gmail; the website stores no email address, so developers (who also have logins) never get warnings. Each line starts with its topic, such as **[Prayer]**, so whoever holds the Gmail can forward one topic with a Gmail filter.
  - **Dashboard:** the iqamah box at the top is now called **Needs attention** and lists the same lines as the email. The iqamah line now reads like the email: "Iqamah times end on Thu 15 Oct (in 7 days)" (it read "Iqamah times end on 15 Oct — update them"); after the end date "Iqamah times ended on Thu 15 Oct — update them" (it read "… on 15 Oct — update them"). The link is still **Open Prayer & iqamah →**.
  - **Only the live site sends.** The local sandbox records the email instead of sending it, and a developer's computer or a preview never sends, even with the real Gmail values.
  - **Help:** a new card, **Website warning emails**, with a picture of the box: what triggers the emails, the two-email rule, and how to pass one topic on with a Gmail filter or shared inbox access.
  - **Three new required settings** (`CRON_SECRET`, `MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS`, `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD`; Settings below). Without them the deploy stops with one message naming each (the live site stays on the previous version). The messages never show a value.
  - Behind the scenes: a new private list in the database, `warning_emails_sent` (warning name, step and day only; no names, emails or amounts), that only the server can read or add to. The nightly reads also count as database activity, so the free Supabase project is much less likely to pause.
  - Stage 2 (season days still "expected", and "close it in Go2Pay" to-dos) comes after PR6.

- **PR6 part 1 of 3 (PR-A): the giving database, data and Go2Pay connection behind the scenes** ([PR6 contract](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md), now approved; WP-2, WP-3, WP-4). Visitors and the board see no new screen yet: the Funds, Seasons and Payments screens come in PR-B, and the new Donate page, Give links and event tickets in PR-C. Needs the database update below.
  - **The plan is approved** and saved as the [PR6 contract](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md) with all your answers of 2026-10-05: four fixed categories (Zakat al-Fitr, Zakat al-Mal, Sadaqah, General), the board makes each Go2Pay product by hand and pastes its link, a "Take payments only" key, no price on our pages ("See price and buy ↗"), no headings unless the board types one, the 5% line in the fine print. The [roadmap](roadmap.md) links it.
  - **What the "Take payments only" key can do** is written down in the [Go2Pay notes](proposals/go2pay.md): it reads orders (so the Payments screen can show totals per fund) and is refused for products, customers, bank accounts and withdrawals. Warning found: a **test** key reads the **real** orders (real givers' names, emails and phones), so the website keeps only what Payments needs and stores nothing. The support email in the notes is now the newer one, to send from so-msa@sgu.edu.
  - **New database lists:** seasons (starting with Ramadan, Eid al-Fitr and Eid al-Adha), funds and tickets, and the Go2Pay page each one links to. The database itself makes sure that: once a fund has been shown on the Donate page its category can never change; a Go2Pay page belongs to one fund or ticket only, ever; a link is never edited or deleted (a new link is added); a season that a fund uses cannot be archived; nothing is ever deleted; visitors can read only funds and tickets that are shown, so a draft and its Go2Pay page stay private until the board ticks "Show". Recent changes will highlight a new Go2Pay link, the moment a fund's category locks, and a change to a season's days.
  - **Nobody can turn on their own "Manage money"** (D-18). On your own People page, under **Dashboard login**, the **Manage money** box is greyed out while it is off, with "You cannot turn on your own 'manage money' permission. Ask another manager." Another manager can still give it to you, and you can still turn your own off.
  - Behind the scenes: the rules (categories and their lines, when a fund shows, "Closes tomorrow", the slip order and headings, the Payments totals with zakat kept apart and no grand total, the CSV without names) are in one place with tests; the website reads Go2Pay only to list orders and check the key, never changes anything in Go2Pay, and refuses a too-powerful key on the live site. The local sandbox has sample funds, tickets and sample orders (made-up names) for the next screens.

- **PR5b part 1 of 3 (PR-A): the words and photos of every page are edited from the dashboard (Pages), with a sign-up forms list and a History for each page** ([PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md), WP1, WP2, WP3). **Needs the database update below on the database the site uses:** without it every public page shows its short fallback text instead of its real text.
  - **Pages** (a new dashboard card, `/admin/pages`): one card per page (Home, About, Prayer & Musallah, Jumu’ah, Musallah directions, Events page, Community page, Newsletter archive, Resources, Get involved, Sign-up forms, FAQ, Donate (hidden again by PR-B until the Donate page reads it), Contact, Support, Leaving the site, Site-wide), each with who saved it last and when. Opening one shows its boxes in sections ("Top of the page", "Our team", …), each with a grey example inside; boxes marked "(optional)" can stay empty and that part of the page is then hidden. Lists (FAQ questions, resources, ways to get involved, …) have **Move up**, **Move down**, **Remove** and **+ Add …**. Photo boxes take a new photo (made smaller on the phone as elsewhere), a description and the "Old photo" tick; at most 3 new photos per save. **Save page** puts the change on the website at once, no deploy.
  - **About → Our team**: the heading, the note under it and the line shown while nobody is listed (the people themselves stay in People).
  - **Site-wide**: the organisation's full and short name, tagline, email, social links and the description search engines show. The menu, footer links, header button and logo (and the Events and Resources filter lists) stay with the developer: they always come from the site's files, so they also show while the database cannot be read.
  - **Support**: the **Show the Support page on the website** switch (off today) and all its text.
  - **Prayer & Musallah**: the three lines under the iqamah times (when Prayer & iqamah has no notice, while no times are published, and when the times cannot be loaded) moved from code into this page's text; the words are the same.
  - **Sign-up forms** (`/admin/pages/forms`): each Google Form the site links to (where it shows, its link, **Open**, the line shown while closed, a short description) and a "How to: a Google Form for an event" card. Links must be Google Forms links (`https://docs.google.com/forms/…` or `https://forms.gle/…`; the database refuses anything else too); anything else is refused with "… · Link: use a Google Forms link (docs.google.com/forms/… or forms.gle/…).". Every form starts closed, as before (their link was a shared example form), so visitors see the same "coming soon" lines until the board pastes each real form and ticks **Open**.
  - **Events → Sign-up link** now also accepts Google Forms links only (same line, "Sign-up link: …"); news and newsletters keep any secure link.
  - **History** on each page: the last 20 changes, each with **Restore the version before this change**. A restored version is checked like any save and shows in Recent changes; a photo replaced since then stays as it is now.
  - **Recent changes**: page changes show as "Edited page text: About" with the sections that changed; **What** has a new choice, **Pages**.
  - Visitors see the same words as before: the database starts with a copy of today's text. Every page now reads its text from the database on each visit.
  - Not yet: the Qur'an verse picker and the help cards come in PR-C; the Donate opening words wait for PR6's Donate page (see PR-B below).
  - For developers: `content/*.yaml` is now only the sandbox's starter text for these pages (and the one-time seed), except the developer-owned parts (menu, header button, footer, logo, filter lists: [developer guide](guides/developer.md)), which the site always reads from YAML; `npm run demo` keeps its edits until restart. New tests: `tests/page-texts-migration.test.ts`, `tests/page-text.test.ts`, `tests/quran.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts`.
- **PR6 part 2 of 3 (PR-B): the Funds, Tickets, Seasons and Payments screens** ([PR6 contract](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md), WP-5, WP-6, WP-7). The board can now set up giving from the dashboard; visitors see nothing new yet (the new Donate page, Give links and event tickets come in PR-C). Needs PR-A's database update.
  - **Dashboard → Payments** (new card). Three tabs: **Money in**, **Funds**, **Tickets**. Any board member adds and edits funds and tickets; only board members with **Manage money** see Money in.
  - **Funds**: each fund has a name, one short sentence ("About this fund", required for both zakat categories), an optional heading (empty by default), one of the four categories (locked once the fund has shown on the Donate page), a season and/or its own days, "Show on the Donate page", an order number, and its Go2Pay page. The **Go2Pay page** box lists the three steps, the exact texts to copy into Go2Pay (title, description and donation caption, note on the receipt) with **Copy** buttons and length counters, and the link box: a typo or another site is refused with "Paste the Go2Pay page link, for example go2pay.gd/557."; a page another fund uses is refused naming that fund; earlier links are listed (their payments still count). Beside the form, the slip as the Donate page will show it and where it sits among the funds shown now. A fund tied to a season that ended shows **Running again in (season, year)**, which asks whether to keep the same Go2Pay page or use a new one.
  - **Funds list**: in the Donate page's order, with the category, whether it shows, when (Gregorian days only), the Go2Pay page it opens, and flags such as "Shows with no end date", "No Go2Pay link" and "Ended on (day) · close it in Go2Pay" (for 7 days). Archived funds can be restored. With no funds yet: **Start with the general fund** fills in "MSA & Musallah" and General.
  - **Tickets**: the same, without category, heading or season; no price box (Go2Pay holds price and stock). Which event uses a ticket is chosen in the event form in PR-C.
  - **Seasons** (new dashboard card): Ramadan, Eid al-Fitr, Eid al-Adha and board-added seasons, each with its rule ("Starts on 1 Ramadan, until the end of Ramadan", "On 1 Shawwal"), this year's days marked expected, confirmed or typed (no Hijri dates, D-78), and how many funds use it. The season page shows the expected days and takes the confirmed first and last day after moon sighting (clear a box to go back); a season a fund uses can't be archived.
  - **Money in** (Manage money only): filters (a season, or From and Until, the last 30 days to start; category; fund or ticket; status), a box for each zakat category ("Never added to any other total"), one **Unrestricted** box with its subtotal, no grand total, then every payment with the giver's name and note. **Download CSV** gives the same rows with notes and without names, made on request and never kept. Unfinished payments are not counted. Messages when the Go2Pay key is off, refused, too powerful, or Go2Pay can't be reached; "Only part of the orders could be read; narrow the dates" when the read ran out of time (the CSV is then refused too).
  - **Recent changes**: funds, tickets, Go2Pay links and seasons read by name, with a new **What** choice "Funds, tickets and seasons" and the labels "Go2Pay link", "Category locked" and "Season days" on highlighted changes.
  - **Help**: nine new cards with a picture each: Seasons and moon sighting, Make a Go2Pay page for a fund, … for a ticket or size, See payments, Sold one in person, Close a fund in Go2Pay, Refund in Go2Pay, If a giver chose the wrong fund, Stopping all giving.
  - **Pages → Go2Pay receipt note** (named "Leaving the site" until PR-C) has the box **Note on Go2Pay receipts** (the MSA's thank-you line, D-32). The fund and ticket editors put "(name) (category)." before it in their copy box. It starts filled in (PR-C, below); if the board empties it, the copy box says so with a link to fill it in.
- **PR6 part 3 of 3 (PR-C): the Donate page, Give links, event tickets, and the docs** ([PR6 contract](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md), WP-8, WP-9, WP-10; [ADR 0019](adr/0019-go2pay-products-made-by-the-board-payments-only-key.md), [ADR 0020](adr/0020-format-rules-product-rules-and-settings.md), [ADR 0021](adr/0021-seasons-follow-the-islamic-calendar.md)). No new database table (the events' ticket column came with PR-A's update). Visitors now see giving.
  - **/donate**: under "Choose where your gift goes" and "The fund you choose is your intention.", each fund the board shows is a paper slip with its category tag, its one sentence (zakat slips also their fixed rule line), "Closes tomorrow" with a clock in its last 3 days, and **Give ↗**. Headings appear only where the board typed one; a group with a heading ends with a thin line, and headings and slips are never centred: they sit on the left, a lone slip too (D-79, D-80). No amounts, no Hijri dates. At the bottom the fine print, including "Go2Pay and payment processing take about 5% of every payment." With no fund shown: "There are no open appeals right now." and **Contact the MSA**; if the database can't be read: "Giving details could not be loaded right now." and **Contact the MSA**.
  - **Give ↗** opens `/donate/<fund>/give` in a new tab, which goes on to that fund's Go2Pay page. A closed, archived or unknown fund shows "This has closed" with **Donate** and **Home**. No Go2Pay address is in any of our pages.
  - **Event tickets**: the event form has a new box, **Ticket (optional)**: any board member picks a ticket from **Payments → Tickets** (each ticket belongs to one event). The event page then shows **See price and buy ↗** (no price on our page) while the event is shown, not cancelled and not over, with **Add to Calendar** outlined beside it (D-82). A postponed event with no new date yet stays on the website with "new date to be announced" but its button pauses until the board sets the new date (D-81); a cancelled or past event, or a ticket taken off the event, shows "This has closed". **Payments → Tickets** says "Used by (event)".
  - **Home** "Support our community" band and **Ramadan → Sponsorship** open **/donate** while it shows funds.
  - **Privacy**: a new "Giving and tickets" paragraph (wording below).
  - **Pages → Leaving the site** is now **Pages → Go2Pay receipt note**: its unused **Notice** box is gone (the old Ramadan donate link that showed it now opens the Donate page), and the page has no Preview tab. **The receipt note starts filled in**, on the live site too: "Thank you for supporting the SGU MSA. JazakAllahu khayran. Questions: so-msa@sgu.edu. This is not a tax receipt." (it is in the PR5b page-text seed, `202610100001_page_texts.sql`, which now seeds the receipt note instead of the old notice: the project has no real data yet, so the migration is edited directly and there is no separate update.)
  - **The `DONATION_URL` setting is gone**: the Donate page lists the board's funds instead. Owner: delete `DONATION_URL` in Vercel after this release.
  - **Help**: the "Make a Go2Pay page for a ticket or size" card gains a step: pick the ticket in the event's **Ticket (optional)** box. The event-form and ticket pictures were re-taken.
  - The sandbox's Give goes to a stand-in checkout page (`/checkout/<number>`, which exists only in the sandbox) instead of Go2Pay; its **Pay** adds a payment that **Money in** shows.
  - Docs: ADRs 0019 (Go2Pay products made by the board, payments-only key), 0020 (where rules live) and 0021 (seasons follow the Islamic calendar); the owner guide's Go2Pay key section (the "Take payments only" key belongs to so-msa@sgu.edu; `off` until launch); the developer guide (a payments-only test key by default; even a test key returns real givers' details; the full-access key warning); architecture, roadmap (PR6 done except the rules audit, WP-11), Go2Pay notes.
- **PR5b part 2 of 3 (PR-B): the Preview tab shows the page itself; the last page words leave the code; verses come from the verse library** ([PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md), WP4, WP5). No database update of its own (it needs PR-A's).
  - **Pages → any page → Preview**: shows the public page drawn from what you typed (before saving), with today's real team, events, updates, newsletter issues and prayer times around it. While a required box is empty it shows the same "Please fix … before saving:" list a save would. Left out of the picture: the Qur'an verse (it has its own screen in PR-C), the **Find us on campus** box, and Home's community updates and featured notice. A photo you just chose shows after **Save page**. **Donate** and **Leaving the site** still say the picture comes with the next update (they wait for PR6's new Donate page).
  - **Contact** and **Community updates**: their headings, lines and links now come from **Pages → Contact** and **Pages → Community page** (same words as before).
  - **Sign-up forms, closed-form button:** a closed form shows its **Text while closed** instead of the button, as in the approved picture; a closed form without that text, or an open form without a link, shows no button at all (before, a made-up "… — Coming Soon" line). Every form starts closed with today's "… Coming Soon" lines, so visitors see no change until the board opens a form.
  - **Footer:** the copyright line follows the year by itself in Grenada time ("© 2026 SGU Muslim Students Association. All rights reserved.", the same words); the year is no longer stored.
  - **Qur'an verses on every page now come from the verse library** (Pages → "Verse on each page", chosen in PR-C's verse screen), never from code: Home (4 places), Prayer, Calendar and each event, Get involved, Donate, Resources, Community and each update, About, Ramadan and the footer. The "?" beside a verse also shows "Translation: Saheeh International". Since review fixes PR-3 the library starts with the 9 verses the site showed before, so every page shows its verse.
  - Not yet: the **Donate** page's opening words (eyebrow, title, lede) still come from code: PR6 rebuilds the Donate page first and this moves after it (contract WP5, tracked in the roadmap's PR6 row). Until then **Pages → Donate** is hidden from the Pages list (a save there would not change the site); it comes back with that change.
  - For developers: each page's body moved unchanged into one presentational component in its feature folder (`AboutChapters`, `PrayerBody`, `FaqSheet`, `ResourcesBody`, `GetInvolvedBody`, `CalendarBody`, `SupportSections`, `HomeBody`), each with a Storybook story in light and dark; previews in `src/features/admin/pages/previews.tsx` with live data from `preview-live.ts`. New tests: `tests/page-previews.test.ts` (no server-only module is reachable from the previews, every starter page previews, no hardcoded verse id, the closed-form rules) and three more in `tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts`.
- **PR5b part 3 of 3 (PR-C): the Qur'an verse picker, and help for Pages, sign-up forms and verses** ([PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md), WP7, WP6; [ADR 0023](adr/0023-quran-verses-picked-from-alquran-cloud.md)). No database update of its own (it needs PR-A's). No new setting. **Its verse search, saved-verse list and Archive were replaced before release by Review 3 E (a fixed verse library, below).**
  - **Pages → Qur'an verses** (a new card at the end of the Pages list, `/admin/pages/verses`), laid out as in the approved picture: **1 · Find a verse**: type a verse number (2:255, or up to 3 verses in a row such as 2:255-257) or a word in English or Arabic (patience, صبر) and click **Search**; each verse found shows its Arabic, its English meaning and "Al-Baqara 2:153 · Saheeh International", with **Use this verse**. **2 · Verses saved on the site**: the verse library, newest first, each with the places it shows on and **Archive**; archived verses fold under **Archived verses** with **Restore** (no delete). If either list cannot be loaded, "The saved verses could not be loaded. Check your connection and reload." shows in its place. **3 · Which verse shows on each page**: one choice per place (Home ×4, Prayer & Musallah, Events, Get involved, Donate, Resources, Community, About, Ramadan, Support, Bottom of every page) from the library or **No verse on this page**, with **Edit | Preview** and **Save verse choices**; **History** under it restores earlier choices.
  - **Nobody types Qur'an text.** "Use this verse" sends only the verse number; the website fetches the Arabic (Uthmani) and the meaning (Saheeh International) from api.alquran.cloud itself and keeps them exactly as given. A verse already in the library is reused (an archived one comes back), never stored twice. A saved verse's text can never be changed; a wrong one is archived. The public pages read only our database: if the Qur'an text service is down, only the search stops ("Could not reach the Qur’an text service …"). A word search takes at most about 15 seconds: if the service is slow or refuses part of it, the verses found so far are shown.
  - Places that still name a verse not in the library (today's starter numbers, such as 13:28 for Home) show "13:28 (no longer offered)" in their choice and no verse on the page until a verse is picked (see "Starter Qur'an verses" under "You or the founder must decide").
  - **Help** (`/admin/help`): three new cards with a picture each: **Edit a page**, **Sign-up forms and Google Forms** (the how-to's five steps, plus Google's tips 1 "send each person a copy" and 2 "email the MSA Gmail for each answer"; tip 4 "Full?" is step 4 and tip 5 "Link to Sheets" step 5) and **Qur’an verses**. The same two tips now also show under the how-to beside **Pages → Sign-up forms**. **Recent changes** card: page text and Qur’an verses are listed among what it records. Dashboard **Pages** card: "…, sign-up forms, FAQ and Qur’an verses →".
  - For developers: `ContentEditor` gained `listVerses`, `addVerse`, `archiveVerse`, `restoreVerse` (Supabase and sandbox); actions in `src/features/admin/pages/verse-actions.ts` (`searchVersesAction`, `saveVerseAction`, archive and restore); the search box is read by `verseQuery`. Docs: ADR 0023, `docs/architecture.md` (verse picker flow), `content/README.md`, the board guide, the roadmap. New tests: four in `tests/quran.test.ts` (including the search's pacing and the Supabase verse library on a stub client; `createSupabaseContentEditor` takes an optional client for this) and one in `tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts` (plus the verse screen in its axe check); the help pictures `pages`, `forms`, `verses`.
- **Review fixes PR-1: the site looks like the real site** (review fixes plan of 2026-10-05, PR-1; amendments of 2026-10-05 in the [data-providers](contracts/data-providers.md), [restyle-public](contracts/restyle-public.md), [PR5a](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md) and [PR5b](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) contracts). One small database update (Settings below).
  - **No demo labels anywhere.** The sandbox (`npm run demo`) and staging look exactly like the live site: the "DESIGN DEMO" banner, the sandbox paragraph on **/admin** (the form itself is unchanged; the sandbox still signs in with `admin` / `admin`), "Illustrative regional calculation — not approved" (Home now shows the real calculation line), "Pending verification" on sample events, "Placeholder entry" on newsletters, the "Photograph to add" boxes on community updates, and the dashboard's "Sandbox: …" lines (Payments, invites, password reset) are gone. Invites and resets in the sandbox answer like the live site (nothing is sent). Next's "N" button no longer shows on `npm run dev` / `npm run demo`.
  - **Sample content reads like normal club news**: events with real places (Charter Hall, the Musallah, Grand Anse Beach), urgent notices "Dhuhr is in Charter Hall today" and "No water at the Musallah this week", a team of made-up people with the founder's roles (from review fixes PR-2: Omar Haddad, President; Maryam Siddiqui, Treasurer; Karim Mansour, Faculty advisor; …), shared logins "MSA Gmail" and "Instagram", and payments from made-up givers (no "Test" badge).
  - **The logo is drawn inside the page**, so it takes the theme's colours: the green ink seal in light mode, the lighter seal in dark mode (before, some browsers drew a black disc). Header, footer, error page and Donate letter. The drawing is in each page once, however many seals it shows.
  - **Urgent band:** the visible "1 of 2" is gone; the dots and arrows stay. Screen readers still hear "1 of 2".
  - **Resources:** visitors see only resources that have a link and are not archived. **Pages → Resources** has an **Archived** switch on each resource ("Archived resources are hidden from visitors and stay here."), and the **Link** hint says a resource without a link is hidden. The box "Text while there is no link" is gone (nothing showed it any more). The starter resources lose the four "coming soon" entries without a link, and the two marked "Archive" (the April 2026 newsletter and "MSA Community Events") start archived. The search box's label is now "Hint in the search box".
  - **Home prayer times** are one straight table: **Prayer | Begins | Iqamah**, the same headings as the Prayer page, with fixed time columns, so every column lines up and Maghrib's time no longer touches Isha. The next prayer's row is bold.
  - **Dashboard forms line up:** in a row of boxes the labels stay at the top and drop-downs are the same height as text boxes (for example **People → Add a person**: Role title and Section; the Show button in the Payments and Recent changes filters).
  - **Events:** the term filter lists T1, T2, T3, T4 and T5 (T3 was missing).
  - The help pictures (`/admin/help`) were re-taken without the banner and with the new sample names.
- **Review fixes PR-3: one musallah room, and Qur'an verses again, as short parts on one line** ([PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md) D-16, [PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) D-24 amendment, 2026-10-05).
  - **No more Jumu'ah room.** The musallah is one room, so **Prayer & iqamah** no longer has a **Room** box under **2. Jumu’ah (every Friday)**, and a Friday change offers **As usual**, **No Jumu’ah this week** or **Different time** (khutbah and iqamah only). The public Jumu'ah card, the home page's Jumu'ah line and "Usually: …" show only the times, and the prefilled urgent notice reads "Jumu’ah this Friday (16 Oct) moves to 1:15 PM". Rooms saved earlier are dropped by the database update (Settings below); older History versions still restore (without the room). Help, the "Show me how" tour and the help pictures say the same.
  - **Replaced before release by Review 3 E (below).** **The verses are back.** The verse library starts with the 9 verses the site showed before (13:28 Home, 5:2, 49:10, 3:92, 4:103, 96:1, 2:183, 94:5–6, 20:114 in the footer), each stored as its whole verse from the Qur'an text service with the words the site showed as its **part**, so every page shows its verse again. The "?" still shows the meaning of the part and the verse number, such as "Qur’an 5:2 (Al-Maaida)". This lifts the PR5b "starter verses" release block.
  - **Replaced before release by Review 3 E (below).** **Choose the words to show.** In **Pages → Qur’an verses**, each saved verse has **Choose the words to show**: tap the first and last word of the Arabic and of the meaning, see the preview and click **Save these words**; **Show the whole verse** puts it back. A part is always made of the verse's own words, in order (checked by the website and by the database); nothing is typed. A verse showing a part is marked "Part of the verse" in the list.
  - **One line.** Every verse and dhikr is now sized to fill one line, getting smaller down to a readable size before it may wrap; only then it takes two even lines, with no empty space left for lines it does not use (the home dhikr was on three lines). The verse preview warns "Too long for one line. Choose a shorter part." when a verse would not fit on one line in the narrowest place a verse shows (the 18rem note beside a page heading), for example the whole of 5:2, or 4:103 and 94:5–6 as the site shows them today (they still fit one line on the Prayer and Support pages of a phone).
  - For developers: `src/lib/quran/excerpt.ts` (the word-for-word rule, mirrored by `quran_excerpt_is_part` in SQL), `ContentEditor.setVerseExcerpt`, `saveVerseWordsAction`, `VerseWords`, `VersePreview`; `ArabicLine` is a client component that measures the text once (`--arabic-em`) while the stylesheet does the sizing (`arabicFit.ts`); the starting library is `src/lib/quran/starter-verses.json`, fetched once by `npx tsx scripts/page-text-seed.mjs --fetch-verses`. New tests: `tests/verse-excerpts-migration.test.ts`, plus cases in `tests/quran.test.ts`, `tests/reminders.test.ts` and `tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts`.

- **Review fixes PR-2: team groups, and drag and drop instead of typed order numbers** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), amendment 2026-10-05; the review-fixes plan, PR-2):
  - **Nobody types an order number any more.** On **People** (each section), **Edit sections**, **Payments → Funds** and **Tickets**, and **New board term → Edit the steps**, every item has a handle ⠿ on the left (drag it with the mouse or a finger) and two arrows on the right (**Move up** / **Move down**, for the keyboard and screen readers). After a change, a bar "The order changed. Save it to keep it." with **Save order** and **Undo** stays at the bottom of the screen; **Save order** saves the whole list at once ("Order saved."). If someone else changed the list meanwhile, nothing is saved and the screen says to reload. A list with one item shows no handle or arrows (its row still lines up with the lists around it).
  - The **Order number** boxes are gone from **Add a person** / **Edit**, **Edit sections**, the fund and ticket forms. A new person goes at the end of their section (also when moved to another section); a new section, fund, ticket or step goes at the end of its list.
  - **Funds** are listed in the Donate page's groups (Zakat al-Fitr, Zakat al-Mal, Funds with a season, Sadaqah, General), each group with its own heading and its own order, because zakat funds always come first on the Donate page.
  - **Pages**: lists inside a page's text (such as the FAQ questions) use the same handle and arrows instead of the **Move up** / **Move down** buttons; **Save page** saves the order as before.
  - **Team groups** (the founder's structure): the starter sections are renamed **Executive board**, **General board** and **Advisors/Faculty** (only sections still called Board, Musallah team and Advisors; a section the board already renamed is left alone). On **Add a person**, **Show on public page?** now starts as **Yes** when the section is the Executive board and **No** for the others (it follows the section until you pick Yes or No yourself; Yes still needs the listing consent). Each section has a new tick box on **Edit sections**, "New people in this section start as “Show on public page: Yes”", ticked only for the Executive board.
  - **Role title** suggests the founder's titles (President, Vice President (Brothers), Vice President (Sisters), Treasurer, Secretary, Musallah coordinator, Social media manager, Brothers coordinator, Sisters coordinator, T1 rep to T5 rep, Pre-med term rep) and every title already used in People. Any other title can still be typed.
  - The sandbox team uses these roles with made-up names: the Executive board (Omar Haddad, President, who is also the sandbox login; Bilal Ahmed; Aisha Rahman; Maryam Siddiqui; Hamza Ali) is on the About page; the General board (six people) and the faculty advisor are not.
  - **Help** and **Show me how**: the People, Team list, Funds, Tickets, New board term and Edit a page cards say how to drag and save; the People tour has a new step **Change the order**. Help pictures re-taken for People, Team list, Inviting and access, Recent changes, Funds, Tickets and New board term.
  - For developers: one shared `SortableList` (`src/shared/ui`, Motion's `Reorder`, no new package) and `OrderedList` (`src/features/admin`) with `saveOrderAction`; a new port `OrderEditor` (`supabase`, `sample`) calling the database function `save_order` (every row or none, rows locked then versions checked so two saves at the same moment never overwrite each other, RLS decides, rows already in place are not touched); migration `202610120001_team_groups_order.sql` (below); `moveHandoverStepAction` removed.

- **Review fixes PR-C: the logo is whole again, and pages arrive faster** (engineering; the review-2 plan, PR-C; [architecture](architecture.md#8-caching-and-streaming-review-fixes-pr-c), amendments 2026-10-05 in the [PR5a](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md), [PR5b](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md), [restyle-public](contracts/restyle-public.md), [data-providers](contracts/data-providers.md), [PR6](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md), [PR7](contracts/pr7-single-pin-map.md), [PR3](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md) and [daily-check](contracts/daily-check-notifications.md) contracts):
  - **The logo is no longer cut off** at the top and the left (its drawing box was applied twice). It is the same seal, green in light mode and light in dark mode, in the header, the footer, the Donate page and the error pages.
  - **Smaller pages.** The seal's drawing is now one small file the browser downloads once and keeps, instead of a copy inside every page: each page is about 35 KB lighter to download.
  - **The page arrives in two parts on one load.** The menu, the footer and each page's text are prepared ahead of time and sent at once; what must be fresh on every visit (events, news, the team, funds and tickets, today's prayer times, the urgent band) follows straight after on the same load. Page text, Qur'an verses, prayer settings and the musallah pin are kept ready until a board member saves a change; a save still shows on the next page load. Urgent notices still end at their exact minute.
  - **Less animation code.** Pages load only the animation parts they use (no drag or layout animations outside the dashboard's lists).
  - **The demo you review runs like the live site:** `npm run demo:review -- -p 4100` builds the sandbox the way the live site is built, then serves it (a developer runs it; Ctrl+C and run it again to start from the sample data). The development demo (`npm run demo`) prepares each page the first time it is opened, which is why pages felt slow.
  - Measured with Lighthouse (phone settings, the production build of the sandbox, median of 3; before → after): Home score 91 → 93, largest text shown at 3.46 s → 3.18 s, first text at 1.66 s → 1.51 s, the page itself 61 KB → 26 KB; Prayer 91 → 93 (3.46 s → 3.17 s); About 89 → 91 (3.61 s → 3.46 s); Calendar 90 → 90 (3.61 s → 3.61 s, first text 1.66 s → 1.51 s). The sandbox's data is in memory, so its server answers in a few milliseconds either way; on the live site the menu and the page text no longer wait for the database on each visit.
  - Two things you may notice: on a page with fresh data the "Loading the latest information…" outline can show for a moment under the menu while the rest fills in (before, the whole page waited and then appeared). And **Back** returns to one of the last 3 pages as you left it (scroll position, opened sections, typed text in a dashboard form), because the browser keeps them; a dashboard page opened by a link starts again from the saved text, as before.
  - For developers: Cache Components are on (`cacheComponents: true`); `export const dynamic` is gone from every route; `"use cache"` + `cacheTag` on page text (`getDocument`), `getVerses`, `getPrayerSettings` and `musallahPin`, a minute's cache on announcement rows; `requestNow()` (`src/lib/runtime/public-cache.ts`) marks per-visit reads; every action calls `publicDataChanged()` (`src/features/admin/public-refresh.ts`); `/admin` and the address-based pages set `instant = false`; the seal is `public/images/brand/seal-sprite.svg` (from `node scripts/seal-art.mjs`), cached for a year; `LazyMotion` with `domAnimation` and `m` from `motion/react-m`; off Vercel Production pages are kept by `src/lib/runtime/local-page-cache.mjs`; `npm run demo:review`; every `/admin` page is exported through `boardPage` (`src/features/admin/page-state/`), which keys it on `useRouter().bfcacheId` so a link starts it over and **Back** keeps it; `CheckedForm` keeps its unsaved-changes baseline from the first mount; the tour counts only shown elements (`src/features/admin/tour/shown.ts`). Tests: `tests/seal-art.test.ts`, the `boardPage` check in `tests/runtime.test.ts`, `tests/pr5a-editors.test.ts`, unit-test stubs for `next/cache` and `next/server` in `tests/support/`, and visibility-aware e2e locators for the pages the browser keeps.
- **Review 2, PR-A: events and forms that guide you** ([PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md) D-20 amendment, [PR4 contract](contracts/pr4-dashboard-simplification-onboarding.md) D-7 and D-10 amendments, 2026-10-05):
  - **Event dates:** an event has a **Start date** and, optional, a **Start time** (empty means all day), then an optional **End date** and **End time** (an empty end date means it ends the same day). An end before the start is refused on the box. A published event no longer needs a start time. The old "an end earlier than the start means the next day" rule is gone: for 10 PM to 3 AM, set the End date to the next day. Visitors see "All day", "5:00 PM – 7:00 PM", "10:00 PM – Sun 11 Oct, 3:00 AM" or "Until Mon 12 Oct" on the cards, and the same in the event page's date line.
  - **Is it still happening?** has **Scheduled**, **Postponed** (new date not known yet: "Postponed: new date to be announced") and **Cancelled**. "New date is known" and "Postponed from …" are gone: a known new date is simply the new Start date.
  - **Also post an announcement?** When an event that is on the website gets a new date, or is cancelled, Save first asks this (Yes or No). Yes posts an urgent notice on every page, with its text ("Beach clean-up moves to Sat 31 Oct" or "Cancelled: Beach clean-up") and **Hide at** (the end of the event's day) filled in and changeable, like the Jumu'ah notice.
  - **Every board form checks its boxes as you type:** once you start on a box, and when you leave it, a wrong box gets a red outline and a short line under it saying why. Save with a red box shows one line at the top, "Some boxes need fixing.", jumps to the first red box and saves nothing. The website still checks again when saving; a problem only it can find (for example "someone else saved first", or a Go2Pay page already used) shows in the same place. Events, announcements, news, newsletters, Prayer & iqamah, the musallah pin, People, team sections, Funds, Tickets, Seasons, Pages and New board term.
  - **Leaving with unsaved changes asks first:** closing or reloading the tab gets the browser's own warning, and clicking a dashboard link opens **Leave without saving?** with **Save**, **Discard** and **Keep editing**. Save does not save while a box is red; after a save it opens the page you clicked.
  - **Show me how** appears only on pages where its tour has something to point at.
  - **Add to Calendar** depends on the device: iPhone, iPad and Mac open the event straight in Apple Calendar; Android opens Google Calendar with the event filled in; other computers get a small menu: **Google Calendar**, **Outlook**, **Apple or other (file)**. Without JavaScript it gives the file. The file now comes from the event's own address (`/calendar/<event>/ics`), so it always has the latest details.
  - Help (Events, Cancel or postpone an event, Create, People, When something goes wrong), the Edit item tour and the "Events" and "When something goes wrong" help pictures say the same.
  - For developers: `src/features/admin/form-check/` (`CheckedForm`, `FieldError`, `problems.ts`, `unsaved.ts`, `UnsavedChangesGuard`); one pure parse per form shared by the action and the browser (`content-form.ts`, `prayer-form.ts`, `person-form.ts`, `handover/handover-forms.ts`, `pages/page-form.ts`, `payment-forms.ts`); `EditorState.fields` replaces `issues`, `problemsOf` replaces `issuesOf`; `src/lib/community/event-notice.ts`, `answeredNotice` in `jumuah-notices.ts`; `src/lib/calendar/targets.ts` and the route `src/app/calendar/[eventId]/ics/route.ts`. Database: `events.end_date` and the new end-after-start checks in `202609090001_community.sql`; `moved_from`, its check and the overnight block removed from `202610070001_notices_events_handover.sql` (edited in place: no hosted data). Sandbox: "Welcome dinner" is no longer postponed, "Night of prayer" ends the next day, and a new all-day "Islamic Awareness Week" runs over three days.
- **Review 3, A: Home top tightened.** On a laptop or larger screen the Home headline ("FAITH. KNOWLEDGE. COMMUNITY.") now starts level with the dhikr at the top, instead of about 300 px lower; the top margin is 4rem instead of 6rem. Phones look the same. The dhikr of the hour is about a sixth smaller (for example 34 px → 28 px on a laptop, 32 px → 27 px on a phone) and still on one line. Under the map in **Find us on campus** (Prayer, Home, Contact), the line "The map loads from OpenFreeMap as you scroll to it. The written directions work without it." is gone; the Privacy page still explains the map.

- **Review 2, PR-B: People in groups, a fixed Executive board, no consent records, and no move arrows** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md), amendment of 2026-10-05 "review 2"; [ADR 0025](adr/0025-team-list-without-consent-records.md); the review-2 plan, PR-B):
  - **"Section" is now "Group" everywhere**: the **Group** box on a person, **Edit groups** (at `/admin/people/groups`, page title **Team groups**), **Add a group**, **Archive this group**, **Archived groups**, Help, the tour, Recent changes ("team group", "Group") and the privacy page.
  - **The Executive board is listed by role**, always in this order: President, Vice President (Brothers), Vice President (Sisters), Treasurer, Secretary, on **People** and on **/about**. It has no ⠿ handle (nothing to drag) and a line under its name: "Listed by role, President first. Change someone's role to change their place." In that group **Role** is a list of those five (required); in the other groups **Role title (optional)** stays a free box with suggestions. Two people with the same role are listed by the order they were added, then by name.
  - **The Executive board and the General board are always kept**: on **Edit groups** they have no name box and no **Archive**, only the "New people …" tick box and "This group is always kept: it cannot be renamed or archived." The board can add its own groups (Advisors/Faculty is one), rename them, put the groups in order, archive an empty one, restore it, and now **delete it permanently** from **Archived groups** once nobody, archived or not, is in it (board members who can manage people; type DELETE).
  - **Move someone to another group** with the **Group** box on their page (they go to the end of that group). Dragging only reorders inside a group.
  - **No consent records any more** (your answer of 2026-10-05): the Listing consent and Photo consent dates and "How … was given" boxes are gone from the person form, the database and the help. **Show on public page: Yes** lists the person; their photo shows whenever one is uploaded. **Remove photo** (was "Remove photo (consent withdrawn)") still deletes a photo, also for an archived person ("Photo removed."). The **Team list** help card (was "Team list and consent") and the privacy page say the same.
  - **No move arrows on dragged lists** (People, groups, Funds, Tickets, New board term steps, lists inside Pages): only the handle ⠿. The handle can also be reached with Tab; the up and down arrow keys (and Home / End) then move the item, and a screen reader hears "Move (name). Press the up or down arrow key to move it." and, after a move, "(name) is now 3 of 7.". The hint under each list is now "Drag ⠿ to change the order."
  - The People and Group forms use the shared red-box check from PR-A: for example Save in the Executive board with no Role gives "Some boxes need fixing." and "Choose one of the Executive board roles." under **Role**; leaving the page with changes asks **Save** / **Discard** / **Keep editing**.
  - The site owner's "add an admin" step in the Supabase SQL editor (`owner_add_admin`) now puts the new person in the **General board**, hidden, because the Executive board needs one of its roles: the board then moves them with **Group** and **Role**. The hand-over starter step is now "Update the team list" ("Role titles, order, groups and “Show on public page?”.").
  - For developers: `people_sections.kind` (`executive`, `general` or null) with the trigger `always_kept` (SGU13) and the RLS insert rule `kind is null`; `executive_role_rank()` and the trigger `executive_role` (SGU12) on `people`; `public_team()` orders the Executive board by role and returns `photo_path` whenever set; the four consent columns, their checks and the consent part of `archived_read_only` are gone (all edited in place in `202610050001_people_access.sql`, `202610050002_activity_log.sql`, `202610050003_archive.sql`, `202610070001_notices_events_handover.sql` and `202610120001_team_groups_order.sql`, whose rename and `owner_add_admin` steps are folded into the first file). `EXECUTIVE_ROLES`, `inGroupOrder`, `personFormSchema(executiveGroupId)` in `src/lib/community/people.ts`; `PeopleEditor.deleteSection`; `GroupsEditor.tsx` (was `SectionsEditor.tsx`); `SortableList` without arrow buttons (the handle is a button with the arrow keys). Help pictures re-taken.
- **Review 3, B: show, hide and move the sections of each page; Pages for the Executive board only** ([PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) and [PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md) amendments of 2026-10-06; [ADR 0027](adr/0027-pages-edited-by-the-executive-board.md); the review-3 plan, section B):
  - **Only people in the Executive board group can change Pages** (every page's words, photos and sections, Sign-up forms, Site-wide, Qur’an verses, the Go2Pay receipt note). There is no tick-box: it follows **People**. Move someone into the Executive board to let them edit pages; move them out (or turn their login off) and it stops at once. Everyone else no longer sees the **Pages** card, and a Pages address sends them to the dashboard. While no Executive board member has a login, the dashboard says **"Nobody can edit pages: add an executive"** with **Open People →**. The database itself refuses a page change from anyone else.
  - **A page is now a list of its sections** (Home, About, Prayer & Musallah, Events, Community, Resources, Get involved, FAQ, Contact, Support): the top of the page first (always first, with **Edit**), then every other section with **Show this section on the page**, a drag handle ⠿ and **Edit**. **Edit** opens that section's boxes under it (**Close** folds them away). Untick the box to hide a section: its words are kept, and its required boxes may stay empty while it is hidden (a photo or a choice stays required). Drag ⠿ (or Tab to it and use the arrow keys) to move it. **Save page** saves the order and what is shown with the words; **History → Restore** puts them back too, and Recent changes lists "Sections: This week, Ways to help (hidden), …".
  - The list is the whole page, so it also has the parts whose words are kept elsewhere: Jumu’ah and the four Musallah directions sections on **Prayer & Musallah** (**Edit** opens their boxes right there since the dashboard polish below), the **Newsletter archive** on Resources, **Common questions** (the FAQ's "On Home") on Home, **Community updates** on Home ("Its content comes from Announcements and Community news."), and **Your first visit** and **Support our community** on Home ("Its words are fixed: ask the site owner to change them.", no Edit). On Musallah directions, Jumu’ah, Newsletter archive and FAQ, a section another page shows says "Shown on the Prayer & Musallah page." (or "Hidden on …") with **Show, hide or move it on Prayer & Musallah**.
  - **The public pages follow**: sections in the saved order, hidden ones gone. Parts drawn together stay together when next to each other (today's times and Jumu’ah side by side, the photos of the way inside the directions band, the Contact card beside the welcome when it comes first); apart, each shows on its own.
  - **Preview shows the whole page** as visitors will see it (including the map, the first-visit box, community updates and the page's verse), without the hidden sections, in the new order, scrolled to the section you opened last.
  - A save with a red box opens its section and marks it "Needs fixing". Showing again a section of another page text whose required boxes were emptied while hidden turns those boxes red (since the dashboard polish below; a History restore that would show it says "Musallah helpers: fill in its required boxes before showing it.").
  - Pages grouping: the Resources search boxes are now in the **Resources** section; Get involved's **Introduction** box is in **Top of the page**; "No events" and "No updates" are now **Events list** and **Updates list**.
  - Help: a new card **What you can change** (who changes what, and what only the site owner changes) and **Edit a page** rewritten for the list; the **Edit a page** picture re-taken.
