  - Board members outside the Executive board meet no link that lands on the dashboard: the **Pages**, **Sign-up forms** and **Qur’an verses** help cards say "Only people in the Executive board can change pages." instead of **Open …**, and a fund or ticket whose receipt note is empty asks them to have an Executive board member add it.
  - On a page's **History**, the restore and its preview skip the required boxes of sections hidden in that version or on the page that shows them, like a save.
  - For developers: `src/lib/content/page-layouts.ts` (`pageLayouts`, `shownIds`, `hiddenSections`, `layoutOwner`), `sections` in the ten page schemas (`pageSectionsSchema`), the YAML and the seed; `PageSection` (`src/shared/layout/PageSection.tsx`, `display: contents`, `data-page-section`); hidden-aware `mergePageText` / `pageTextProblems` / `pageTextSaveSchema` / `previewPageText`, `sectionProblems`, `sectionOfPath`, `pageSectionTitle`; `PageTextEditor` as a list, `previewSlots`, `page-editor.ts` (`pageEditorRefusal`, `layoutOwners`), `requirePageEditor`; `AdminIdentity.canEditPages`; `editsPages` / `nobodyEditsPages`; `CheckedForm` `onProblems`; `SortableList` hides only its own handle when it has one item (a list inside it keeps its handles). Database: `can_edit_pages()` and the policies `executive_insert` / `executive_update` on `page_texts`, and `sections` in the seeded documents, edited in place in `202610100001_page_texts.sql` (no hosted data). Tests: `tests/page-sections.test.ts`, `tests/page-texts-migration.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/page-sections.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts`.
- **Dashboard polish after review 3** (found on the screenshots of `integration` at `dbb89ac`; [PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) amendment "dashboard polish"):
  - **Every section is edited on the page that shows it.** On **Pages → Prayer & Musallah**, **Edit** on Jumu’ah, Directions, Musallah information, Musallah helpers or Photos of the way now opens its boxes right there, like the page's own sections (the same on Home for **Common questions** and on Resources for **Newsletter archive**). The note "Its words are edited on Musallah directions." and the jump to another Pages screen are gone. The words are still stored once: **Pages → Musallah directions**, **Jumu’ah**, **FAQ** and **Newsletter archive** show and edit the same words. **Save page** saves the page and those words together, all or nothing; a box that needs fixing turns red on the page you are on, and the Preview tab shows what you typed in them.
  - **History**: Restore on a page still puts back only that page's own words, order and shown/hidden sections. The words of a section kept with another page text are undone in that text's History: under **History** a line says so ("Restore here leaves the words of Directions, Musallah information, Musallah helpers and Photos of the way as they are: they are kept with Musallah directions, which has its own History.") with a link **History of Musallah directions**. (Why: the Jumu’ah heading also shows on Home, so restoring it from Prayer & Musallah would change Home without saying so.)

(it said required boxes are "listed at the top", which stopped being true in review 2 PR-A). The **Edit a page** help card says the same, and says sections kept with another page text are edited and saved right on the page.
  - **Dashboard pages start higher**: the empty band of about 150 px above the breadcrumb on every dashboard page is gone on a computer; the top now matches the public pages after review 3 A (phones unchanged in feel).
  - **Event page Status**: the side panel's **Status** said "Confirmed" for every event, even a postponed or cancelled one. It now says "Scheduled", "Postponed: new date to be announced" or "Cancelled: (reason)", the same words as the line under the event's title (one rule).

Tests: `tests/page-texts-migration.test.ts` (`save_page_texts`), `tests/page-sections.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/page-sections.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/notices.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/giving.spec.ts`.

- **Review 3, D: History and Restore say exactly what they do** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md) and [PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md), amendments of 2026-10-06 "review 3 D"; the review-3 plan, D):
  - On every History list (each page in **Pages**, **Pages → Qur’an verses**, and **Prayer & iqamah**), **Restore the version before this change** no longer restores at once. A box opens first: "This puts “About” back to how it was on Oct 5, 2026, 2:14 PM. The 3 changes made to it after that will be replaced. Nothing else on the website changes. You can undo this from History." Under it, **This version** shows that version: for a page, the page itself as it would look (today's photos stay, as before); for prayer, each box of Prayer & iqamah with that version's answer. Then **Restore** or **Cancel**.
  - The date is when that version was saved; the number counts the change you picked and every newer one.
  - A version that today's save rules would refuse (for example a page box that is now required, or prayer settings that no longer give correct times) is checked before the box opens: the box then says why ("This earlier version cannot be restored: …", the refusal Restore already gave) and offers only **Cancel**.
  - A restore never deletes anything. It is added as a new change, headed "Restored page text: About" or "Restored prayer settings", with the line "Restored to the version of Oct 5, 2026, 2:14 PM", in History and in **Recent changes**. To undo a restore, restore the version before it.
  - The History intro on both screens now reads the same, and the **Pages** and **Prayer history** help cards describe the box; the Prayer history help picture now shows the box open after one change.
  - Seasons have no History list (only **Restore** of an archived season, which brings it back unchanged), so nothing changes there (question below).
  - For developers: `page_texts.restored_from` and `prayer_settings.restored_from` (time of the version put back; null after any other save), edited in place in `202610100001_page_texts.sql` and `202609090001_community.sql`; the shared `VersionHistory` + `RestoreVersion` (`src/features/admin/history/`), wording in `systemText.history`; `historyList`, `versionBefore` and `restoredVersion` in `src/lib/community/activity.ts`; the sandbox dates its starting page text and prayer settings by its start time; `restoredPageText` applies the save rules, so the preview and the restore refuse alike; the Prayer & iqamah box names (`prayerBoxNames`) and Hijri choices (`hijriChoices`) are one list in `schema.ts` for the form, its messages and the preview.

- **Review 3, E: Qur'an verses from a fixed library of short whole verses** ([PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) D-24 amendment of 2026-10-06, [data-providers contract](contracts/data-providers.md) amendment, [ADR 0026](adr/0026-fixed-quran-verse-library.md), which replaces ADR 0023; the review-3 plan, section E):
  - **The site keeps its own verse library:** every whole verse of the Qur'an that fits on one line where a verse shows, 1655 verses, each with its Arabic (Uthmani) and English meaning (Saheeh International) exactly as api.alquran.cloud published them. "Fits on one line" is the same rule the site sizes Arabic by: at most 29 letters, spaces and signs (harakat not counted), about 11.5 times the text size, the width of the narrowest place a verse shows (the 18rem note beside a page heading). The library never changes, and the website never contacts the Qur'an service any more (not from the dashboard either).
  - **No more parts of verses.** Every verse shows whole. **Choose the words to show**, "Part of the verse", the "Too long for one line" warning, the live search (**Find a verse**, **Search**), **Verses saved on the site**, **Archive** and **Restore** for verses are gone.
  - **Pages → Qur’an verses** is now one list, **Verse on each page**: each place (Home ×4, Prayer & Musallah, Events, Get involved, Donate, Resources, Community, About, Ramadan, Support, Bottom of every page) shows its verse in Arabic with its meaning and name, and two buttons: **Change verse** (or **Choose a verse** when the place is empty) and **No verse**. **Change verse** opens a search inside the library: type a verse number (94:5), a surah number (94) or a word in English or Arabic (patience, صبر; Arabic without harakat is fine); matching verses show as you type (at most 20, with how many there are), each with **Use this verse**. A number the library does not have says why ("2:255 is not in the library: it is too long for one line, or there is no such verse."). **Save verse choices**, the **Preview** tab and **History** work as before.
  - **New starter verses** (whole, short, pre-filled for you to approve; see "You or the founder must decide"): Home under the welcome 1:5, beside events 99:7, beside community updates 51:55, beside giving 92:5; Prayer & Musallah 23:2; Events 99:7; Get involved 55:60; Donate 92:18; Resources 96:1; Community 51:55; About 23:1; Ramadan 97:3; Support 94:5; bottom of every page 96:5.
  - The dhikr of the hour and the tasbih are unchanged (their own fixed list). **Recent changes** no longer lists "Qur'an verse" entries (the library never changes; verse choices still show as changes to "Verse on each page"). The Help card **Qur’an verses** and its picture describe the new steps.
  - For developers: the library is `src/lib/quran/verse-library.json`, fetched once by `npx tsx scripts/page-text-seed.mjs --fetch-verses` (two requests, whole Qur'an) and turned into the insert in `202610130001_one_room_verse_library.sql` by `--verses`; `quran_verses` is now `surah`, `ayah`, generated `reference`, `surah_name`, `arabic`, `meaning`, select only (edited in place in `202610100001_page_texts.sql`). `ContentProvider.getVerse(reference)` and `getVerseLibrary()` (1000 rows a page) replace `getVerses`; both cached with no tag. Removed: `QuranSource` and its adapters, wiring `quran`, the `ContentEditor` verse methods, `verse-actions.ts`, `VersePicker`, `VerseLibrary`, `VerseWords`, `excerpt.ts`, `starter-verses.json`, SGU11 and the `verses` list in `content/reminders.yaml`. New: `VerseChooser`, `src/lib/quran/search.ts` (`searchVerses`, `plainArabic`), `src/lib/quran/verse.ts`. Tests: `tests/quran.test.ts`, `tests/verse-library-migration.test.ts` (was `verse-excerpts-migration`), the library case in `tests/page-texts-migration.test.ts`, and one rewritten in `tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts`.

- **One board handbook** (`handover/SGU-MSA-Website-Handbook.docx`, a Word file of 16 pages). It is now the only place for board how-to, accounts and emergency steps: who can do what (with what account holders may and must never do in Supabase, Vercel, Google and Go2Pay), everyday tasks, money with Go2Pay, sign-up forms, a new board term, and accounts and emergencies (the locked Gmail, recovery email so-msa@sgu.edu, backup codes, the two app passwords and where each goes, adding the first admin, the site is down). The board owns it and edits it in Word or Google Docs. `docs/guides/board.md` now only points to it; the README, AGENTS.md and the developer and owner guides link to it. Its steps match what is now in `integration`: the Photos screen ("Choose from uploaded photos", "Space used: … of 1 GB", warnings at 80% and 95%, Delete only for files nothing uses), daily check part 2 (an email only when something needs fixing, at most 2 per problem, one email a night), season pages and the Merch screen, "Manage people" and "Manage money" for the Executive board only, shared sections, and one person per Executive board role ("Make (name) (role) instead?", PR #94; `owner_add_admin` gives the first free role). The new board term now hands each role over one at a time and gives "Manage people" to each new executive right after.
- **The dashboard's Help page is now short** ([ADR 0028](adr/0028-board-how-to-in-one-handbook.md), final plan B: one place per topic). Dashboard → **Read the board editing guide** opens **Help**, which says the step-by-step help is in the **SGU MSA Website Handbook** (the president keeps it) and lists the six **Show me how** tours (The dashboard; Add an event or an announcement; Prayer & iqamah; People and logins; New board term; Recent changes), each opening its screen with the tour running. The long help cards and their 64 pictures are gone, with the links into them ("Help: See payments" on Payments, "Help: Make a Go2Pay page for a fund / for a ticket or size" on the fund and ticket forms; their numbered steps stay). The dashboard tour's **Help** step now reads "Where to find the board handbook, and every “Show me how” tour. “Show me how” at the top of a page replays its tour." For developers: removed `src/features/admin/help-pictures.ts`, `public/admin-help/`, `scripts/help-pictures.mjs`, `npm run help:pictures`, `tests/help-pictures.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/help-pictures.spec.ts` and the `data-tour` marks only those pictures used (`funds-list`, `flag-*`); new `tourPages` in `src/features/admin/tour/steps.ts`.
- **One clean database baseline** (final plan A5). The 14 database files in `supabase/migrations/` are folded into four, run in order: `202610150001_schema.sql` (every table, rule, function, trigger and file-storage rule), `202610150002_starter_data.sql` (team groups, the prayer settings row, the musallah pin, hand-over steps, seasons with Ramadan's page, the three merch collections, all page text; no people or sample data), `202610150003_quran_verses.sql` (the 1655-verse library) and `202610150004_recent_changes.sql` (the Recent changes triggers, last so the starter rows are not listed). No leftover upgrade steps (backfills, renames, room clean-ups, the resources fix-up) remain. Proof: the old 14 files and the new four were each applied to a throwaway Postgres 17 database (the `npm run test:db` image) and compared: the `pg_dump --schema-only` output and every row of every table (and the storage bucket) are identical, except two intended differences: the events "Google Forms only" rule is fully checked from the start (before, it was `NOT VALID` to spare old rows, and a clean database has none), and Recent changes starts empty (before, one technical entry was logged when a later file updated the Executive board group). Staging and production are **wiped and reinstalled** with the new Settings steps ("Supabase: wipe and install the clean database"), which were tested the same way: the wipe leaves nothing behind and a wiped-then-installed database matches a fresh one. For developers: tests that named old files now read `202610150001_schema.sql` or run all four files; the database suite's first admin is added with `owner_add_admin` (no backfill).
- **"Manage people" and "Manage money" are for the Executive board only** ([PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md) amendment of 2026-10-06; user decision 2026-10-06):
  - On a person outside the Executive board, the two tick boxes **Manage people** and **Manage money** are greyed out, with "Only Executive board members can have Manage people or Manage money." once under **Permissions**, also when sending an invite. The database refuses it too, and an invite with either ticked for someone outside the Executive board (an old screen) is refused before any email is sent.
  - **Moving someone into or out of the Executive board, or changing an executive's role, needs Manage people.** Without it, **Group** and **Role** of an executive are greyed out, the **Executive board** choice is greyed out for everyone else, and a line under **Group** says why. Changing an executive's name, photo or "Show on public page?" still works for every board member.
  - **Moving someone out of the Executive board turns off their Manage people and Manage money** in the same save. The screen says so under **Group** before you save. When the move would be refused (yourself, or the last person who can manage people), the line under **Group** gives that reason instead.
  - **Nobody can move themselves out while they can manage people, and the last person who can manage people stays in the Executive board** (the same guard as before for removing Manage people). So the club always has an executive who can manage people and edit Pages.
  - The site owner's emergency step in the Supabase SQL editor, `select public.owner_add_admin('email', 'Full name', true);`, now puts the new admin in the **Executive board** (hidden from the public list), because Manage people needs the Executive board: in the first free role, President first (one person per role, see below). A role can be named as a fourth value, for example `…, true, 'Treasurer');`; its current holder then moves to the General board with no role title. With `false` it still goes in the General board.
  - For developers: edited in place in `202610050001_people_access.sql` (no hosted data): `in_executive_board()`, the trigger `executive_only` on `admins` (SGU14 `not_executive`), the triggers `executive_moves` (SGU15) and `leave_executive` on `people`, `grant_access` answers `not_executive`, `set_access` answers `not_executive` (also in `202610090001_payments_seasons.sql`), `owner_add_admin(email, full_name, manage_people, executive_role default 'President')`, and the D-9 backfill files old admins under the Executive board as President. Mirrors: `permissionsProblem`, `touchesExecutive`, `leavesExecutive` and `accessLocks(…, executive)` in `src/lib/community/people.ts`; the sandbox adapter applies them; `savePersonAction` checks before the database.
- **Shared words, option A** ([PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) amendment of 2026-10-06; user decision 2026-10-06):
  - **A section whose words show on more than one page now shows on every page that uses it, with a small label "Shared · also on (page)".** The Jumu’ah heading and note show on **Pages → Prayer & Musallah** ("Shared · also on Home") and on **Pages → Home** ("Shared · also on Prayer & Musallah"); on Home, **Common questions** now also holds the questions themselves ("Shared · also on FAQ"), and on **Pages → FAQ** the **Questions** say "Shared · also on Home". Editing them on any of those pages changes them everywhere: the words are still stored once.
  - **Preview has a page switcher** when the words you edit show on other pages: "Preview the page:" with a button per page (for example **FAQ** and **Home**). Each page is drawn with what you typed, before you save.
  - **Jumu’ah, Musallah directions and Newsletter archive are no longer in the Pages list.** Their words are edited on the pages that show them (Prayer & Musallah, Home, Resources), and those cards' one-line descriptions in the Pages list now name them. Their own screens stay, for their History: they say "Its words are edited on the page that shows them:" with a link to each page, and the **History of …** links under a page's History still open them.
  - A shared section counts as hidden only when every page that shows it hides it: hiding Jumu’ah on Prayer & Musallah no longer lets its heading be left empty, because Home still shows it.
  - For developers: `LayoutSection.with`, `PageLayout.inside`, `placesOf`, `pagesShowing`, `drawnOn`, `previewPages` and `showingPages` in `src/lib/content/page-layouts.ts`; `hiddenSections` now means "hidden everywhere it is drawn"; `editedOn` and `pagesListed` in `pages-list.ts`; previews' `sharedOver` is by page; `EditorTabs` takes `previewControls`. No database change.
- **The live site can launch before the club Gmail is recovered** ([daily-check contract](contracts/daily-check-notifications.md) D-17, the user, 2026-10-06). On Vercel Production, `MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS` and `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD` may now **both** be `off` instead of stopping the deploy. Then:
  - the build log prints a **warning** (not an error) naming both settings and saying warning emails are off; the deploy goes on;
  - the dashboard's **Needs attention** box shows "Warning emails are off: set up the MSA Gmail" until both are set;
  - the nightly check still runs (`CRON_SECRET` is still required) and the box still lists problems; it just emails nothing.
  - One `off` and one real value is still refused, a missing value still stops the deploy, and `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` is still required.
- **Photos screen and reusing photos (final plan A6)** ([PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md) and [PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) amendments of 2026-10-06):
  - The dashboard has a new **Photos** card. **Photos** lists every uploaded photo and PDF, newest first: the picture (a PDF shows a file icon), the day it was uploaded, its size, and **Used on:** with a link to each event, announcement, news item, newsletter, person or page that uses it ("(archived)" after an archived one).
  - On top: "Space used: … of 1 GB (…%)" with a bar. The free plan has 1 GB for files; from 80% full a red line says space is running out.
  - **Delete** shows only on a file nothing uses. It asks first ("Delete this file?"); a deleted file cannot come back. A file still in use cannot be deleted.
  - Every photo box (events, announcements, news, People, and every photo on Pages) has **Choose from uploaded photos**: pick a photo that is already uploaded instead of uploading it again. **Undo** puts the box back.
  - **Every public picture can now be changed from the dashboard, except the logo.** The one that could not, Home's **Your first visit** photo, is now in **Pages → Home → Your first visit** (only the photo; its words stay fixed). Merch and season-page photos come with the season/merch change (PR5c).
  - For developers: `/admin/photos`, `src/features/admin/photos/` (`photo-library.ts`, `photo-uses.ts`, `photo-actions.ts`, `PhotoChooser.tsx`, `DeleteFile.tsx`), `/admin/media/[name]` (board-only file reads), `ContentEditor.listFiles/readFile/fileInUse`, `ContentProvider.getStorageUse`, `src/lib/media/storage-space.ts`; `community_storage_bytes()` added in place to `202609090001_community.sql`; Home's seed regenerated in `202610100001_page_texts.sql` (`visit.image`).
- **Daily check, part 2 (final plan A4)** ([daily-check contract](contracts/daily-check-notifications.md) amendment of 2026-10-06). Same rules as before: an email only when something needs fixing, one email a night with everything in it, at most 2 emails per problem, nothing on a normal night. The dashboard's **Needs attention** box and the email now also list:
  - a season day still "expected" from 3 days before it ("Eid al-Fitr is still “expected” on Tue 9 Mar 2027"; Ramadan's first and last day each);
  - a fund or ticket that ended and whose Go2Pay page must be closed by hand, for the 7 days its list says "close it in Go2Pay";
  - a fund or ticket waiting for "Running again", from 45 days before its season;
  - the photo space at 80% and again at 95% full (new tag `[Photos]`).
  - If any of these cannot be read one night, that night sends nothing; the next night catches up.

- **Home top balanced on a computer** (the user, 2026-10-06, after review 3 A made it look crammed). On a laptop or desktop at least 1024 px wide and 784 px tall, the space above the Home headline now grows with the screen height, so the headline block sits roughly in the middle of the first screen under the announcement bar: about 80 px at 1280 × 800, 98 px at 1440 × 900, 160 px (the most) at 1920 × 1080 and taller, instead of 64 px everywhere. The prayer card on the right still starts level with the headline. Shorter laptop screens (such as 1366 × 768), tablets and phones keep the compact top, so the links stay in view. For developers: `.hero` in `src/features/home/home.module.css`, `clamp(5rem, 50svh - 22rem, 10rem)` under `(min-width: 64rem) and (min-height: 49rem)`.
- **Season pages and merch are edited from the dashboard** ([PR6 contract](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md) and [PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md) amendments of 2026-10-06). Any board member can do all of it; no file is edited any more.
  - **Seasons → (a season) → Public page:** tick **Give this season its own page**, then write an introduction, add schedule rows (What, When, Where; drag to reorder), choose a sign-up form from the forms list and add photos (each with a description; drag to reorder, the first is the cover). **Edit | Preview** shows the page before saving. The page's address shows on the screen and in a new **Public page** column of the Seasons list.
  - **/ramadan** is now the Ramadan season's page, and every other season with a page is at **/seasons/(its name)**, for example `/seasons/islamic-awareness-week`. The page stays up all year: in the season it says "Ramadan 2027"; outside it says "Ramadan 2027 is expected to start around 8 Feb." (or "starts on Mon 8 Feb." once you confirm the first day) and keeps its words and photos labelled with the year it last ran ("Ramadan 2026"). **No Hijri dates on these pages.** Confirmed days are still typed in the season's form (no "Eid is tomorrow" button).
  - In Ramadan, a **Tonight** card shows the night of the month, **Iftar** (Maghrib's start time) and **Tarawih** (Isha's iqamah), both from Prayer & iqamah: nothing is typed twice.
  - The page also lists the season's **events** (new **Season (optional)** box in the event form) and its **open funds** (funds tied to the season), with Give buttons.
  - Removed from /ramadan because they were file text with no dashboard box: the "Ramadan at SGU" title (the page title is the season's name), the "Ramadan Calendar" button, the sponsorship band's text and "Your support helps provide" icons, and the volunteer heading. The board's introduction, schedule, funds and sign-up form replace them.
  - **New Merch screen** (Dashboard → **Merch**): collections (drag to reorder; **On sale now?** Yes or No), each collection's items (drag to reorder) with name, description, garment, colour and photos, and each item's **sizes**. A size is its own Go2Pay product, made by hand in Go2Pay with its own price and stock, then its link pasted, like a ticket. Sizes never show on the Tickets screen.
  - **/merch** now comes from the Merch screen: the collection on sale leads, each item with its photos (uploaded photos open in the photo viewer), details and "See price and buy ↗" with one button per size on sale. No price on our page. Past collections say "Not for sale". The page's opening words are in **Pages → Merch** (Executive board).
  - An item that still has sizes on sale cannot be archived: archive its sizes first ("Archive this item's sizes first.").
  - The **Photos** screen's **Used on:** also names season pages and merch items ("Season page: Ramadan", "Merch item: Shukr long-sleeve"), with links.
  - **Recent changes** has a new **Merch** filter.
  - For developers: `content/ramadan.yaml` and the merch collections in `content/merch.yaml` are gone (`merch.yaml` keeps only the starter opening). Edited in place (no hosted data): `202610090001_payments_seasons.sql` (season page columns and `season_slug`, `events.season_id`, `merch_collections`, `merch_items`, size columns on `payment_items`), `202610100001_page_texts.sql` (key `merch`, photo rules), `202610120001_team_groups_order.sql` (`save_order` lists). Staging must be rebuilt from the clean files.
- Docs only (no visible change): **database access rules** are written down in the [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md) (§2, §4–§6, §10, §13) and the handbook's chapter 6 (6.1, 6.4, 6.9–6.11): who can open the database, structure changes only as new reviewed files (staging first), content only through the dashboard, emergency SQL written down, adding the first admin (the President first) without "Send invitation", a new-developer checklist, password-reset safety, and the clean public code repository with a separate private history/tracker. To test: follow the current owner guide rather than this superseded historical summary.
- Developers never get a login on the live dashboard (decided 2026-10-07; the planned "developer access" is cancelled): they test in the local sandbox or on staging. Written in the owner guide's new-developer checklist and handbook chapter 6. People screen: the "Listed by role, President first…" line under Executive board is gone.

- **One person per Executive board role** (the user, 2026-10-06; [PR3 contract](contracts/pr3-people-permissions-activity.md) amendment of that date). President, Vice President (Brothers), Vice President (Sisters), Treasurer and Secretary are each held by one current person at a time; the database refuses a second one, also on **Restore**.
  - Choosing a taken role on a person's page asks under **Role**: "Omar Haddad is President. Make Sara Malik President instead?" with **Yes** and **Cancel**. **Yes** moves Omar to the end of the **General board** in the same save, with an **empty role title** (their "Show on public page?" answer stays as it was); both changes show in Recent changes. Nobody is deleted. **Cancel** puts the role back.
  - Only someone who can manage people gets **Yes**. If the holder is you, or the last person who can manage people, the question says why they stay and offers only **Cancel**.
  - Restoring an archived person whose role someone else holds now is refused: "Someone else holds this person's Executive board role now. Move that person out of the role first, then restore."
  - For developers: the trigger `people_one_role_holder` (SGU17, one transaction lock so two saves of a role run one after another) and the function `save_person_taking_role` (security invoker: RLS and the People triggers apply), edited in place in `202610050001_people_access.sql`; `roleHolder` / `roleHolders` in `src/lib/community/people.ts`; `PeopleEditor.savePersonTakingRole`; the sandbox mirrors both.
- **Review fixes P1a: one way to pick dates and times, one save state, plain error messages, and "Appears on"** (the approved plan of 2026-10-07, P1 items 1–4). Every dashboard editor changes the same way:
  - **Times read on a 12-hour clock everywhere in the dashboard.** Every time box is three drop-downs, **Hour : Minutes AM/PM** (for example `7 : 30 PM`), whatever the computer's language; times are still saved as before. The prayer table's **Today** column and the before → after lines in History and Recent changes show "5:30 AM" instead of "05:30".
  - **Dates and times that the rules refuse are greyed out instead of refused after Save.** An event's or notice's end, "Hide after" and "Show until" cannot be before their start; an urgent notice's and a Jumu'ah or event notice's **Hide at** cannot be before today; on the start day an event's end time cannot be at or before its start time; **Times end on** and a **Changes for one date** day cannot be in the past (the plan's P2 rule); a confirmed Islamic season day stays within 5 days of the expected day (the plan's P4 rule; P4 removed the old "treated as last year's" message); Recent changes' and Payments' **Until** cannot be before **From**. Every other date box allows any day (see "You or the founder must decide").
  - **One save state in every editor.** While something is changed and not saved, a small **Unsaved changes** label stays at the bottom right of the form (since the review follow-ups: a bar along the bottom of the screen with **Save**, below); while the save runs it says **Saving…**; after the save a **✓ Saved** note appears at the bottom of the screen for 4 seconds and fades (it simply goes with "reduce motion" on). The long green "Saved…" lines at the top of the lists are gone, and the address no longer gets `?saved=1` (or `?pin=saved`, `?login=saved`, `?password=saved`). People keeps its one extra sentence in the note: "Saved. The person who held that role is now in the General board." Saving a new password keeps its sentence in the same note: "Your password is saved. You were signed out on your other devices." A list you drag into order says "Order saved." in the same note, instead of a line that stayed under the list.
  - **Error messages are always plain words.** A box never shows a pattern, a field's code name or "Invalid…"; anything the site did not word itself reads "Fill in this box.", "Use 160 characters or fewer.", "Choose one from the list." and so on. A page loaded before an update says "This page is out of date. Reload it and try again." A test checks every form's rules for this on each pull request.
  - **"Appears on: …" at the top of every editor**, with links that open the public page in a new tab: Events → Events page and Home; Announcements → Home and Community page; Community news → Community page and Home; Newsletters → Newsletter archive; a person and Team groups → About; Funds → Donate; Tickets → Events page; Prayer & iqamah → Prayer & Musallah and Home; the Musallah location → Prayer & Musallah, Home and Contact; Merch → Merch; a season → its own page (when it has one); each Pages screen → its page. Screens whose work stays in the dashboard (New board term, Photos, Recent changes) show none.
  - For developers: `DateField` and `TimeField` in `src/shared/ui` (each use names its rule from `src/lib/time/field-rules.ts`; Storybook in light and dark), `SaveStatus` inside every `CheckedForm` and one `SavedToast` in the admin layout (`src/features/admin/save-state/`; a save ends with `redirectSaved(path)`, a one-minute cookie for `/admin` that holds only a name from the fixed `savedNotes` list), one zod error map (`src/lib/validation/zod.ts` + `messages.ts`; every file imports zod from there, tested), and the "Appears on" registry `src/features/admin/appears-on.ts`, which takes each page's name and address from the Pages list.
- Docs only (no visible change): AI coding agents now have a required **dashboard UX gate** (the 12 rules and final checklist from your 2026-10-06 review). A dashboard change cannot be called ready to merge until every checklist heading passes or is explained, and editing one of the four baseline database files is a blocker: database changes are new files, applied to staging first with your OK. Nothing to test.

- **Review fixes P1b: the dashboard's shared parts** (the approved review-fixes plan of 2026-10-07, "P1. Shared dashboard system layer", items 5–8). Nothing changes on the public site.
  - **Notifications bell.** Every dashboard page has a bell beside **Sign out**, with a number when something needs you. Click it: **Needs attention** lists the problems (the daily check's list: iqamah times ending, season days still "expected", funds to close in Go2Pay or waiting for "Running again", photo space; "Warning emails are off"; fewer than two people who can manage people; nobody who can edit Pages; these last two only for people with **Manage people**, who can fix them), each with its link. **For your information** lists a prayer settings change of the last 7 days ("… on Wed 7 Oct at 6:02 AM (Grenada time)", dated like the lines above it), with **×** to close it in this browser. On a phone the panel is as long as its lines and the page scrolls; on a bigger screen a long panel scrolls inside, with a shadow at the edge that has more. Each line is worked out again on every page, so it goes by itself once fixed; problems have no ×. Escape or a click outside closes the panel. The **Needs attention** box, the manager warning (dashboard and New board term), the Pages-editor warning and the prayer-change box are gone.
  - **Archive actions follow the item.** One shared set of buttons everywhere something can be archived (events, announcements, news, newsletters, People, groups, funds, tickets, sizes, seasons, merch collections and items, hand-over steps, shared logins): a current item has **Archive this …**; an archived one has **View** (in lists), **Restore** and, for content and People, **Delete permanently** (managers only; in a list it shows only to them). Archived lists now restore in place instead of only **Open**.
  - **History and Recent changes** are shorter: no explanation paragraphs, "No changes yet." when empty, and History shows the last 5 changes with **Show more** for the rest (up to 20).
  - **Full-page preview.** The **Edit | Preview** tabs are gone. Every editor that had them (events, announcements, news, newsletters, Pages, funds, seasons, merch collections and items) has an eye button **Preview** above the form. It opens the real public page, with what you typed and nothing saved, over the whole screen, with **Desktop**, **Tablet** and **Phone** widths (each lays the page out as that screen would, shrunk to fit). **Close** or Escape goes back with everything typed kept. An event shows its own page (the event's address, with the month beside it); a fund shows the whole Donate page with the fund as typed among the funds shown today, so its heading and place in the order are seen on the page itself (this replaces the "On the Donate page" and "Where it sits" lines), with the Go2Pay note above the page. The page in the preview can be reached with Tab and scrolled with the arrow keys or Page Down (a ring marks it). Event, news and newsletter lists show their dates as "Tue 20 Oct 2026".
  - For developers: `ArchiveActions` / `ArchivedRow` / `ArchivedEntry` (`src/features/admin/ArchiveActions.tsx`), `boardNotifications` + `readNotifications` + `NotificationBell` (`src/features/admin/notifications/`), `EditorPreview` + `PreviewFrame` (an iframe with no address, the site's styles copied in, a React portal) replace `EditorTabs`, `NeedsAttention` and `ManagerNotice`; `CollapsedList` in `history/`; `ActionForm` takes a kept-values action; `EventDetail` (`src/features/calendar/`) draws both `/calendar/[eventId]` and the event preview; `readyDonatePage` (`payment-policy.ts`) builds both `/donate` and the fund preview. No database change.

- **Review fixes P4: people, payments, seasons and the team list** (the approved plan of 2026-10-07, "P4. People, payments and seasons" and "Team list on the public site"). Needs the database update `202610160003_team_list_and_campus_pin.sql` (see Settings below).
  - **About: the Executive board is always shown, in tiers.** The President; then the two Vice Presidents; then the Treasurer and the Secretary, each with a larger portrait photo when one is uploaded (one column on phones). An Executive board member with no photo still shows. There is no "Show on public page" choice for the Executive board any more.
  - **"View all" under the Executive board** opens the rest of the team right below on the same page (General board, Advisors/Faculty and the board's own groups, in the People order), by name and role only, with no photos; the Executive board is not repeated. The button then reads **Show less**. It works with Enter and Space.
  - **Everyone else keeps "Show on public page"**, now a tick box, ticked for a new person (in every group, unless the group's start answer is changed on **Edit groups**). Untick it for someone who doesn't want their name online.
  - **Photos are for the Executive board only.** The photo box shows only for them, with a nudge "No photo yet. Add one: …" while they have none; People lists "No photo yet" under them, and the bell has a closable line "Add a photo of …" that goes by itself once every photo is added. Someone moved out of the Executive board loses their photo (the file is deleted; since the review follow-ups the photo is kept hidden instead, below). The save and the database both refuse a photo for anyone else.
  - **Archiving a person is now "No longer on the team".** The button and its wording say what happens: off the About page, login off, their entry and history kept under Archived, restorable.
  - **Change email** (People → a person → Dashboard login, for people who can manage people): changes the email they sign in with; their password stays. Someone invited who has not signed in yet gets the invite again at the new address.
  - **Permission boxes only where they can be used.** For someone outside the Executive board, the **Manage people** / **Manage money** boxes are replaced by one line saying they are for the Executive board only (also on the invite form). After **Save permissions** the boxes and labels show what was saved.
  - **The musallah pin stays within about 1.5 km of the SGU campus.** A dot dragged further jumps back with "The pin must be on or near the SGU campus. Drag it back onto the campus."; nothing is saved.
  - **Funds say why they are not on Donate.** Each fund on **Payments → Funds** reads "On the Donate page now", or "Not on the Donate page: …" with every reason (no Go2Pay link, "Show on the Donate page" is unticked, starts on (day), its season starts on (day), ended on (day), waiting for "Running again", its season is over or its dates can't be worked out; days with the weekday, such as "Tue 9 Mar 2027"), from the same rule that builds **/donate**. An ended fund's red label now reads only "Close it in Go2Pay", since the line above already says when it ended (tickets keep "Ended on (day) · close it in Go2Pay").
  - **Donate:** near the top, before the funds, a notice reads "Payments are made on Go2Pay, an outside service. About 5% goes to fees." (the fee line at the bottom is gone; a season page's funds show it above them too). Each fund's button reads **Give on Go2Pay ↗** and opens a new tab.
  - **Seasons:** under each confirmed-day box, "Choose a day from (day) to (day)." names the 5 days either side of the expected day (the picker greys out the rest; a day typed by keyboard outside them is refused before saving with "Choose a day within 5 days of the expected day."). The message "A confirmed day is more than 5 days from the expected day, so it is treated as last year's and ignored" is gone; last year's confirmed day no longer shows in the box.
  - **For testers: the sandbox clock stands still.** `npm run demo` fixes "today" at the moment it starts (or at `SITE_DEMO_DATE`), so a fund whose **Show from** is the real today stays "Not on the Donate page: starts on (day)" until the demo is restarted after that day. Staging and the live site use the real Grenada date.
  - For developers: `public_team_list()` (with each group's kind; `public_team()` keeps its columns for the photo storage rule and reads it), the trigger `people_photo_executive_only` (SGU40), `shown_by_default` now defaults to true, and the check `musallah_location_near_campus`, all in the new file `202610160003_team_list_and_campus_pin.sql` (tested on PGlite in `tests/team-list-migration.test.ts`); mirrors `listedPublicly`, `photoAllowed`, `executiveTiers`, `executivesWithoutPhoto` (`src/lib/community/people.ts`), `nearCampus` (`src/lib/maps/place.ts`) and `closedReasons` (`payment-policy.ts`, now behind `canPayNow`); `AccountInviter.changeEmail` (Supabase Auth `updateUserById`, the secret-key adapter) and `changeEmailAction`; `TeamSeats` + `TeamMore` on About; `confirmedDayReach` / `confirmedDayProblems` (`payment-forms.ts`) in the season form and its save.
- **Review fixes P2: Prayer & iqamah** (the approved review-fixes plan of 2026-10-07, "P2. Prayer & iqamah"). Needs the database update `202610160001_jumuah_sessions.sql` (Settings below).
  - **"4. Hijri date" is gone** from Prayer & iqamah. The Hijri date on the website stays as calculated: saving the prayer settings (or restoring an older version from History) sets it back to "as calculated" (since the review follow-ups the database update itself sets it, below). The dashboard card now reads "Iqamah, Jumu’ah and one-day changes →", the "Show me how" tour has no Hijri step, and History no longer lists a Hijri line.
  - **Each prayer shows what it gives today, as you type.** Under each iqamah box: "Adhan 12:31 PM → Iqamah 12:45 PM today", worked out from what is typed (a fixed time, or the minutes after today's calculated adhan), the typed **Times start on** / **Times end on** and a typed **Changes for one date** for today, with the same rule as the public page. When today is outside the dates it says so: "No iqamah today. Starts Tue 20 Oct" (or "Ended …"); a one-date change for today reads "Iqamah 5:45 AM today (one-date change)". The old **Today** column, which showed the saved time, is gone. Switching **Fixed time** / **After adhan** keeps what you typed in each, so switching back brings it back.
  - **Past dates stay greyed out** for a **Changes for one date** day and **Times end on** (already since P1a; checked again).
  - **Several Jumu’ah prayers.** **2. Jumu’ah (every Friday)** is now a list: each prayer has a **Khutbah** and an **Iqamah** time (iqamah times before the khutbah are greyed out), **+ Add a Jumu’ah prayer** (up to 4), **Remove**, and drag ⠿ to change the order. With no prayer: "No weekly Jumu’ah. Add a prayer to show one."
  - **A one-date change can change or cancel any prayer.** On a Friday, **Different time** shows that Friday's own list, starting from the weekly prayers: change a time, remove one prayer to cancel only that one, or add one. **No Jumu’ah this week** still cancels all of them. The urgent-notice text and **Hide at** follow it ("Jumu’ah this Friday (16 Oct) moves to 1:15 PM and 2:15 PM"; Hide at 30 minutes after that Friday's last iqamah).
  - **Visitors see every prayer.** The Jumu’ah card on **/prayer** lists them as **First prayer**, **Second prayer**…; a changed Friday shows "Changed this Friday, (date)" once, that Friday's prayers, the reason and "Usually:" with one line per usual prayer. Home reads "Jumu’ah, khutbah 12:45 PM, iqamah 1:00 PM; khutbah 1:45 PM, iqamah 2:00 PM". Every time on the site now keeps "12:45 PM" on one line (a phone no longer breaks "PM" onto the next line).
  - **History** lists one line per weekly prayer ("Jumu’ah, first prayer: khutbah 12:45 PM, iqamah 1:00 PM"), and a changed or cancelled Friday under **Changes for one date** shows its prayers or "No Jumu’ah this week". On a phone the drag handle ⠿ of a Jumu’ah prayer sits beside its name.
  - The sandbox's sample prayer settings now have two Jumu’ah prayers (12:45 / 1:00 PM and 1:45 / 2:00 PM). The starter hand-over step "Check content" no longer mentions the Hijri date setting (in the database update too, only where the board has not reworded it).
  - For developers: `jumuah_sessions` (zod `jumuahSessionList` in `src/lib/prayer/adjustments.ts`; database `valid_jumuah_sessions`) replaces `khutbah` / `jummah_iqamah`, which an older History version still holds and is read as a list of one; a changed Friday is `{change: "changed", sessions: […]}` (the one-time shape is read as a list of one). `JumuahSessionsField` (`src/features/admin`, on the shared `SortableList` and `TimeField`) is used for the weekly list and a changed Friday; form boxes `jumuah_<n>_khutbah` and `exception_<n>_jumuah_<m>_…` (`postedSessions`); an old page posting `khutbah` is "out of date". `iqamahOn` (one prayer's iqamah on a date: one-date clock > minutes after the adhan > current fixed clock) and `clockOf` in `congregation.ts` are shared by `congregationTimes` and the preview, which reads the typed dates and one-date changes through `useFormBoxes`. The database update also rewrites a changed Friday stored in the one-time shape into the list shape (skipped when the new rule would still refuse it). `timeOfDay` joins the time and AM/PM with a no-break space. `restoredPrayerSettings(before)` takes no second argument. Tests: `tests/jumuah-sessions-migration.test.ts` (new), `tests/community.test.ts`, `tests/review.test.ts`, `tests/pr5a-editors.test.ts`, `tests/prayer-migration.test.ts`, `tests/verse-library-migration.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/demo.spec.ts`.

- **Review fixes P3: events, content and pages** (the approved plan of 2026-10-07, "P3. Events, content and pages"). Needs the new database file `202610160002_lists.sql` (see Settings; staging first, after your OK).
  - **Lists.** A new dashboard card **Lists** (any board member) holds three lists: **Event categories**, **Terms** and **Resource categories**. Add one with the box under the list and **Add**; rename one in its box and **Save name**; drag ⠿ to change the order, then **Save order**. **Delete** shows only for an option nothing uses; one in use says "Used by 3 events. Move them first." instead. On a phone, **Save name** and **Delete** sit side by side under the name. The starter lists are the old ones: MSA, Academic, Informational, Faith, Social; Newsletter, Infographic, Guide, Academic, Faith, Ramadan, Incoming students; no terms yet.
  - **Events get Category (optional) and Term (optional)** drop-downs (under **Place**), filled from the Lists. The Events page's category buttons and **Term** list now come from the Lists, in their order, and show only options that have events in the tab shown (**Upcoming events** or **Past events**); with none, that filter is hidden. Switching tabs puts the choice back to **All**. Each event card and event page shows its category (with its colour dot) and its term by name (the old "Term 1" style and the T1–T5 list are gone).
  - **Resources**: a resource's **Category** (Pages → Resources, was **Kind**) is chosen from the Lists' resource categories; the Resources page shows only categories with resources.
  - **The calendar rings today** (the small month on /calendar and on each event page). A day with an event keeps its own dot in the category's colour; the next event is no longer ringed (its card keeps its ink edge).
  - **Pictures fit their kind.** An event's picture (a flyer) is shown whole, never cropped, on the event page; a community post's picture is shown whole on its own page (lists still crop it to fit the card); team photos keep the face in a fixed crop; banners and scenery fill their frame as before.
  - **"Duplicate" is now "Create from this event"** (and "Create from this announcement"): it opens a new, unsaved item with the same details, the picture, category and term, and **no date**; choose the date and save. Nothing is saved until you press **Save**, and there is no "(copy)" in the title.
  - **Newsletters can be an image.** **The newsletter** accepts a PDF or a JPEG, PNG or WebP image (up to 3 MB), or a link. On Resources the button says **Open PDF**, **View image** or **Visit link** to match. A back issue with no file and no link is not listed (as a resource without a link).
  - **No "Coming soon" anywhere.** A closed sign-up form now hides its button (the **Text while closed** box is gone). "Link coming soon", "Pending verification", "Board roster to be confirmed.", "Details to be confirmed.", "Contact information to be confirmed." and "Official SGU resources will be linked here…" are no longer shown, and their Pages boxes are gone: a Musallah detail or a Support contact that is not confirmed is simply not shown. The sandbox's Ramadan schedule now has times instead of "To be confirmed" (the live starter schedule: see "You or the founder must decide").
  - **Home**: the sample "A warm welcome to new students" notice is gone from the sandbox; the verse under the welcome is a small divider (smaller Arabic, much less space around it, its **?** clear of the Arabic); the space between "More than a place to pray", "Common questions" and "Support our community" is even.
  - **Band sections stay last.** About's **Contact**, Home's **Support our community** and Prayer's **Musallah helpers** are bands across the page: in Pages they sit under the dragged list with "Always last on the page." and no ⠿; they can still be hidden. Moving Contact can no longer split the About page.
  - For developers: `list_options` + `save_order` with `list_options` + `events.category_id`/`term_id` + newsletter WebP in `202610160002_lists.sql` (PGlite test `tests/lists-migration.test.ts`); `src/lib/community/lists.ts`; `ContentProvider.getLists`, `ContentEditor.listOptions/saveListOption/deleteListOption`; `/admin/lists` (`ListsEditor`, `list-actions.ts`); the `list-option` Pages field; `SafeImage` `fit` (`contain`, `portrait`, `cover`); `startFrom` replaces `duplicateAsDraft`; `LayoutSection.band` + `withBandsLast`. `developerOwnedPaths` no longer lists `events.terms` or `resources.categories`.

- **Review fixes P5: Term export** (the approved plan of 2026-10-07, "P5. Term export"). A new dashboard card, **Term export**, only for board members with **Manage people** (anyone else sees no card, is sent back to the dashboard, and the download answers "Only board members who can manage people can export a term."). Nothing changes on the public site.
  - **Download the term:** type **Name of the term** (for example 2026 Fall) and pick **From** and **To** (To cannot be before From: those days are greyed out). **Download zip** stays off until all three are filled in, with "Fill in the name and both days to download." beside it. One click downloads `2026 Fall.zip`, made while it downloads and never kept on the website. Inside, a folder per year and term (`2026/Fall`, from the From day's year and the name) holds six spreadsheets that open in Excel and Google Sheets: **Events**, **Community news** and **Newsletters** dated from From to To (current and archived; dates as 2026-09-12, times as 6:30 PM, Published, Archived on), and **People**, **Funds** and **Resources** as they are on the day of the export (they have no date). Beside them, folders **Events**, **Community news**, **Newsletters**, **People** and **Resources** hold every photo and PDF those rows use, named after the item ("2026-09-12 Welcome dinner.webp"); each row names its file. A file that has gone missing is listed in "Files not found.csv". If a list cannot be read, no zip is made and the screen says "The term could not be read. Check your connection and try again." with the boxes as they were.
  - **Free up space:** once the zip for the same days has started downloading ("The zip is downloading. Open it to check it."), **Free up space…** opens a question that says exactly what goes: "3 photos and PDFs (2.4 MB) of 2 archived items dated 1 Sep 2026 to 15 Dec 2026 will be deleted. The items stay in Archived without them. This can't be undone, so keep the zip." Under it a tick box, "I opened the zip and can see the photos", must be ticked before **Remove files** turns on (the website itself also refuses without it: "Tick “I opened the zip and can see the photos” before removing files."). **Remove files** clears the photos and PDFs of the **archived** events, community news and newsletters dated in those days and deletes the files; the items stay archived with all their words, Recent changes and History show each one as changed, and nothing on the website changes (archived items are never shown). A file that a current item, another day's item, a person, a page, a season or a merch item still uses is kept, and stays on the archived item too, so a later **Restore** brings it back with it ("1 more stays, because another item or page still uses it."). The question counts only the items that lose a file. Afterwards: "Space freed: 3 files removed (2.4 MB).". Nothing to remove: "No archived item from these days has a photo or PDF to remove.". If the files left cannot be counted afterwards: "Free up space is done, but the website could not count what was removed. Photos shows what is left." There is no import back. **Download zip** stays off with the reason beside it: "Fill in the name and both days to download." while a box is empty, or "To: choose the From day or a later one." when To is before From.
  - **Vercel free (Hobby) plan:** the zip streams, so the 4.5 MB response limit does not apply; the download may run up to 5 minutes (the plan's most). The free database holds at most 1 GB of files, which downloads well within that on a normal connection; if a very large term ever fails, export it in two shorter ranges. Each export counts its size against Supabase's free monthly download (egress) allowance.
  - **Sandbox:** the `board` / `board` sign-in now has neither **Manage money** nor **Manage people** (it had Manage people before), so the manager-only screens can be tried as a non-manager.
  - For developers: one new additive migration, `supabase/migrations/202610160004_term_export.sql` (`release_archived_files(from_day, until_day)`: security invoker, "manage people" only; it tries clearing the term's archived items in a block it always rolls back to learn which files `community_file_in_use()` would then no longer find, clears only those (logged per row) and returns them; PGlite test `tests/term-export-migration.test.ts`). `ContentEditor` gains `listRecordsDated`, `releaseArchivedFiles` and `readFiles` (signed in once, so files stream after the response starts), with sandbox adapters. `src/lib/media/zip.ts` writes a plain zip with Node's own zlib (no new dependency), `src/lib/csv.ts` is the one CSV cell rule (the Payments CSV uses it too), and `src/features/admin/term-export/` holds the screen, the zip's contents, the route's reads and the two actions. **No secret key**: the export and Free up space run as the signed-in manager, so RLS and the storage rule decide (ADR 0012, 0022 unchanged). Apply the migration to **staging after the user's OK** (Settings below).
- **Review follow-ups (your decisions of 2026-10-07).** Six small fixes; the two database files below are changed in place because neither has been applied anywhere yet.
  - **"Unsaved changes" is a thin bar along the bottom of the screen** (your pick, option B). It appears only once something is changed: "● Unsaved changes" with a **Save** button that saves the same form as the form's own Save button (it is greyed out while that one is, for example while a photo is made smaller). While saving it says "Saving…"; after the save the bar goes and "✓ Saved" shows as before. The page gets extra room at the bottom while the bar shows, so it never covers the end of a form, and a box the page scrolls to (a red box after Save) stops above it. It replaces the small floating label. Every dashboard form gets it; on a screen with several forms (Lists, Prayer & iqamah with its pin) it speaks for the one changed last. On a screen where you also drag things into order (Lists, Edit groups), the "The order changed. Save it to keep it." box with **Save order** and **Undo** stays just above the bar, never under it.
  - **Someone moved out of the Executive board keeps their photo, hidden.** It is not shown on About, not in **View all**, and not in their dashboard page (no **Remove photo** while they are outside the Executive board); it is not deleted, so it shows again when they come back to the Executive board. This also applies to the person moved to the General board by "Make (name) (role) instead?". The form line now reads "Moving them out of the Executive board hides their photo. It shows again if they come back." The database update `202610160003_team_list_and_campus_pin.sql` keeps the photo too (it no longer clears it).
  - **The Hijri date is as calculated straight away:** the database update `202610160001_jumuah_sessions.sql` now sets the stored Hijri correction to "as calculated", so the website does not wait for the next prayer save.
  - **People → Permissions → Manage money** now says what it does: "Can see Money in (the Go2Pay payments, with givers’ names and notes) and download it." (Funds, tickets and merch stay open to every board member.)
  - **"Appears on" on the Pages screens with no page of their own:** **Site-wide** and **Qur’an verses** say "Appears on: every page"; **Go2Pay receipt note** says "Appears on: Go2Pay receipts". (Sign-up forms already say where each form shows.)
  - **New board term → shared login → Last changed** offers no day after today, and a later day typed in is refused under the box: "Choose today or an earlier day."
- **Board handbook updated** (`handover/SGU-MSA-Website-Handbook.docx`) for the review fixes (#96–#105): a short "How every dashboard screen works" (Unsaved changes bar, ✓ Saved, 12-hour times, full-page Preview, Appears on, History "Show more"), the notifications bell, Lists, Create from this event, newsletter images and links, the team list and photos, No longer on the team, Change email, why a fund is not on Donate, Give on Go2Pay ↗, several Jumu'ah prayers and the live iqamah line (every Hijri-adjust step removed), the season ±5 days, the campus pin limit and Term export with Free up space. Outdated steps replaced, not added to.
- **Term export: Events.csv has Category and Term columns** (after Season), with the names from Dashboard → Lists; empty for an event with none. To test: give an event a category and a term, download its term, and check both columns in Events.csv.

### How to test (after the Release PR is merged)

- **Tracking docs:** follow the README/developer-guide audience links; check AGENTS.md mentions tickets, updates and the $0 spending ceiling. Verify the epic/child tickets are accessible to maintainers. No website behavior changes; org settings remain an operator task with actual values verified in #110.

From PR2 on, "looks the same as before" in the steps below means the same text, sections and layout: the colours, fonts, header and footer are the new Quiet Paper ones (step 20).

From review 2 PR-A on (step 76), a save with problems no longer lists them at the top: each box turns red with its reason under it and the top says only "Some boxes need fixing.", and the event form has **Start date** / **Start time (optional)** / **End date (optional)** / **End time (optional)** instead of **Date** / **Starts** / **Ends (optional)**. Earlier steps that expect "Please fix N things before saving:", **Starts** or **Ends**, **New date is known**, "Postponed from …" or "(next day)" follow step 76 instead; the sample "Welcome dinner" is no longer postponed.

From review 2 PR-B on (step 79), People has **groups** instead of sections and keeps no consent records. **Edit sections**, **Team sections**, **Add a section** / **Add section**, **Archive this section** / **Archive section**, **Archived sections** and the **Section** box are now **Edit groups**, **Team groups**, **Add a group** / **Add group**, **Archive this group** / **Archive group**, **Archived groups** and **Group** (messages say "group" too). The Executive board is listed by role (no ⠿, and **Role** is a list of five); it and the General board cannot be renamed or archived, so where an earlier step renames or archives a group, use one the board added. The listing and photo consent boxes are gone, a listed person shows on **/about** with their photo whenever one is uploaded, **Remove photo (consent withdrawn)** is now **Remove photo** ("Photo removed."), Recent changes shows no consent fields, the help card **Team list and consent** is now **Team list**, and the hand-over step is "Update the team list". Dragged lists have no up/down arrow buttons: where an earlier step clicks an arrow, drag ⠿, or press Tab to the ⠿ and use the arrow keys. Earlier steps that fill in consent, click **Edit sections** or an arrow, or look for "Team list and consent" (steps 21, 23, 26–30, 41, 49, 53, 62, 67 and 72) follow step 79 instead.

From review 3 B on (step 82), Pages is for people in the Executive board group (the sandbox account is the President, so it can), and a page with sections is a list: where an earlier step changes a box in a page's section, first click **Edit** next to that section. The Resources search boxes are in **Resources**, Get involved's introduction in **Top of the page**, and "No events" / "No updates" are **Events list** / **Updates list**.

    4. Open **/prayer**. Under **Today · (date)** there is one table with **Prayer**, **Begins** and **Iqamah**; Sunrise is greyed and has no iqamah; the next prayer is larger, circled in ink and marked **Next** (after Isha nothing is circled). The congregation times are the ones saved in the dashboard, shown on the 12-hour clock (13:30 saved reads "1:30 PM", step 38). The line under the table names the calculation and the iqamah note.
    5. Beside it (on a phone: below it) the inked **Jumu’ah** card (dark in light mode, light in dark mode) shows Khutbah, Iqamah and Location, or "Jumu’ah details will be published here." The home page's **View Prayer & Jumu’ah** and any **/prayer#jumuah** link land on it.
    6. **Find the Musallah**: the eight numbered steps, "Your destination is the SGU Musallah.", the photo, the **Find us on campus** box (as in step 12) and the three route photos with captions. The home page's **Find the Musallah** link scrolls here.
    7. **Musallah information** lists each item, with "Details to be confirmed." in italics where not confirmed; the helpers band shows the same button or "Coming Soon" text as before.
23. PR3 fixes (FIX). On a developer's computer (`npm run demo`) or on the staging site, never on the live site (these steps add test sections):
    1. Sign in at **/admin** (sandbox: username **admin**, password **admin**), click **People**, then **Edit sections**.
    2. Under **Add a section**, type "Restore test", then click **Add section**: "Saved."
    3. Under "Restore test", click **Archive this section**, then **Archive section**: "Section archived." At the bottom of the page, **Archived sections** lists "Restore test" with a **Restore** button.
    4. Click **Restore**: "Section restored." and "Restore test" is back in the list.
    5. Add a section "Twin test", archive it, then add a new section with the same name "Twin test". Under **Archived sections**, click **Restore** next to "Twin test": it is refused with "A current section already has this name. Rename that section first, then restore this one."
    6. Staging only, after the updated database files are applied (see Settings below): Supabase (staging) → **Table Editor** → **people_sections**, open the archived "Twin test" row (its **archived_at** has a date), change its **name** and click **Save**. Supabase refuses with an error that says the entry is archived.
    7. Archive the test sections you added that are still current ("Restore test" and the new "Twin test"): click **Archive this section**, then **Archive section** for each, so only real sections stay current.
    8. On GitHub, pull request #30 (PR3 fixes, FIX) shows a green **Validate pull request / database** check (it tests the other fixes: an archived person's login stays off, and any board member can delete a file nothing uses).
24. PR3 logins and passwords (WP-D). Steps 1-6 on a developer's computer (`npm run demo`; no email is sent there). Steps 7-10 on a developer's computer running the site against **staging** (`npm run dev` with staging in `.env.local`; staging has no website of its own), after staging's email settings are done (see Settings below), never on the live site. Staging's email links open only on that computer, because they start with its address (`http://localhost:3000`). Sending an invite needs a secret key, which a developer's computer never has for a hosted project, so the invite email is tested on the developer's local test setup instead (Developer setup below), and these steps make the test login with the owner's SQL step:
    1. Open **/admin** (signed out). Under "Board sign in" it says "For invited board members. Need access? Ask a board member who can manage people." Under the **Sign In** button there is a **Forgot your password?** link.
    2. Click **Forgot your password?**, type any email such as test@example.com and click **Send reset link**. You see "If this email belongs to a board account, a reset link is on its way. Check your inbox and spam folder."
    3. Sign in (username **admin**, password **admin**), click **People**, then **Add a person**: name "Login test", section **Advisors/Faculty**, answer **No**, **Save changes**. Click **Edit** next to "Login test" and scroll to **Dashboard login**: it says "Login test has no login and cannot open the board dashboard."
    4. Type the email test-login@example.com, tick **Manage money** and click **Send invite**. You see "Invite email sent to test-login@example.com. The link works once and expires in 24 hours." (the sandbox sends nothing) and the labels **Invited** and **Manage money**. Click **Send the invite again**: "Invite email sent again to …". On **People**, "Login test" shows **Invited**.
    5. Back on "Login test": click **Deactivate login**, then the **Deactivate login** button: "Login deactivated. …" and the label **Login deactivated**. Click **Reactivate login**: "Login reactivated. …" and **Invited** again.
    6. Open your own entry, **Omar Haddad**: under **Dashboard login** the **Manage people** box is greyed out with "You cannot remove your own access or your own 'manage people' permission. Ask another manager.", and under **Deactivate login** the button is greyed out with the same reason.
    7. Make a test login on staging: Supabase (staging) → **Authentication** → **Users** → **Add user** → **Create new user**, type an email address you can read, any long password (you will not need it) and tick **Auto Confirm User**. Then **SQL Editor** → **New query**, paste `select public.owner_add_admin('that email', 'Login staging test', false);` with the email between the first quotes, and click **Run**. Until the MSA Gmail is connected, Supabase's built-in email sends only to the email addresses of the Supabase organization's members and only a few emails per hour, so use such an address.
    8. On the developer's computer, open **http://localhost:3000/admin/forgot-password**, type the test email and click **Send reset link**: "If this email belongs to a board account, a reset link is on its way. …". The email "Reset your SGU MSA dashboard password" arrives (check Spam). Open its **Choose a new password** link on that same computer: a page "Continue to choose your password" opens; click **Continue** and you land on **Choose your password**. Type "password" in both boxes and click **Save password**: the browser stops it with its own message asking for at least 12 characters. Type a password that meets the rule under the box in both boxes and click **Save password**: the dashboard opens with "Your password is saved. You were signed out on your other devices." In **People**, "Login staging test" (under **Executive board**) now shows **Login active**.
    9. Open a private (incognito) window on the same computer and sign in at **http://localhost:3000/admin** with the test email and its new password. In the normal window, ask for a reset link for the test email again, open the new email's link there, click **Continue** and choose another password. In the private window, reload **/admin/dashboard**: you are sent back to the sign-in page. In the normal window, open the same reset link again and click **Continue**: "This link has expired or was already used. …"
    10. In the private window, sign in with the test account again (newest password). In the normal window, sign out, sign in with your own staging account, open "Login staging test" in **People** and use **Deactivate login**. In the private window, reload **/admin/dashboard**: you are sent back to sign-in, and signing in says "This account does not have board access. Ask a board member who can manage people." Leave the test entry deactivated (it can be archived once the Archive button arrives in PR3 step 6).
25. PR3 Recent changes (WP-E). Steps 1-6 on a developer's computer (`npm run demo`) or on the staging site, never on the live site (they change sample or test items). After the release, on the live site do only steps 2-3 (open Recent changes and look):
    1. Sign in (sandbox: username **admin**, password **admin**). Open **Events**, click **Edit** next to "An evening of reflection" (on staging: any test event), change the title to "An evening of reflection TEST" and click **Save changes**.
    2. Go back to the dashboard (**Dashboard** in the trail at the top). There is a **Recent changes** card next to **People**. Click it.
    3. The first entry reads "Edited event: An evening of reflection TEST", then the date and time (Grenada time) and your name (sandbox: "Omar Haddad"), then "Title: An evening of reflection → An evening of reflection TEST". The first time an item is saved from the dashboard there may also be a line "Text: changed (spaces or line breaks only)": the form sends line breaks in a different form than the stored text (see "Needs your confirmation").
    4. Open **People**, click **Edit** next to any person, change only the **Name** and save. In **Recent changes** the new entry says "Edited People entry: …" and "Name: changed", without the old or new name. Put the name back the same way.
    5. Open **Prayer & iqamah**, type "Test note" in **Schedule notice** and click **Save prayer settings**. Go to the dashboard and click the **notifications bell** (top right): under **For your information** it says "Prayer settings changed recently" with your name and the time. Click **See what changed in Recent changes**: the first entry has the "Prayer change" label and a gold edge, and shows "Notice: … → Test note". Remove the note again the same way (on staging, leave the schedule notice as it was before).
    6. Put the event title back. If the list has more than 25 entries, scroll to the bottom and click **Older changes**: earlier entries show, and **Newest changes** goes back.
    7. Optional, 8 days later on staging: the "Prayer settings changed recently" line is gone from the notifications (the change is still in Recent changes).
26. PR3 fixes, part 2 (FIX2). Steps 1-4 on a developer's computer (`npm run demo`) or on the staging site; step 5 on staging only; never on the live site (they add test entries):
    1. Email link (sandbox): open **http://localhost:3000/admin/auth/confirm?token_hash=test&type=invite**. A page "Continue to choose your password" with a **Continue** button opens, and nothing else happens. Click **Continue**: you are on the sign-in page with "This link has expired or was already used. …" (the sandbox never sends real links, so every link counts as used). Then open **http://localhost:3000/admin/auth/confirm?token_hash=test&type=signup** (a broken link): you go straight to the sign-in page with the same message, with no **Continue** page. On staging, step 24.8 shows the same page with a real link, and **Continue** opens **Choose your password**.
    2. Sign in (sandbox: username **admin**, password **admin**), click **People**, then **Add a person**: name "Log test", section **Advisors/Faculty**, answer **No**, no photo, **Save changes**.
    3. Open **Recent changes** (dashboard card). The newest entry is "Added People entry: …" and lists **Name: changed**, but not the consent dates, consent methods or photo (they were left empty).
    4. Open **Community news** (or Events), click **Edit** next to an item whose description has several lines, change nothing and click **Save changes**. In **Recent changes** no new entry appears for it. (The first such save of an item saved before this change can still show one entry, once.)
    5. Staging only, after the updated database files are applied (see Settings below): Supabase (staging) → **Table Editor** → **people**. Pick an archived test person that has a photo, or give "Log test" a photo and its photo consent in the dashboard and archive it once the Archive button arrives (PR3 step 6). Empty its **photo_path**, **photo_consent_on** and **photo_consent_method** and click **Save**: it is saved and the person stays archived. Now change its **role_title** and click **Save**: it is refused ("Archived entries are read-only. Restore it first.").

27. PR3 archive, restore, duplicate and prayer history (WP-F). Steps 1-8 on a developer's computer (`npm run demo`, sign in with username **admin**, password **admin**) or on the staging site, never on the live site (they change sample or test items):
    1. Dashboard → **Events** → **Create new**: title "Archive test", start time **17:00**, location "Test room", tick **Published**, **Save changes**. Open **/calendar** in another tab: "Archive test" is listed.
    2. Back in **Events**, click **Edit** next to "Archive test". At the bottom there is no **Delete permanently**; open **Archive this item** and click **Archive**. You see "Archived. It is off the website; find it under Archived." Reload **/calendar**: "Archive test" is gone.
    3. Click the **Archived** tab: "Archive test" is listed. Click **Open**: you see its date and summary but no form to edit it. Click **Restore**: "Restored as an unpublished draft." and the **Current** list shows it as **Draft**. **/calendar** still does not show it until you publish it again.
    4. Dashboard → **Announcements** → **Edit** next to any announcement → **Duplicate as a new draft**. A new page opens saying "Duplicated. This copy is an unpublished draft…", with the title ending in "(copy)" and **Published** not ticked.
    5. On that copy open **Archive this item** → **Archive**, then **Archived** tab → **View** the copy → **Delete permanently**. Type **delete** (small letters) and click **Delete permanently**: it is refused. Type **DELETE** and click again: "Permanently deleted." and the copy is gone. (On staging, a board member _without_ "manage people" sees "Only a board member who can manage people can delete permanently." instead of the box.)
    6. Dashboard → **People** → **Add a person**: name "Archive person", section **Advisors/Faculty**, answer **No**, add any photo with its photo consent (today, **In person**), **Save changes**. Click **Edit** next to it, invite any test address (sandbox: "Invited. Sandbox: no email was sent."). Open **Archive this person** → **Archive person**: "Archived. They are off the public team list and their login is off…". Click **Archived** → **Open** "Archive person". Under **Dashboard login**, **Reactivate login** is greyed out with "Restore this person first.". Click **Remove photo (consent withdrawn)**: "Photo and photo consent removed." and **Photo: No**. Click **Restore**: the edit form is back and the login still says "Login deactivated".
    7. Archive "Archive person" again, open it under **Archived**, **Delete permanently** → type **DELETE** → **Delete permanently**: "Permanently deleted, together with their login account." On staging, Supabase → **Authentication** → **Users** no longer lists the test address.
    8. Dashboard → **Prayer & iqamah**: change **Fajr** (for example to 05:12) and **Save prayer settings**. Scroll to **History**: the newest entry shows Fajr old → new. Click **Restore the version before this change** in that entry: "Earlier prayer settings restored." and **Fajr** shows the old time again. Open **/prayer**: the old Fajr time shows. Open **Recent changes**: the newest entry is "Edited prayer settings" with Fajr new → old.

Steps 1-6 on a developer's computer, either the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with username **admin**, password **admin**) or running against staging (`npm run dev` with staging in `.env.local`; staging has no website of its own), never on the live site (they add a test entry). After the release, on the live site do only step 1 and step 7:
