30. PR3 help and guides (WP-H). On a developer's computer (`npm run demo`, sign in with username **admin**, password **admin**, or running against staging), or after the release on the live site (steps 1-6 only read):
    1. Dashboard → **Read the board editing guide**. The page starts with **Topics**, a list of links. Click **Inviting and access**: the page jumps to that card.
    2. Check that these cards are there, each with numbered steps: **People**, **Team list and consent**, **Inviting and access**, **Your password and Forgot password**, **Recent changes**, **Archive, restore and duplicate**, **Delete permanently**, **Prayer history** and **End of term**.
    3. In **Your password and Forgot password**: it says to click **Continue** on the page the email link opens, and shows "At least 12 characters, including a lower-case letter, a capital letter, a number and a symbol.".
    4. Nowhere on the page does it say to contact the site owner for a password reset. **Correct or remove an item** says to use **Archive this item**, not to delete.
    5. In **People**, click **Open People →**: the People list opens. Back on Help, **Open Recent changes →** opens Recent changes and **Open Prayer & iqamah →** opens the prayer page (scroll down: **History**).
    6. On a phone the page fits the screen without sideways scrolling, in light and dark.
    7. Owner: open the [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md) on GitHub, §10. Follow it for staging (the settings below): every click it names exists, and the test invite arrives from "SGU MSA". If a screen looks different, tell the developer which step.
31. PR8 board feedback, shared parts (on a phone about 320–390 px wide and on a computer, light and dark):
    1. Open the home page in light mode: the page is light paper with deep green text (not warm beige with black text). Switch to dark: Dusk, as before. On a phone, the browser's top bar is light paper in light mode.
    2. Home: the dhikr beside the prayer times is on one line (morning, evening and night; the after-prayer and Friday dhikr may take two even lines). Under **Upcoming at MSA**, 5:2 is on one line; under **Our community, built by students**, 49:10 is on one line. On a 320 px phone nothing makes the page scroll sideways.
    3. Scroll to the footer: the verse is Arabic only ("وَقُل رَّبِّ زِدْنِى عِلْمًۭا") with a small **?** beside it, and no English sentence. Tap **?**: "My Lord, increase me in knowledge." and "Qur'an 20:114 (Taa-Haa)". Press Escape or tap outside: it closes.
    4. Tap any other **?** on Home or **/prayer**: the bubble shows the meaning and the reference only, with no "Arabic: Tanzil …" source line.
    5. Footer **Connect**: the first two icons are the Instagram camera-outline logo and the Facebook "f" logo, in the same colour as the email icon. A screen reader still reads them as "MSA on Instagram" and "MSA on Facebook", and they open those pages.
32. PR8 Events (on a phone and on a computer, light and dark):
    1. Open **/calendar**. The small month opens on the month of the next upcoming event, with that day circled in ink and a coloured dot on each day that has an event. The arrows change the month. Click a dotted day: the page jumps to that event's card, which gets a thin outline.
    2. Click **Past events**: the cards and the dots switch to past events and nothing is circled. Click **Upcoming events** again. Click a category (for example **Faith**): only those events stay, and the count under the **Term** list changes. Choose a term in **Term**: the same. A combination with no events shows "No matching events" and "Try another category or term."
    3. On a card, click **Add to Calendar**: a calendar file downloads and the button says "Downloaded" for a moment. Click a card's title: its event page opens.
    4. On the event page: the title, the date and time, **Add to Calendar** (and **Register** if the event has a link), the small month with this day circled, and Location, Source and Status. An SGU date says "This is an SGU date shown for convenience…"; an unconfirmed one says "This entry has not yet been confirmed. Details may change." **All events** goes back. A wrong event address shows the "page not found" page.
    5. Both pages show 5:2 with its **?** (meaning and reference).
33. PR8 Donate letter (on a phone and on a computer, light and dark):
    1. Open **/donate**. One paper sheet sits in the middle of the page: the seal at the top, "Giving at SGU MSA", the big heading **Support** with _our community_ in grey italics under it, and "Thank you for wanting to support Muslim student life at SGU."
    2. Under a thin line with a small three-dot ink mark, while no fund is listed: "There are no open appeals right now." and a **Contact the MSA** button that opens **/contact**. There is no card or payment form.
    3. At the bottom of the sheet: the Arabic verse 3:92, level (not tilted), on one line on a computer, with a **?**. Tap the **?**: it shows the English meaning and "Qur'an 3:92 (Aal-i-Imraan)".
    4. Switch to dark mode with the sun/moon button: the sheet turns dark, all text stays readable, and the verse and seal are still there.
    5. Once the board lists funds, the sheet shows their slips instead (step 75).
34. PR8 Resources (on a phone and on a computer, light and dark):
    1. Open **/resources**. The heading "Resources" / _Publication library_, the intro, and the Arabic verse with its **?** (click it: "My Lord, increase me in knowledge." and "Qur'an 20:114 (Taa-Haa)").
    2. On a computer the search and categories are on the left; **All** is circled in ink and every category shows a number. Click **Guide**: only guides are listed, the circle moves and the line under the categories says how many (for example "2 resources"). On a phone the categories are a sideways-scrolling row above the list.
    3. Type "zzzz" in the search: "No resources match this search." and "Try a different search or category." Clear it: the list comes back.
    4. A resource with a link shows **Open** (it opens the file or page); one without a link shows its "coming soon" label in italics instead.
    5. Scroll to **Back issues**: each newsletter shows its period, label, summary and **Open PDF** (or "Link coming soon"). With no newsletters it says "Newsletter editions will be listed here."
    6. On the home page, click the newsletter link: **/resources** opens with **Newsletter** circled.

    1. Open **/get-involved**. At the top: "Contribute", **Get Involved**, the line "Every part of the MSA is run by students. These are the ways in." and, beside it (on a phone: below it), the Arabic verse 5:2 with its **?** (click it: the meaning shows). Then the intro sentence.

Click **02 Give a Student Khutbah**: the ring moves to it and its sheet shows the four steps (Apply, Prepare, Review, Deliver) and the note "Submitting the form is an expression of interest…". Click **03 Host a Halaqa**: "The form asks about" lists Topic, Proposed facilitator, Audience, Preferred timing, Assistance needed. **04 Volunteer** shows the photo of students on the lawn.

The others are listed below it, newest first, each with its date ("3 October 2026" style), kind, title and summary. Click anywhere on an update: its page opens.

50. PR4 fix: old prayer settings with both a fixed time and minutes keep working, and the board picks one (D-14, amended 2026-10-05).
    1. In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`): Dashboard → **Prayer & iqamah**. Each prayer has one **Fixed time / After adhan** switch and one box, so both can no longer be typed for one prayer. Step 2 checks an old setting that has both.
    2. Optional, on **staging only** (never production), together with the developer, after the database update above. This copies an old live setting on purpose:
       1. Dashboard → **Prayer & iqamah**: pick a prayer set to **Fixed time** with a time, for example Dhuhr. Write down its time.
       2. Supabase → **sgu-msa-staging** → **SQL Editor** → **New query**, paste this (change `dhuhr` if you picked another prayer) and click **Run**: `update public.prayer_settings set iqamah_offsets = iqamah_offsets || '{"dhuhr": 10}'::jsonb where id = 1;`
       3. With the developer, open the local site connected to staging, then open **/prayer**: the page shows the times (not "temporarily unavailable"), and the Dhuhr **Iqamah** is 10 minutes after the Dhuhr **Begins** time.
       4. Reload Dashboard → **Prayer & iqamah**: the page opens (not "Settings could not be loaded"); Dhuhr shows both boxes (your time and `10`), neither **Fixed time** nor **After adhan** picked, and the red line "Dhuhr iqamah: pick Fixed time or After adhan, not both." under them.
       5. Click **Save prayer settings** without picking: the box at the top says "Please fix 1 thing before saving:" with the same line, and nothing is saved.
       6. Put it back: pick **Fixed time** for Dhuhr (the minutes box disappears; your time is still there) and click **Save prayer settings**: "Prayer settings saved." **/prayer** shows that time again.

Hijri date**; there is only one **Save prayer settings** button; there is no "Calculation method", "Asr convention", "Leadership has confirmed" or minute-correction box. On a phone each prayer shows as a small block (name and today's time, the switch, the box) and the page does not scroll sideways.
    3. Write down the Fajr time. For **Fajr** click **After adhan**, type `20` and click **Save prayer settings**: "Prayer settings saved." Open **/prayer**: the Fajr **Iqamah** is exactly 20 minutes after the Fajr **Begins** time.
    4. Back on **Prayer & iqamah**, click **+ Add a date**, pick the coming Friday, leave **Reason** empty, choose **No Jumu’ah this week**, empty **Times end on**, and save. A red box says "Please fix 2 things before saving:" and lists "Times start on and Times end on: …" and "Changes for one date: add a reason (shown to visitors).". Everything you typed is still there (the Friday, the choice, Fajr's 20).

The card is not thrown away: the red box lists "Changes for one date: choose a valid date." and "Changes for one date: add a reason (shown to visitors).", and the Fajr time is still there. Click **Remove this date**.
    8. Under **4. Hijri date** write down the date beside **One day later**, choose it and save. Home and **/prayer** show that Hijri date. **Recent changes** lists "Hijri date adjustment". Choose **As calculated** again and save.
    9. Put everything back: Fajr **Fixed time** with the time you wrote down, **Remove this date** on the card, save. **History** below still lists your changes and **Restore the version before this change** still works.

53. PR4 "Show me how" tours (WP-6). In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`), then on staging. Use a private (incognito) browser window so the browser has not seen the tour yet. Do it once on a phone and once on a computer:
    1. Sign in. The dashboard opens with the tour already running: the **Events** card is lit up and a box says "Step 1 of 5 · Your content" with **Skip tour** and **Next**.
    2. Click **Next** four times, reading each step (Prayer & iqamah, People, Recent changes, Help). The last step has **Back** and **Done** but no **Skip tour**. Click **Done**: the dimming goes away.
    3. Reload the page: the tour does **not** start again. Sign out and sign in again in the same window: it still does not start.
    4. Click **Show me how** (beside **Sign out**): the tour starts again at step 1. Click **Skip tour**: it ends.
    5. Open **Prayer & iqamah** and click **Show me how**: six steps (automatic start times, Daily iqamah, Jumu’ah, Changes for one date, Hijri date, Save and History). Each step points at the right part of the page.
    6. Open **Events**, then **Edit** on any event, and click **Show me how**: five steps (name, date and time, Who can see it?, Save, Duplicate or archive). On **Create new** the last step is left out (a new item cannot be archived yet), so the box counts four steps and step 4 of 4 ends with **Done**.
    7. Open **People** and click **Show me how**: four steps (Add a person, Show on public page?, Dashboard login, Edit sections). The **Archived** tab has no **Show me how**.
    8. Keyboard only (computer): on the dashboard press Tab until **Show me how** is highlighted and press Enter. The **Next** button is highlighted: press Enter to move on, Tab to reach **Skip tour** or **Back**, and Escape to end the tour.
    9. Open **Recent changes** and **Help**: they have no **Show me how** (no tour planned for them).
    10. Optional: switch the site to dark (round button at the top) and replay one tour: the box is readable.
    11. Old hidden expiration date (D-15), on staging only, with the developer: in Supabase → **SQL Editor** run the read-only check `select id, title, date, expires_on from public.events where expires_on is not null;` and the same with `newsletters`. Until each one listed is saved once, a past date there still keeps it off the website. Open each in the dashboard and click **Save** without changing anything: "Saved", and running the same check again no longer lists it.

61. PR5a editors and public pages (PR-B). In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`); on staging after PR-A's database update, the same steps work with real items:

    3. Phone with **Reduce motion** on (iPhone: Settings → Accessibility → Motion; Android: Settings → Accessibility → Remove animations): the band never moves by itself; ‹ › still change the notice, without sliding. With the screen reader on as well (iPhone VoiceOver, Android TalkBack), tapping **›** reads the new notice aloud.
    4. Dashboard → **Announcements**: "Urgent now: 2" above the list. **Create new**: type a headline, choose **Urgent**. **Hide after** is replaced by **Hide at (exact time)** with a date and a time box, and the two current urgent notices are listed. Set **Hide at** to today at a time that has passed, choose **Everyone on the website** and **Save**: "Hide at: choose a time later than now." Type a headline over 80 characters: "Headline: urgent notices fit 80 characters; put the rest in the details." Fix both and save: "Saved", and "Urgent now: 3". Home's band shows "of 3". On staging, check it disappears from the band at the **Hide at** minute (reload after it).

Click **Close**: everything typed is still there, including a chosen photo. With a **Show from** date in the future the preview says "Scheduled: appears on …".
    7. **Events** → "Welcome dinner": **Is it still happening?** shows **Postponed** and **New date is known**. Open **/calendar**: "Beach clean-up" is struck through with "Cancelled: Example reason…", "Qur'an night" says "Postponed: new date to be announced", "Welcome dinner" says "Postponed from …" and "Night of prayer" shows "10:00 PM – 3:00 AM (next day)". Home's week shows the same lines.
    8. On "Beach clean-up", click **Add to Calendar** and open the file in a phone or computer calendar: it is titled "Cancelled: Beach clean-up" and marked cancelled. Postpone an event of yours with **New date is known**, change its **Date** and save: it says "Postponed from (the old date)"; re-adding its calendar file moves the saved entry to the new date.
    9. Choose **Cancelled** with an empty **Reason**: the form will not save. Set it back to **Scheduled** and save: the line disappears.
    10. Open an event's page with a sign-up link: the button reads **Sign up →**. Archive an event, then open its old address: "This event is no longer listed", with **Events** and **Home**.
    11. **Prayer & iqamah** → **+ Add a date**: the coming Friday, a reason, **No Jumu’ah this week**. **Also show an urgent notice on every page?** appears. Click **Save prayer settings** without answering: the browser asks you to choose. Choose **Yes**: the text says "No Jumu’ah at the Musallah this Friday (…)" and **Hide at** is that Friday, 30 minutes after the usual Jumu'ah iqamah. Save: "Prayer settings saved", and Home's band includes the notice. Click **Save prayer settings** again: no question, and **Announcements** has the notice once. On another Friday choose **No Jumu’ah this week**, answer **No** and save: no new notice (**Announcements** still says the same "Urgent now" number). Try **Different time** on another Friday: the text says "Jumu’ah this Friday (…) moves to (khutbah time)". Clean up: remove the dates and archive the notices.

64. Daily check, the dashboard box and the sandbox email (WP-5, WP-6). In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`), on a phone and on a computer:
    1. Dashboard: click the **notifications bell** at the top. Under **Needs attention** it says "Iqamah times end on (today's date, for example Thu 8 Oct) (today)" with **Open Prayer & iqamah →**. Click the link: the Prayer & iqamah page opens.
    2. Set **Times end on** to a date 10 days from today and click **Save prayer settings**. Open the dashboard's notifications bell: no iqamah line. Set it to 7 days from today and save: the line says "(in 7 days)"; 1 day from today: "(tomorrow)".
    3. Set **Times start on** to two weeks ago and **Times end on** to yesterday, save: the box says "Iqamah times ended on (yesterday) — update them". Reload: still there. Set **Times end on** back to today and **Times start on** to today, save.
    4. Repeat 1 in dark mode (moon button): readable.

    6. Developer only (needs a terminal): start the sandbox with a local `CRON_SECRET` of 32+ letters (for example `CRON_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32) npm run demo`), then run `curl -i http://localhost:3000/api/daily-check`: **401 Unauthorized**. With `-H "Authorization: Bearer <the secret>"`: `{"warnings":1,"emailed":1}`; run it again: `{"warnings":1,"emailed":0}` (the same problem is not emailed twice). Adding `?secret=<the secret>` to the address instead of the header is refused (401). Nothing is sent: the sandbox only records the email.

Set `MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS` to a Gmail address but leave `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD=off`: it stops with "MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS and GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD go together". Put both back to `off`.
65. Daily check on the live site (after the Release PR and the Settings below; owner, about 10 minutes, [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md#the-daily-check-warning-emails-to-the-msa-gmail) steps 5 and 6):
    1. Vercel → the `sgu-msa-site` project → **Settings** → **Cron Jobs**: one line `/api/daily-check`, `0 5 * * *`. If it offers **Run**, click it, then **View Logs**: status 200 (the logs show the status, not the answer's text). Check the MSA Gmail: no email (nothing to fix).
    2. Live dashboard → **Prayer & iqamah** → **Times end on** 6 days from today → **Save prayer settings**. The dashboard's notifications bell lists it. Run the job again: status 200. The MSA Gmail gets "SGU MSA website: 1 thing needs attention" from "SGU MSA website" (look in Spam the first time and mark "Not spam"). Its link opens the live Prayer & iqamah page.
    3. Run the job once more: status 200, no second email.
    4. If the board wants one topic forwarded: in the MSA Gmail add the forwarding address and a filter (owner guide), wait for the next night's check or move **Times end on** to tomorrow and run the job (the last call): the forwarded copy arrives. Write down whether Gmail forwarded a message the account sent to itself (unconfirmed, below).
    5. Put **Times end on** back to the real date and save. Next night: no email, unless the real date is within 7 days.
    6. Open `https://(the site)/api/daily-check` in a browser: "Unauthorized".
    7. Only if the site launched with both Gmail settings `off` (D-17): the deploy's build log (Vercel → **Deployments** → the deployment → **Build Logs**) shows "Warning: MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS and GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD are "off": warning emails are off …", and the dashboard's **notifications bell** says "Warning emails are off: set up the MSA Gmail". Run the job: status 200, no email. Once both are set and the site redeployed, the line is gone; then do 1 to 5.

76. Review 2, PR-A: events and forms that guide you. In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`), on a computer and on a phone (390 px), light and dark; on staging once the database is rebuilt from the clean files (Settings below) the same steps work:
    1. **Events → Create new**: the boxes are **Start date**, **Start time (optional)**, then **End date (optional)**, **End time (optional)**. Type an Event name, a Start time of 1:00 PM and an End time of 1:00 PM: as soon as you leave End time it gets a red outline and "Choose a time after the start time." under it. Type "http://example.com" in **Sign-up link**: it turns red while you type ("Use a secure web address …").
    2. Choose **Everyone on the website** and click **Save**: one line at the top, "Some boxes need fixing." (no list), the cursor jumps to **End time**, and **Place** is red too ("Add where it happens, or save as a draft."). Nothing is saved. Fix the boxes one by one: each red outline goes as soon as the box is right.
    3. Clear both times (all day), set **End date** two days later, add a Place, clear the link and save: the calendar card says "Until …" with that day. Set Start time 10:00 PM, End date the next day, End time 3:00 AM: the card says "10:00 PM – (the next day's date, e.g. Sun 11 Oct), 3:00 AM". An End date before the Start date is refused on its box.
    4. Edit a published event and change its **Start date**: **Also post an announcement?** appears. Save without answering: "Choose Yes or No." under it. Choose **Yes**: the text says "(name) moves to (new day)" and **Hide at** is the end of the old day (or the new day, if earlier). Save: the urgent band on every page shows it. Edit another published event, choose **Cancelled** with a reason: the question appears with "Cancelled: (name)". Choose **No** and save: no notice. Choose **Postponed** on a third: no question; the card says "Postponed: new date to be announced". Clean up: archive the test notice.
    5. **Leave without saving?** Change a box, then click **Dashboard** in the breadcrumb: the dialog asks **Save**, **Discard** or **Keep editing**. Keep editing stays. With a red box, Save in the dialog closes it and shows the red box (nothing saved). Fixed, Save in the dialog saves and then opens the Dashboard. Discard leaves without saving. Change a box and reload the tab: the browser asks before leaving. With nothing changed, links open straight away.
    6. Repeat 1–2 quickly on **Prayer & iqamah** (clear Times end on), **People → Add a person** (Save empty), **Payments → Funds → Add a fund** (link "go2pay.gd/12x"), **Seasons**, **Pages → About** (clear Page title) and **New board term → Edit the steps** (clear a step): each shows the red box with its line and "Some boxes need fixing." at the top.
    7. **Show me how** is on the Dashboard, Prayer & iqamah, People, an event or announcement form, New board term and Recent changes, and nowhere else (for example not on Edit sections or Payments).
    8. **Add to Calendar** on an event: on a Windows or Linux computer a menu offers Google Calendar, Outlook and Apple or other (file); Google Calendar and Outlook open a new event with the name, place and times filled in; the file opens in the computer's calendar app. On an iPhone (Safari) it opens Apple Calendar's "Add" sheet; on a Mac it opens the file in Calendar; on Android it opens Google Calendar. A cancelled event says "Cancelled: …" in all of them.
    9. Help → **Events** and **Cancel or postpone an event** describe the Start/End boxes and the announcement question; the **When something goes wrong** picture shows "Some boxes need fixing." at the top of the event form.

80. Review 3, E: Qur'an verses from the fixed library. In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`), on a computer and on a phone (390 px), light and dark; on staging once the database is rebuilt from the clean files (Settings below) the same steps work with the real database:

The notes beside **Upcoming at MSA**, the community strip and the giving band, and the footer verse, are each one whole verse on one line. 81. Review 3, D: History and Restore. In the sandbox (`npm run demo`, sign in with `admin` / `admin`), on a computer and on a phone (390 px), light and dark; on staging once the database is rebuilt from the clean files (Settings below):
    1. Open **Pages → About**. Change **Our team → Heading** to "Test one" and click **Save page**; change it to "Test two" and save again.
    2. Scroll to **History**. The intro says "The last 20 changes to this page, newest first. Restore puts back the version from before a change; …". On the second entry from the top (its change ends "→ Heading: Test one"), click **Restore the version before this change**.
    3. A box titled **Restore this version?** opens: "This puts “About” back to how it was on (date, time). The 2 changes made to it after that will be replaced. Nothing else on the website changes. You can undo this from History." Under **This version** the About page shows the old team heading. Click **Cancel**: the box closes and the heading is still "Test two".
    4. Open it again and click **Restore**: "The earlier text is back on the website. …"; the heading box shows the old heading, and so does **/about**.
    5. Back on **Pages → About**: the newest History entry is "Restored page text: About" with the line "Restored to the version of (date, time)". **Recent changes** shows the same entry first. All earlier changes are still listed (nothing was deleted).
    6. Undo it: on the "Restored page text: About" entry click **Restore the version before this change**, then **Restore**: the heading is "Test two" again.
    7. **Prayer & iqamah**: change **Fajr** and save. In **History** click **Restore the version before this change** on that change: the box names “Prayer & iqamah”, says "The 1 change made to it after that will be replaced.", and **This version** lists each prayer's iqamah, Jumu'ah, the dates, the Hijri date and the note of that version. **Restore**, then check **/prayer** shows the earlier Fajr.

### Settings to set before merging the Release PR

- Vercel → Settings → Environment Variables (Production). Every one is required; the [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md#required-variables) explains each:
  - `SITE_DEMO_MODE` = `false`
  - `SUPABASE_URL` = the production Supabase Project URL (`https://….supabase.co`)
  - `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` = the production project's publishable key (`sb_publishable_…`, never a secret key)
  - `SITE_URL` = the site address, https, nothing after the domain (today `https://sgu-msa-site.vercel.app`)
  - `SITE_DESIGN` is no longer read (PR2): delete it from Vercel, or leave it; it is harmless.
  - `DONATION_URL`: **no longer read** (PR6 PR-C). Delete it from Vercel after this release is live; it is ignored either way.
  - `MUSALLAH_LOCATION_CONFIRMED`, `MUSALLAH_LATITUDE`, `MUSALLAH_LONGITUDE`: **no longer needed** (PR7). Leave them until the release with PR7 is live, then delete them (Production and Preview); they are ignored either way.
  - `ENABLE_VERCEL_MEASUREMENTS` = `true` or `false`. If `true`, also click **Enable** on the project's **Analytics** and **Speed Insights** tabs.
  - Do **not** set `SITE_DEMO_DATE` on Vercel.
  - `GO2PAY_API_KEY` = `off` (PR6 step 1). **New and required:** without it the deploy stops. Keep `off` until PR6 launches; the live key will then be a "Take payments only" key made by so-msa@sgu.edu (P-70, answered 2026-10-05) ([owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md#the-payments-key-go2pay_api_key)). **After launch** (key set, Go2Pay approved): open one fund's **Give** link on the live site and check Go2Pay's page shows no TEST banner; then, in Go2Pay, check one product's stock ("how many are for sale") is what the board meant.
- Vercel → Settings → Environment Variables (Production), **new and required for the daily check** (without them the deploy stops; exact clicks: [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md#the-daily-check-warning-emails-to-the-msa-gmail)):
  - `CRON_SECRET` = a new random secret (`openssl rand -hex 32`, or 40 letters and digits from a password manager).
  - `MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS` = the MSA Gmail address (`sgumsapresident@gmail.com`, the club Gmail; confirm below).
  - `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD` = a **new** app password on that Gmail named `SGU MSA daily check` (myaccount.google.com/apppasswords), not the `SGU MSA website` one.
  - **Until the club Gmail is recovered** (D-17): set **both** `MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS` = `off` and `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD` = `off`. The deploy goes on with a warning in the build log, the dashboard says "Warning emails are off: set up the MSA Gmail", and no warning email is sent. Never set only one to `off` (the deploy stops). Replace both with the real values when the Gmail is back, then redeploy. `CRON_SECRET` is still required.
- **Review fixes P4 database update: apply to staging after the user's OK.** After the four baseline files, run `supabase/migrations/202610160003_team_list_and_campus_pin.sql` once: Supabase (**sgu-msa-staging**) → **SQL Editor** → **New query** → paste the whole file → **Run** ("Success. No rows returned"; running it again is harmless). Then test step 97.14. Production gets the same file, by a human, with the Release PR. It adds `public_team_list()`, makes the Executive board always listed with photos only for them (a photo of someone moved out of the Executive board is kept, hidden, for when they come back; review follow-ups), ticks "Show on public page" by default in every group, and keeps the musallah pin within about 1.5 km of the starting pin (the "MSA Mosque" place). If the board ever moved the pin further than that, the file stops with a check error: move the pin back on campus first.
- **Supabase: wipe and install the clean database** (final plan A5; replaces every earlier "migration … applied by a human" item here). The database is now four files in `supabase/migrations/`, run **in this order**: `202610150001_schema.sql` (tables, rules, functions, file storage), `202610150002_starter_data.sql` (team groups, prayer settings row, musallah pin, hand-over steps, seasons, merch, page text), `202610150003_quran_verses.sql` (the verse library), `202610150004_recent_changes.sql` (the Recent changes log; last, so the log starts empty). They give exactly the same database as the old 14 files did (checked by comparing both on two test databases). Do **staging** first and test it, then **production**. A human does this, never an app or an AI agent. No hosted database holds real data yet, so wiping loses nothing; if you are not sure, ask the developer to export it first (owner guide §15).
  1. **Pick the project.** Supabase → open **sgu-msa-staging** (later **sgu-msa-production**). Check the name at the top before every step.
  2. **Empty and delete the file bucket** (skip if **Storage** shows no `community` bucket). **Storage** → on the `community` bucket click **⋮** → **Empty bucket** → confirm; then **⋮** → **Delete bucket** → type `community` → confirm. (Supabase allows this only from the Storage screen, not from SQL. Button names may differ slightly: use the closest match.)
  3. **Wipe the old tables and functions** (skip on a new, empty project). **SQL Editor** → **New query** → paste this → **Run**. It says "Success. No rows returned". Logins under **Authentication → Users** are kept.

     ```sql
     -- Removes every table, function and file rule of the website in the public schema (not Supabase's own).
     drop policy if exists admin_upload on storage.objects;
     drop policy if exists admin_read on storage.objects;
     drop policy if exists published_attachment on storage.objects;
     drop policy if exists admin_delete_unreferenced on storage.objects;
     do $$
     declare item record;
     begin
       for item in select format('%I.%I', n.nspname, c.relname) as name,
           case c.relkind when 'v' then 'view' when 'm' then 'materialized view' else 'table' end as kind
         from pg_class c join pg_namespace n on n.oid = c.relnamespace
         where n.nspname = 'public' and c.relkind in ('r', 'p', 'v', 'm')
           and not exists (select 1 from pg_depend d where d.objid = c.oid and d.deptype = 'e')
       loop
         execute format('drop %s if exists %s cascade', item.kind, item.name);
       end loop;
       for item in select p.oid::regprocedure::text as name from pg_proc p join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace
         where n.nspname = 'public' and not exists (select 1 from pg_depend d where d.objid = p.oid and d.deptype = 'e')
       loop
         execute format('drop function if exists %s cascade', item.name);
       end loop;
     end $$;
     ```

     Then check it is empty: `select count(*) from pg_tables where schemaname = 'public';` shows `0`.

  4. **Install the four files, in order.** For each file: on GitHub open it on the `integration` branch (on `main` for production, once the Release PR is merged), click **Raw** (or the copy button) and copy the whole file; Supabase → **SQL Editor** → **New query** → paste → **Run**, once. Each says "Success. No rows returned". If one shows an error, stop, keep the message (no keys in it) and ask the developer: do not run the next file.
  5. **Check the install.** New query, paste, **Run**:

     ```sql
     select
       (select count(*) from public.people_sections) as groups,          -- 3
       (select count(*) from public.prayer_settings) as prayer_rows,     -- 1
       (select count(*) from public.musallah_location) as pins,          -- 1
       (select count(*) from public.handover_steps) as handover_steps,   -- 6
       (select count(*) from public.seasons) as seasons,                 -- 3
       (select count(*) from public.merch_items) as merch_items,         -- 3
       (select count(*) from public.page_texts) as page_texts,           -- 19
       (select count(*) from public.quran_verses) as verses,             -- 1655
       (select count(*) from public.activity_log) as recent_changes,     -- 0
       (select count(*) from pg_tables where schemaname = 'public' and not rowsecurity) as tables_without_rls, -- 0
       (select public from storage.buckets where id = 'community') as bucket_public,           -- false
       (select file_size_limit from storage.buckets where id = 'community') as bucket_limit;   -- 3145728
     ```

     The one row must show exactly the numbers in the comments.

  6. **Add the first admin.** **Authentication → Users → Add user → Create new user**: their email and a strong first password (12+ characters with a lower-case letter, a capital, a number and a symbol), turn on **Auto Confirm User**, **Create user**. Then SQL Editor → New query → run, with their email and name:

     ```sql
     select public.owner_add_admin('their.email@example.com', 'Full name', true);
     ```

     Check: `select p.full_name, p.role_title, s.name as group_name, a.can_manage_people from public.admins a join public.people p on p.id = a.person_id join public.people_sections s on s.id = p.section_id;` shows them in **Executive board** as **President** with `true`. They sign in at `/admin`, change their password with **Forgot your password?** once email works, and invite the others from **People**.

  7. **Auth settings** for the project: the "Supabase (PR3, WP-D and WP-H)" item below (email templates, URL configuration, password rule, SMTP once the MSA Gmail is back).
  8. **Test staging** (How to test, first the sign-in, a page edit and restore, an event, the map pin, then the others), and only then repeat steps 1 to 6 on **production**, before the Release PR is merged. The board confirms the musallah pin on site (Prayer & iqamah → Musallah location).
  9. Later, **only after** the release is live and checked: a separate update may drop the retired prayer columns (`confirmed`, `method`, `madhab`, `adjustments`, `latitude`, `longitude`), still in the clean files because the database had to stay exactly the same. It is not written yet (PR7 contract WP-6).
- **Supabase: apply `202610160001_jumuah_sessions.sql`** (review fixes P2; several Jumu’ah prayers). Apply it to **staging after the user's OK**, then to production with the Release PR. A human does this, never an app or an AI agent. Supabase → check the project name at the top → **SQL Editor** → **New query** → paste the whole file (GitHub, `integration` branch, **Raw**) → **Run**: "Success. No rows returned". Running it twice changes nothing. Check: `select jumuah_sessions, khutbah, jummah_iqamah from public.prayer_settings;` shows the weekly Jumu’ah as a list (`[]` if none was set) and two empty texts; since the review follow-ups it also sets `hijri_adjustment` to `0` (the Hijri date as calculated; check with `select hijri_adjustment from public.prayer_settings;`); a changed Friday in `exceptions` now holds `"sessions": [ … ]`. The retired `khutbah` and `jummah_iqamah` columns are dropped later with the other retired prayer columns, in their own update, after you confirm.
- **Supabase: apply `202610160002_lists.sql`** (review fixes P3), **on staging first and only after the user's OK**, then on production before the Release PR is merged. A human does it, never an app or an AI agent: on GitHub open `supabase/migrations/202610160002_lists.sql` on the `integration` branch → **Raw** → copy all; Supabase (check the project name) → **SQL Editor** → **New query** → paste → **Run**, once ("Success. No rows returned"). It adds the Lists table with its starter options, an event's category and term, "Save order" for the Lists, and newsletter images; it changes no existing row and is safe to run twice. Run it after the four baseline files.
- Vercel → Settings → Environment Variables (Production), new for PR3 (WP-B): `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` = a secret key of the **production** Supabase project. To make one: Supabase (production) → **Project Settings** → **API Keys** → **Secret keys** → create a key named `website-invites` and copy the `sb_secret_…` value. In Vercel add `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` with that value, environment **Production** only, then **Redeploy**. Never paste it anywhere else (not in an issue, chat or `.env.example`). The value `off` is refused on the live site. Owner guide §10, "The invite key".
- Supabase (PR3, WP-D and WP-H), **staging first, then production**, so invite and reset emails work. Full steps: [owner guide](guides/owner-setup.md) §10. Until the MSA Gmail is connected, add admins with `select public.owner_add_admin('email', 'Full name', true, 'President');` after **Add user → Create new user** (never "Send invitation"), the President first. Write `'President'` only for the first admin; for anyone else write their own role, or leave it out (`…, true);` gives the first free role), because whoever holds the role you write moves to the General board ([owner guide §10, First admin, or everyone locked out](guides/owner-setup.md#first-admin-or-everyone-locked-out), D-2):
  - **Authentication → Emails → Templates**: open `supabase/templates/invite.html`. Its first line looks like `<!-- Subject: You're invited to the SGU MSA website dashboard -->`: copy only the words after "Subject:" (without the closing `-->`) into **Subject** of **Invite user**, then paste everything from the second line to the end into the message body, and **Save**. Do the same with `supabase/templates/recovery.html` for **Reset password**. Without them the email links do not reach the new pages.
  - **Authentication → URL Configuration**: **Site URL** = on **staging** `http://localhost:3000` (staging is used only from a developer's computer, so its email links open there); on **production** the `SITE_URL` value (today `https://sgu-msa-site.vercel.app`). **Add URL**: the Site URL followed by `/admin/auth/confirm` (for example `https://sgu-msa-site.vercel.app/admin/auth/confirm`).
  - **Authentication → Sign In / Providers → Email**: **Email OTP expiration** `86400` (24 hours, D-3), **Minimum password length** `12`, **Password requirements** "Lowercase, uppercase letters, digits and symbols" (D-16); **Save**. The website's set-password form checks the same rule, but Supabase is the one that decides.
  - **Connect the MSA Gmail** (D-2; Gmail sends about 500 emails a day). Exact clicks: [owner guide §10](guides/owner-setup.md#connect-the-msa-gmail-for-invite-and-reset-emails).
    1. Turn on 2-Step Verification on the MSA Gmail account.
    2. Create the app password `SGU MSA website` and keep it only in the club password manager.
    3. Supabase (staging first, then production) → **Authentication** → **Emails** → **SMTP Settings**: enable custom SMTP with `smtp.gmail.com`, port `587`, sender name `SGU MSA`, the Gmail address and the app password.
    4. Send yourself a test invite, check it arrives (also in Spam), then clean up as in owner guide §10 (the test entry is archived and deleted permanently, so no test data stays on the live site).

- **Supabase: apply `202610160004_term_export.sql`** (review fixes P5, Term export; one new function, nothing removed). **Staging first, and only after the user's OK**; then production before the Release PR is merged. A human does this: Supabase → **SQL Editor** → **New query** → paste the whole file → **Run** ("Success. No rows returned"). Check: `select count(*) from pg_proc where proname = 'release_archived_files';` gives 1. Then How to test step 99.9.

### Needs your confirmation

#### You or the founder must decide

- **Review fixes P4: the team list's look (please confirm from the pictures in `.plans/mockups/review-fixes/p4/`, `about-team-*`).** The tiers are centred cards 15rem wide, the portrait is 4:5 above the role, and a card without a photo is just role and name; **View all** sits on the left under the tiers; the other groups are a two- or three-column list of name and role with a thin line under each. Say if you want, for example, round photos, the tiers left-aligned, or a smaller photo on phones (now the full width of the screen).
- **Review fixes P4: "Show on public page" is now a tick box** (it was Yes/No buttons with nothing chosen for a new person). The database update also changes every group's start answer to "ticked", the General board and Advisors/Faculty included (it was "No" for them). Say if Advisors/Faculty should start unticked again (Edit groups can change it any time). People added with the owner's SQL line (`owner_add_admin`) outside the Executive board still start unticked (that baseline function is unchanged).
- **Review fixes P4: where the photo nudge shows (please pick).** Now in three places: a closable line in the bell, "No photo yet" on People, and the note in the person form. Options: **A** keep all three; **B** only People and the form (no bell line); **C** only the bell. The bell line shows to every board member, not only to the person without a photo.
- **Review fixes P4: Change email behaviour (please decide).** It is offered for invited, active and deactivated logins (not for archived people). The new address is taken as confirmed: no email goes to the old or new address for an active login, and the change takes effect at once. Supabase Auth keeps the email, so the change is not in Recent changes. Say if an active person should be emailed, or if the change should show in Recent changes (that needs a small extra database step).

Developer choices, FYI: the warning is measured for the narrowest place a verse shows (the 18rem note beside a page heading at its smallest readable size, 1.1rem), so 4:103 and 94:5–6 as the site shows them today get the warning although they fit one line on the Prayer and Support pages of a phone; a pause mark (such as ۖ) cannot start or end a part; choosing every word stores no part (the whole verse); the smallest sizes are 1.1rem (margin verses and footer), 1.4rem (dhikr) and 1.5rem (the Home verse band). Say if any of these should change.
- **PR5b starter Qur'an verses (answered 2026-10-05: option C, parts of a verse; done in review fixes PR-3, kept for history):** the verse library starts empty. Its rule (D-24) is to keep each verse exactly as the Qur'an service gives it, and the copy step stopped, as the contract requires, because 8 of the 9 verses on the site today are only part of a verse (for example 5:2 shows "And cooperate in righteousness and piety." from a long verse; 13:28, 4:103, 3:92, 20:114, 49:10, 2:183 too; 96:1 differs only by the opening "Bismillah" the service adds). Options: **A.** show the whole verse in those places (some become long, 5:2 a full paragraph); **B.** the board picks a shorter whole verse for those places with the verse picker (PR-C); **C.** allow part of a verse in the library (changes D-24; the picker would need a way to choose the part). Recommended: **B** (whole verses only, nothing typed). **Since PR-B, every page reads its verse from the library only, so with the empty library no page shows a verse** until verses are added (PR-C's picker, or option A/C): this must be settled, and the verses added on staging and production, before the Release PR. **PR-C's picker is in** (Pages → Qur’an verses): with option B the board picks each place's whole verse there, on staging and then production, before the Release PR.
- **PR5b Support verse (new, PR-B):** "Verse on each page" has a **Support** place (94:5–6 today), but the Support page has never drawn a verse, so a verse chosen there shows nowhere. Options: **A.** show it on `/support` like the other pages (a margin note under the heading; a small layout addition); **B.** remove the Support place from the verse list. Recommended: **A** if the board wants Support switched on, otherwise **B**.
- **PR5b PR-C wording not in a picture** (gone with Review 3 E except "Save verse choices"; kept for history) **(please confirm or reword):** on **Pages → Qur’an verses** (the rest follows the approved `verse-picker` picture): the search box's name "Verse number or word"; "Searching…"; "N verses found." / "Showing the first 20 verses found. Add a word to find fewer." / "No verses found. Try another word." (the picture had "18 verses found. Showing the first 3."; the service gives at most 20 and the screen shows them all); "Type a verse number, such as 2:255, or a word, such as patience."; "There is no verse 2:999. The verses already on the site are not affected."; after **Use this verse**: "Al-Baqara 2:153 is saved on the site. Choose where it shows under “Which verse shows on each page”." (the picture's "new" / "just added" marks are not built); "No verses saved yet. Find one under “Find a verse” and click “Use this verse”."; "The saved verses could not be loaded. Check your connection and reload."; "Archived verses (N)" and "An archived verse shows on no page. Restore puts it back in the list above."; "Archiving…". The Pages list card **Qur’an verses** ("Which Qur’an verse shows in each place."); the dashboard Pages card "…, sign-up forms, FAQ and Qur’an verses →". The two Google Forms tips: "Send each person a copy of their answers: in the form’s Settings → Responses, set Collect email addresses to Responder input and Send responders a copy of their response to Always." and "Get an email in the MSA Gmail for each new answer: in the Responses tab, click ⋮ → Get email notifications for new responses." under a "Tips" heading. The three help cards' text (Edit a page, Sign-up forms and Google Forms, Qur’an verses). - **PR4 simpler content forms (WP-5)**, decided while building, beyond the approved pictures:
  - The announcement hint reads only "Only the newest one with this ticked shows." (the picture added "(PR5 replaces this with Normal / Featured / Urgent.)", a note for you, not for the board).
  - **Describe the picture** has its hint "One sentence, for people using screen readers." on all three forms with pictures (the picture showed it on the event form only). The picture box keeps its existing line "JPEG, PNG, or WebP; maximum 3 MB."
  - The old line "Plain text. Paragraph breaks are preserved; HTML is not needed." is gone (not in the pictures). The summary boxes are two lines high (old summaries can have line breaks).
  - Under **The newsletter**, the file and link boxes have no visible name (as in the picture); screen readers call them "Newsletter PDF" and "Link to the newsletter". Choosing **Link to it** on an issue that has a PDF **takes the PDF off the issue** when you save ("Saving takes the attached PDF off this issue."), because otherwise the PDF would still be what visitors open. Choosing **Upload a PDF** without a file, when no PDF is attached, is refused with "The newsletter: choose a PDF or add a link." instead of quietly keeping an old link.
  - **More options** sits after the other news boxes, just above **Who can see it?**, and opens by itself when **Hide after** has a date.
  - **Changed by the developer side (2026-10-05):** the problem messages now use each form's box names ("Event name:", "Starts:", "Describe the picture:"…) instead of the old names ("Title:", "Start time:", "Picture description:"…), so each message names a box that is on the form. This was not your answer to Q57 (you said you did not understand it). Say if you prefer the old names.
  - ~~An event or newsletter that still has an old "Expiration date" keeps it (hidden).~~ **Resolved in step 6 (D-15, planner amendment):** saving such an event or newsletter now clears the hidden date, so it can no longer hide the item or refuse the save. The read-only check stays useful to find them: `select id, title, date, expires_on from public.events where expires_on is not null;` and the same for `newsletters`. Until each one is saved once, a past date there still keeps it off the website, as before.
  - The button is **Save** (as in the picture; before: "Save changes"). The page scrolls to the top after a refused save so the list of problems is in view.

#### Answered (2026-10-07)

- **Review fixes P5: Term export** (user, 2026-10-07): **Free up space** gets a tick box, "I opened the zip and can see the photos", in its question; **Remove files** stays off until it is ticked, and the website refuses without it (option A of the review question). Everything else stays as built: which items are in the term (events, community news and newsletters by their own date, current and archived; People, Funds and Resources as they are on the day; no announcements, seasons or merch), Free up space only on archived events, community news and newsletters, the folders (the year of the From day, then the name without that year; a name with another year is kept whole, so **2026 Fall** with From in 2020 gives `2020/2026 Fall`), the spreadsheet columns and formats, **no secret key** (everything runs as the signed-in manager), no "removed" note on the archived item, and the screen's wording.
- **Review follow-ups** (user, 2026-10-07: "keep it simple: no new settings, no new options"):
  - **Where "Unsaved changes" sits:** B — a thin bar along the bottom of the screen with **Save**, shown only after a change (built, What changed above).
  - **Someone moved out of the Executive board:** their photo is kept hidden, not deleted, and shows again if they come back.
  - **The Hijri date:** always as calculated; the database update sets the stored correction to "as calculated".
  - **Manage money hint:** says what it does now.
  - **Screens with no page of their own:** "Appears on: every page" or the real place.
  - **A shared login's Last changed:** today or earlier only.
  - **When something has no link or no form:** hide only its button and keep the rest.

#### Answered (2026-10-05)

From your replies to `.plans/questions-for-user.md` ("All recommended, except …") and the second answer sheet; Q-numbers are that file's.

- **P-70 Go2Pay key:** C — a "Take payments only" key; the board makes, edits and closes Go2Pay products by hand and pastes the link into the site; the support email is still sent, from so-msa@sgu.edu, which owns the Go2Pay account (user, Q1). Developers' test key: a payments-only test key by default; a full-access test key is allowed on a developer's own computer, with the developer-guide warning (Q95 B). Production keeps `GO2PAY_API_KEY` = `off` until PR6 launches.
- **PR6 product creation fails with the test key:** no longer blocks anything under P-70 C (the site never creates products); the support email still reports it (Q2).
- **PR6 board reminder, P-58 (5% on zakat):** nobody covers it; the 5% is lost, also on zakat. No top-up line on zakat slips, and zakat funds may be listed (user, answer sheet 2).
- **Privacy, Data Protection Act 2023 / SGU IT / brand rules:** no extra steps ("i dont think they care", user, Q90).
- **Clean-up, photo manifest text:** A — the two out-of-date sentences in `docs/archive/notes/ASSET_MANIFEST.md` are fixed in this change (Q61).
- **New design, Hijri date:** moving it one day on **Prayer & iqamah** is enough (Q86); Hijri dates appear only with prayer times and on the calendar (user, answer sheet 2). The prayer times were checked against mobile apps (user, Q84).
- **PR4 prayer behaviour changes:** accepted (Q87); the read-only check results in Settings above are still sent to the developer.
- **PR2 seal artwork:** the logo the user gave is final; the seal switches with the theme (teal in light, a lighter version in dark) (user, Q89).
- **PR3 help wording, email wording, email link page wording and privacy wording:** approved (Q59).
- **PR4 help pictures and help text (WP-7), and the help pictures decided while building:** approved (Q56).
- **PR4 tours (WP-6):** approved (Q58). The Q58 note on a prayer with both a fixed time and minutes after ("show error … cant have both") is already built: the save refuses both, and the dashboard asks the board to pick one (D-14).
- **PR8 Home and Prayer, Events, Get Involved and FAQ layout details:** approved (Q60).
- **Developer choices (FYI) below:** no objections (Q62).
- **PR6 PR-C review questions** (user, 2026-10-05; recorded as D-79 to D-82 in the [PR6 contract](contracts/pr6-go2pay-payments.md)):
  - **Donate page, where a headed group ends:** A — a thin line across the slips after a group with a heading (D-79).
  - **Donate page, slip alignment:** never centre slips; every slip is on the left, also a lone slip under a heading (Iftar under Ramadan) and a lone slip in the last row; group headings such as "Ramadan" are on the left too (D-80).
  - **Postponed event with no new date:** the event stays on the website with "Postponed: new date to be announced"; only **See price and buy ↗** pauses, and its ticket link says "This has closed", until the board sets the event back to **Scheduled** with the new **Start date**. The recommended option; the help cards say how it works, so say if it should differ once you have read them (D-81).
  - **Event page buttons:** **See price and buy ↗** stays filled; **Add to Calendar** is outlined beside it. Without a ticket, Add to Calendar stays filled as the page's main button (D-82; say if it should be outlined there too).

#### Developer choices — FYI, already applied (say if any should differ)

- Review 3 E, the verse library: "fits one line" uses the site's own size estimate (0.39 of the text size per letter, which errs on the wide side), so every library verse surely fits; a few verses that would just fit in the real font are left out (1655 of 6236 verses are in). Long verses such as 2:255 cannot be chosen. The search runs in the board's browser over the whole library (about 340 KB, sent with the Qur'an verses screen only), so results show as you type; Arabic is matched without harakat and with the alef, yeh and teh marbuta forms folded together. The migration `202610130001` was renamed to `…_one_room_verse_library.sql` (same number) since it no longer holds excerpts.
- Review fixes PR-C, a dashboard form left without saving: the browser keeps the last 3 visited pages for **Back**, so a board page opened by a link (or after a save) starts again from the saved text, as before this change, and text discarded in "Leave without saving?" does not come back; only the browser's **Back** button shows a page as it was left, typed text included, and leaving it again asks "Leave without saving?". Say if **Back** should start over too.
- Daily check (engineering, E-1 to E-7 in the [daily-check contract](contracts/daily-check-notifications.md)): `CRON_SECRET` is required also with sample data (`off` there answers every call with 401); a developer's computer without the Supabase secret key gets an "off" already-emailed list (nothing remembered, nothing sent); a mailer that is "off" ends the run as 200 with `"emailed":0`; `emailed` counts the problems listed in that night's email; the Nodemailer types come with Nodemailer 10 (no `@types/nodemailer`); the sandbox test of the box is in `tests/e2e/demo.spec.ts` (the `editor.spec.ts` tests need a real test account).
- PR7 PR-A (engineering, E-1 to E-10 in the [PR7 contract](contracts/pr7-single-pin-map.md)): the pin is its own table, not columns on the prayer settings, so a prayer **History** restore never moves it and the two saves never block each other (E-1); there is no "confirmed" switch: the seeded pin shows from the release and the board confirms it on site (E-2); no restore button for the pin: Recent changes shows every old and new value (E-3); pin moves are not highlighted in Recent changes (E-4); Apple's current directions address, which needs iOS 18.4 / macOS 15.4 or later, with the Google button beside it for older devices (E-5); the sandbox pin is the same Google place (E-9). Also decided while building: the starting pin is added before the change log is switched on, so Recent changes starts with the board's first move; pin moves are listed under **What → Prayer & iqamah**; the dashboard's "today's iqamah" preview uses the same pin; until PR-B the **Find us on campus** box shows the drawn placeholder with both links on every device.

- PR7 PR-B (engineering): MapLibre (`maplibre-gl` 6.12.0) is served from the site itself: `scripts/map-library.ts` copies its three files into the gitignored `public/maplibre/` on every `next dev`, `build`, `start` and Storybook run (so the map stories work on a fresh clone and in CI), and the page imports it from there only when a map is shown, so the library and its worker share one download, the worker is same-site and the security headers need no `blob:`. The map opens at zoom 16.5 on the pin; rotation and tilt are off. The map counts as "can't be shown" only if it fails before it first draws; a missing tile later is ignored. Campus building names: named buildings within 600 m of the pin from OpenStreetMap's public Overpass service (no key), at most 200 names of up to 80 characters, cached a day per pin by the server and an hour by the browser; any failure means no names, never an error, and with no good copy kept the server waits an hour before asking again (each request gives up after 5 s), so a slow or down Overpass never slows the map. The ⓘ credit starts closed (closed as soon as it is added, not only once the map has loaded); the sources' own credit lines are blanked so only the D-15 text shows. In the dashboard the map lets one finger move it (no cooperative gestures), and clicking the dot focuses it for the arrow keys (MapLibre stops a press from focusing it). The dot is an "application" region (role) rather than a button, since it has no Enter or Space action; a hidden polite live region announces each accepted move. Without JavaScript the empty map box and its loading line are hidden (CSS `scripting: none`). The help pictures wait for the map to draw before they are taken.
- PR5b PR-B (engineering, E-13 to E-18 in the [PR5b contract](contracts/pr5b-page-text-forms-reminders.md)): the Preview is drawn in the browser by each page's own components from the typed text, with the live lists read once on the server when the page editor opens (E-13); it leaves out verses, the map box and Home's community updates and featured notice (E-14); while a save would be refused it lists the same problems instead of the page (E-15); a closed form without its closed text, or a form without a link, shows no button (the approved picture's "button hidden"; E-16); the copyright line keeps today's words with the organisation's full name and the year in Grenada time (E-17); a library verse is named "Qur’an 94:5–6 (Ash-Sharh)" and its "?" adds "Translation: Saheeh International" on its own line (E-18). The Sign-up forms preview lists each form's name, where it shows and its button as visitors see it.
- PR5a PR-C (engineering, E-23 to E-28 in the [PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md)): New board term is three screens (checklist, Edit the steps, one account); ticking saves at once without leaving the page; Move up / Move down swaps two steps; a reworded step keeps its tick; the shared-login table becomes cards in a narrow column and the account name is its edit link; the picture's "Term started …" line and red "change it" on an old password date are left out (no term start is stored and no age rule was decided). Recent changes filters are a plain form in the page address; a broken filter value is ignored. Remove login needs the deactivated login's version on screen and the typed name (capitals and outer spaces ignored), and is offered for an archived person's login too. The HEIC test uses a generated HEIC-like file, and the Safari row is checked by hand (step 62.12).
- PR5a PR-B (engineering): the urgent band is built in `src/shared/ui/UrgentNoticeRotator.tsx` from the approved band picture (variant D) with the site's tokens and Motion (already installed), with a Storybook story in light and dark (E-3); it keeps the same two-row layout and a fixed two-line height at every screen size; it is hidden on `/admin` and starts again at the newest notice on each page. The status box sits at the end of the event form; its reason is kept only for **Cancelled** (switching to another status clears it). "Postponed from" is the date the event had before its first postponement, kept only once the **Date** box differs from it; otherwise the event says "new date to be announced". The preview shows a photo already saved, not one just chosen (that one is only uploaded on Save). The preview's "Scheduled" line uses the server's Grenada day. A page loaded before this update that saves a cancelled or postponed event is refused with "Someone else changed this; reload before trying again." (contract, Failure Modes). The Jumu'ah notice is saved right after the prayer change, from the same Save; whether a Friday "changed" is checked again on the server against the saved settings, so a re-save never posts a second notice. A link to an archived event shows the signpost but still answers with status 200 (as every not-found page on the site does today, because pages stream). Before the Jumu'ah question is checked, a Prayer & iqamah page that someone else saved meanwhile is refused with "Could not save. Reload to get the latest schedule before editing." (not a question that page never showed). The Jumu'ah notice text box stops at 80 characters, the urgent band's limit. One small fix in PR-A's rules: an unreadable **Hide at** now shows only "Hide at: choose a valid date and time.", not also "choose a time later than now.".
- PR5a PR-A (engineering, E-10 to E-17 in the [PR5a contract](contracts/pr5a-notices-events-continuity.md)): **Duplicate** makes a copy that is not urgent and is scheduled (as it is already not featured); a person who held an archived shared login can still be deleted permanently, and the login's holder box becomes empty; the sandbox's urgent notices end two hours after the demo starts and in three days. The Supabase versions of the New board term lists and the Recent changes filters are checked by hand on staging (step 60 here, and PR-C's steps), not by an automatic test (E-17).
- PR8 12-hour times (D-8): the dashboard still takes and shows times as 24-hour (for example 13:30), so its forms, History and Recent changes are unchanged; only the public pages switch to "1:30 PM". Midnight reads "12:00 AM" and noon "12:00 PM". The time and its AM/PM never split across two lines. Times typed as words in a content file (Ramadan iftar "6:40 PM", or "After Isha") show exactly as written.
- PR8 Contact ("Calling card", D-10): no new words, only the page's existing ones; "Get _in touch_" and "Find _the Musallah_" are split like the other Quiet Paper headings, and the welcome line is in grey italics under the main heading, as in the picked picture; **View directions** is an underlined link (before, an outlined button); the card lists the footer's social links by their names ("MSA on Instagram", "MSA on Facebook") and skips the "Email the MSA" link because the email is already shown; an email that is not a valid address shows "Contact details will be published here." instead of a broken link.
- PR8 Privacy and system pages ("Ledger", D-7): no new sentences. Layout choices from the picked picture: the page's one-line summary (until now only shown to search engines and link previews) is the italic line under **Privacy**; three sentences of the notice are repeated beside the heading as promises; **Questions** shows a separate **Contact & Visit** link (the menu's name for the page) circled in ink, and its "should review this notice … before launch" sentence sits apart in italics; the error page's **Try again** button gained a small circular-arrow icon. The loading page's outline pulses gently unless the device asks for less motion.
- PR8 Donate letter (D-2): no new words, only the page's existing ones; the heading's second half (_our community_) is set in grey italics on its own line, as in the picked picture; the ready state is circled with the ink ring like the next prayer; the picture's "design preview" note and its `?state=ready` switch were only for choosing and are not on the real page, so developers see the ready state in Storybook instead.
- PR8 Ramadan ("Tonight first", D-7): no new words on the page; the lantern photo moved from the top of the page to beside **Volunteer during Ramadan**, and the small labels "Contribute", "Transparency" and "Archive" are gone, both as in the picked picture. Today's date, with the Hijri date under it (D-11), is shown on the Tonight sheet, all year. The **Ramadan Calendar** button is circled in ink out of season, and **Iftar** in season. The picture's "design preview" note and its `?state=` switch were only for choosing and are not on the real page; developers see the in-season sheet in Storybook (**Pages → Ramadan tonight → In season**).
- Settings check formats: `true`/`false` and `off` in lower case; `SITE_URL` is the address only (http only on a developer's computer); a Supabase secret key is refused as the publishable key; empty counts as missing; in demo mode Supabase is never contacted (`DONATION_URL` is no longer read, PR6 PR-C).
- PR8 board feedback (shared parts, D-6): "short" Arabic means 26 letters or fewer, not counting the vowel marks; those stay on one line and get slightly smaller in narrow spaces instead of wrapping. Longer texts may take two even lines. (Replaced by review fixes PR-3: every text is sized to one line first.) The footer reminder is read out as "Reminder", like the margin verses. Say if any of these should differ.
- Hijri pairing (D-11): on Home the date moved from the Jumu’ah line to just above the Hijri date, so it shows once; on Ramadan the months ("Feb–Mar 2027") follow the Hijri year typed in the heading's small line. Say if either should differ.
- The Release PR waits for PR8 (PR2, D-5), so the whole new look goes live in one step: do not merge it before PR8.

- PR3 is complete with step 8 (WP-H); release it only after the settings above are done on staging and production.
- PR3 database rules: archiving someone with an active login needs "manage people"; Restore does not turn a login back on; order numbers are always typed; the owner's interim SQL puts a new admin in Board.

(shown when an entry's section is no longer current, so the board picks a new one instead of the first in the list); a new person starts with no section or order number; choosing **No** keeps an earlier consent record; people in an archived section show under "In an archived section"; the current dashboard look stays until PR8.
- PR3 help and guides (WP-H): the help page starts with a **Topics** list of links to each card; new cards use short numbered steps (button names in bold) and, where useful, a link that opens the screen ("Open People →", "Open Recent changes →", "Open Prayer & iqamah →", "Open Forgot password →"); the old cards for events, announcements, news, newsletters, photos and prayer are unchanged except "a person’s old photo is deleted when you replace it"; the password card shows the exact rule from the set-password form; "Delete permanently" is described as for privacy cases only ([ADR 0013](adr/0013-activity-log-archive-and-history.md)); the card titles and wording (quoted in What changed). Owner guide: the test invite is cleaned up by deactivating and archiving the test entry; a leaked secret key is replaced (delete in Supabase, new key, Vercel, redeploy); a lost Gmail holder or 2-Step Verification phone stops emails, so the holder is recorded privately and handed over at turnover (§13).
- PR3 plumbing: sandbox changes all show the demo's start time; a sandbox password change stores nothing; reusing your current password counts as success and signs out your other devices; " (copy)" shortens a 160-character title; Restore unpublishes but keeps "Featured"; sample names "Omar Haddad", "Maryam Siddiqui", "Dr. Kareem Mansour".

Reload to try again." when People or sections cannot be read.

An item (or notice) last saved before this change logs one "changed (spaces or line breaks only)" entry on its next save, once.

(the wording D-10 names), because it now stands alone instead of beside each role; the same text shows when the list cannot be loaded (the contract's failure rule), not a separate error; **/about** is now built on every visit (like **/prayer** and **/community**) so People changes show at once. The page keeps its current layout until its own PR8 design step.

A one-date change no longer has to carry minute corrections, but old ones are still accepted so the live site keeps saving until the release.

(on the row and in the refused save's list), and an old **History** version with both for one prayer is refused rather than changed silently. Say if either should differ.

- Review fixes PR-2 (engineering): "Show on public page?" starting as Yes is a per-section setting the board can change on **Edit sections** (`people_sections.shown_by_default`), not a rule tied to the name "Executive board" in code, so renaming the section keeps it working. The role suggestions are the founder's list plus every title already used in People (the board adds a suggestion by using it once). Saving an order is one database step for the whole list (`save_order`): either every row moves or none, a list changed by someone else is refused, and rows already in place keep their version. Funds are ordered only inside their Donate-page group. The step numbers ("7. …") are gone from the Edit the steps headings, because the order now changes by dragging before it is saved. A person moved to another section goes to the end of it.

Announcement rows are kept at most about a minute (the band is still filtered by the clock on every visit). While a page's fresh part is on its way, the existing "Loading the latest information…" outline shows under the menu (no new wording). Next keeps the last 3 visited pages for **Back**; a board page opened by a link starts over (see the FYI under "Needs your confirmation").

Give Manage people to another executive first.", "You cannot move yourself out of the Executive board while you can manage people. ### Developer setup (not needed by the board)

- The local sandbox is described in the [developer guide](guides/developer.md#local-sandbox-try-the-dashboard-with-sample-data).

- Unit tests load server-only modules through `tests/support/register.mjs` (test-only; production keeps the real guard).

- `.env.local` now needs every variable in `.env.example` (no defaults): `SITE_DEMO_MODE`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `SITE_URL`, `MUSALLAH_LOCATION_CONFIRMED` (`DONATION_URL` was removed by PR6 PR-C: delete it from `.env.local`). `npm run demo` sets the sample switch and seed date itself. `SITE_DESIGN` and `npm run review` are gone (PR2): delete `SITE_DESIGN` from `.env.local`.
- PR2: run `npm ci` (new fonts, `motion`, `radix-ui` and Storybook; the five old theme fonts and DM Sans are removed). `npm run storybook` shows the shared UI; CI runs `npm run build-storybook`. The skills and AGENTS.md now describe one design with light and dark (`data-theme`), not six `data-design` themes.

- Parallel agent sessions work in temporary git worktrees under `.claude/worktrees/` (ignored by git).
- Sharing `npm run dev` over an ngrok tunnel now works: `next.config.ts` allows `*.ngrok-free.dev` in `allowedDevOrigins` (dev server only; no effect on builds or the live site). Before this, Next 16 refused the live-reload connection from the tunnel address and the page never became interactive. To test: `ngrok http <dev port>`, open the `https://…ngrok-free.dev` link from another network, click **Visit Site**, and check the theme switch and menu respond.
- `npm run test:db` (needs Docker) checks the database rules on a throwaway Postgres; `npm run test:backend` now takes the `stack` suite and also needs `SUPABASE_TEST_DB_URL` and an `E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD` meeting the new password rule ([developer guide](guides/developer.md#local-testing)). The local Supabase stack now has the email provider on (public sign-ups stay off), 24-hour links and the 12-character password rule.
- The `designing-ui-components` skill no longer assumes six themes, never offers the discarded review themes, and says not to run `/design-sync` until a design direction is chosen (D1).
- `.env.local` also needs `CRON_SECRET=off` (also for `npm run demo`), `MSA_GMAIL_ADDRESS=off` and `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD=off` (daily check WP-1; the last two only with `SITE_DEMO_MODE=false`). Run `npm ci` (new package: `nodemailer`). A computer never sends warning email, even with real Gmail values. `npm run test:db` also checks the new `warning_emails_sent` rules; `npm run test:backend` checks the ledger through the local API.
- `.env.local` also needs `GO2PAY_API_KEY` (PR6 WP-1): `off`, or the MSA's **Take payments only** Go2Pay test key (`g2p_test_…`) from the owner (P-70 C); even a test key returns real givers' details, so never log or store them ([developer guide](guides/developer.md)). A live key is refused on your computer. `npm run demo` ignores it. Never print or commit it.
- `.env.local` also needs `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` (PR3 WP-B): keep `off` on your computer (invite emails off; nothing else changes). Only test against a local stack with a local secret key, never a hosted project's. `npm run demo` ignores it.
- PR3 WP-D: `npm run test:backend` now also checks the invite and reset emails on the local stack (Mailpit on port 54324): token-hash links, the D-16 rule and other sessions ending after a reset. `tests/e2e/editor.spec.ts` has a full browser walkthrough (invite email → set password → reset signs out another browser → deactivation) that runs when the app points at the local stack with `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` set to the stack's local secret key, and skips itself when it is `off`.
- Done (2026-10-05): `.mcp.json` points the Supabase MCP at the staging project (`qglftmxfjvxmrzppkxks`, read-only). Production (`thhpkztmjoiaiuscdmdh`) is never connected to an MCP. Start Claude Code from the repo root and run `/mcp` to sign in to supabase and vercel ([developer guide](guides/developer.md#working-with-ai-agents)).
- Review fixes PR-C: review the sandbox with `npm run demo:review -- -p 4100` (a production build, then serve it; one seed time for both; a restart erases edits). `npm run demo` (development) stays for working on the code. Pages may not export `dynamic`, `revalidate` or `fetchCache` any more (Cache Components); read "now" through `requestNow()`; see [Data caching and time](guides/developer.md#data-caching-and-time).
- `docs/architecture.md` section 9 now shows every database table, column, type and key as a Mermaid ER diagram, built from all files in `supabase/migrations/` applied in order (refreshed after #86: `events.end_date`, `people_sections.kind`, `restored_from` on `page_texts` and `prayer_settings`, `quran_verses` as the fixed verse library keyed by surah and ayah). Update it in the same PR as any migration change. Nothing to test; no setting.
